1478    /// Store one administrator public key on the radio's device identity
1479    /// (`PROP_DEV_ADMINS`), then persist with a chained `CMD_SAVE` when the
1480    /// device can.
1481    ///
1482    /// This is the bench half of node management: a key listed here may
1483    /// manage this radio over the mesh, so the phone puts its own node key
1484    /// on a radio it is attached to and manages it later from across the
1485    /// valley. The list is what authorizes an administrator — no pairwise
1486    /// provisioning follows, because the session is derived from the two
1487    /// identities.
1488    ///
1489    /// Requires an attached, otherwise-idle session on a device advertising
1490    /// `CAP_ADMIN`. Failures surface as `operation_error` with the device's
1491    /// status name — `NOMEM` when the list is full (capacity
1492    /// [`ulcp_max_dev_admins`]), `ALREADY` when the key is already listed,
1493    /// which callers should treat as success.
1494    pub fn insert_device_admin(
1495        &self,
1496        public_key: Vec<u8>,
1497    ) -> Result<UlcpSessionUpdateRecord, MobileError> {
1498        self.insert_device_key(cap::ADMIN, prop::DEV_ADMINS, public_key)
1499    }
1500
1501    /// Remove one administrator public key from the radio's device identity
1502    /// (`PROP_DEV_ADMINS`), then persist with a chained `CMD_SAVE` when the
1503    /// device can.
1504    ///
1505    /// Same preconditions as [`Self::insert_device_admin`]. Emptying the
1506    /// list is how a device stops being manageable over the mesh at all.
1507    /// `ITEM_NOT_FOUND` surfaces as `operation_error` and callers should
1508    /// treat it as success — the key is not listed either way.
1509    pub fn remove_device_admin(
1510        &self,
1511        public_key: Vec<u8>,
1512    ) -> Result<UlcpSessionUpdateRecord, MobileError> {
1513        self.remove_device_key(cap::ADMIN, prop::DEV_ADMINS, public_key)
1514    }
1515
1516    /// Queue one complete raw UMSH frame on `STR_PHY_RAW`.
1517    ///
1518    /// The platform adapter supplies only opaque bytes from `MobileMeshSession`;
1519    /// Rust owns the ULCP command, stream identifier, metadata, TID, and
1520    /// confirmation matching. `nocca` sets `TX_FLAG_NOCCA` so the device
1521    /// transmits without its pre-transmit channel-activity check — used for
1522    /// immediate MAC acks (see [`MobileMeshOutboundFrameRecord::nocca`]).
1523    pub fn transmit_raw(
1524        &self,
1525        data: Vec<u8>,
1526        nocca: bool,
1527    ) -> Result<UlcpSessionUpdateRecord, MobileError> {
1528        let mut state = self.inner.lock().expect("ULCP session mutex poisoned");
1529        let raw_pipeline_active = state
1530            .expected
1531            .values()
1532            .all(|expected| matches!(expected, ExpectedResponse::RawTransmit));
1533        if state.stage != SessionStage::Attached || !raw_pipeline_active || data.is_empty() {
1534            return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1535        }
1536        let mut available_tid = None;
1537        for _ in 0..usize::from(frame::TID_MAX) {
1538            let candidate = state.allocate_tid();
1539            if !state.expected.contains_key(&candidate) {
1540                available_tid = Some(candidate);
1541                break;
1542            }
1543        }
1544        let tid = available_tid.ok_or(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
1545        state.expected.insert(tid, ExpectedResponse::RawTransmit);
1546        let mut metadata = [0u8; umsh_ulcp::TxMeta::WIRE_LEN];
1547        let flags = if nocca {
1548            umsh_ulcp::meta::TX_FLAG_NOCCA
1549        } else {
1550            0
1551        };
1552        umsh_ulcp::TxMeta {
1553            flags,
1554            ..umsh_ulcp::TxMeta::default()
1555        }
1556        .encode(&mut metadata)
1557        .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
1558        let mut frame = vec![0u8; data.len() + 16];
1559        let len = umsh_ulcp::frame::str_send(
1560            &mut frame,
1561            tid,
1562            umsh_ulcp::ids::stream::PHY_RAW,
1563            &data,
1564            &metadata,
1565        )
1566        .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
1567        frame.truncate(len);
1568        let mut update = state.update(vec![frame]);
1569        update.raw_transmit_started_transaction_id = Some(tid);
1570        Ok(update)
1571    }
1572
1573    /// Consume one complete ULCP frame and advance the session reducer.
1574    pub fn consume(&self, frame: Vec<u8>) -> Result<UlcpSessionUpdateRecord, MobileError> {
1575        let parsed = Frame::parse(&frame).map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
1576        if parsed.command() == Some(Cmd::StrRecv) {
1577            if parsed.header.tid() != frame::TID_UNSOLICITED {
1578                return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1579            }
1580            let payload =
1581                StreamPayload::parse(parsed.payload).map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
1582            if payload.stream != umsh_ulcp::ids::stream::PHY_RAW {
1583                return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1584            }
1585            let metadata = BufferedRxMeta::decode(payload.metadata)
1586                .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
1587            let mut state = self.inner.lock().expect("ULCP session mutex poisoned");
1588            if state.stage == SessionStage::Idle {
1589                return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1590            }
1591            return Ok(state.update_with_received(vec![UlcpReceivedFrameRecord {
1592                data: payload.data.to_vec(),
1593                rssi_dbm: metadata.rx.rssi_dbm,
1594                lqi: metadata.rx.lqi.map(core::num::NonZeroU8::get),
1595                snr_cb: metadata.rx.snr_cb,
1596                was_buffered: metadata.flags & RX_FLAG_BUFFERED != 0,
1597                was_acknowledged: metadata.flags & RX_FLAG_ACKED != 0,
1598                age_seconds: metadata.age_s,
1599            }]));
1600        }
1601        let response = inspect_ulcp_property_frame(frame)?;
1602        let mut state = self.inner.lock().expect("ULCP session mutex poisoned");
1603        let mut outbound = Vec::new();
1604        let mut raw_transmit_result = None;
1605        let mut operation_error = None;
1606
1607        if response.transaction_id == frame::TID_UNSOLICITED {
1608            if response.command == Cmd::PropIs as u8 {
1609                state
1610                    .responses
1611                    .insert(response.property_id, response.clone());
1612                // Carried out verbatim as well as folded into the
1613                // snapshot, so a consumer caching values by property
1614                // number hears about it without knowing the property.
1615                state.pushed_properties.push(UlcpPropertyPushRecord {
1616                    property_id: response.property_id,
1617                    value: response.value.clone(),
1618                });
1619            }
1620            state.apply_property(&response)?;
1621            state.refresh_attached_snapshot(Some(response.property_id))?;
1622            return Ok(state.update(outbound));
1623        }
1624
1625        let expected = state
1626            .expected
1627            .remove(&response.transaction_id)
1628            .ok_or(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
1629        match expected {
1630            ExpectedResponse::Property(property) => {
1631                if response.property_id == prop::LAST_STATUS && property != prop::LAST_STATUS {
1632                    // A capability-gated property the device declines is one
1633                    // unknown setting, not an unusable device: drop any value
1634                    // cached from an earlier read so the reduction reports it
1635                    // as unreadable, and carry on with the rest of the queue.
1636                    let expected_property = matches!(
1637                        state.stage,
1638                        SessionStage::Inspection | SessionStage::Refreshing
1639                    );
1640                    if expected_property {
1641                        state.responses.remove(&property);
1642                    } else {
1643                        operation_error = Some(ulcp_operation_error(
1644                            format!("read property {property}"),
1645                            response.value.as_slice(),
1646                        )?);
1647                        let optional_initial_property = state.stage == SessionStage::Initial
1648                            && matches!(
1649                                property,
1650                                prop::DEV_KEY | prop::DEV_NAME | prop::BATTERY | prop::HOST_KEY
1651                            );
1652                        state.stage_failure_pending |= !optional_initial_property;
1653                        if state.stage == SessionStage::Initial && property == prop::HOST_KEY {
1654                            state.host_key_unsupported = true;
1655                        }
1656                    }
1657                } else {
1658                    if response.property_id != property || response.command != Cmd::PropIs as u8 {
1659                        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1660                    }
1661                    state.responses.insert(property, response.clone());
1662                    state.apply_property(&response)?;
1663                }
1664            }
1665            ExpectedResponse::Claim => {
1666                if response.property_id == prop::LAST_STATUS {
1667                    operation_error = Some(ulcp_operation_error(
1668                        "claim host identity".to_owned(),
1669                        response.value.as_slice(),
1670                    )?);
1671                    state.stage_failure_pending = true;
1672                } else {
1673                    if response.property_id != prop::HOST_KEY
1674                        || response.command != Cmd::PropIs as u8
1675                    {
1676                        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1677                    }
1678                    // Whatever the device reports is its host key, even if
1679                    // it is not the one just written — a claim that did not
1680                    // take means this radio belongs to someone else, which
1681                    // `ownership()` reads off this value and reports as
1682                    // `OtherHost`. That is an answer, not a broken session.
1683                    state.radio_host_key = Some(response.value.clone());
1684                    state.responses.insert(prop::HOST_KEY, response);
1685                    if state.has_capability(cap::SAVE)? {
1686                        state.stage = SessionStage::Saving;
1687                        let tid = state.allocate_tid();
1688                        state.expected.insert(tid, ExpectedResponse::Save);
1689                        outbound.push(ulcp_save(tid)?);
1690                    } else {
1691                        state.start_inspection(&mut outbound)?;
1692                    }
1693                }
1694            }
1695            ExpectedResponse::Save => {
1696                if response.property_id != prop::LAST_STATUS
1697                    || response.command != Cmd::PropIs as u8
1698                {
1699                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1700                }
1701                if inspect_ulcp_status(response.value.clone())? != 0 {
1702                    operation_error = Some(ulcp_operation_error(
1703                        "save claimed host identity".to_owned(),
1704                        response.value.as_slice(),
1705                    )?);
1706                    state.stage_failure_pending = true;
1707                } else {
1708                    state.start_inspection(&mut outbound)?;
1709                }
1710            }
1711            ExpectedResponse::ConfigurationProperty(property) => {
1712                if response.property_id == prop::LAST_STATUS {
1713                    operation_error = Some(ulcp_operation_error(
1714                        format!("set property {property}"),
1715                        response.value.as_slice(),
1716                    )?);
1717                    state.stage_failure_pending = true;
1718                    // The status frame describes the write, not the property.
1719                    // Filing it under the property would leave the snapshot
1720                    // reducing a status code as that property's value.
1721                    state.responses.remove(&property);
1722                } else if response.property_id != property || response.command != Cmd::PropIs as u8
1723                {
1724                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1725                } else {
1726                    // A `CMD_PROP_IS` is the device's authoritative value,
1727                    // whatever was written. It reports what the device holds
1728                    // — clamped to hardware, reduced to what it supports,
1729                    // changed for a reason this host has no view of — and a
1730                    // value differing from the write is that report, not a
1731                    // fault. The snapshot published to the UI is what the
1732                    // device says, never what was asked for. Failure is a
1733                    // `PROP_LAST_STATUS`, handled above.
1734                    state.responses.insert(property, response.clone());
1735                    state.apply_property(&response)?;
1736                }
1737            }
1738            ExpectedResponse::SaveConfiguration => {
1739                if response.property_id != prop::LAST_STATUS
1740                    || response.command != Cmd::PropIs as u8
1741                {
1742                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1743                }
1744                if inspect_ulcp_status(response.value.clone())? != 0 {
1745                    operation_error = Some(ulcp_operation_error(
1746                        "save radio configuration".to_owned(),
1747                        response.value.as_slice(),
1748                    )?);
1749                    state.stage_failure_pending = true;
1750                } else {
1751                    state.finish_configuration()?;
1752                }
1753            }
1754            ExpectedResponse::RawTransmit => {
1755                if response.property_id != prop::LAST_STATUS
1756                    || response.command != Cmd::PropIs as u8
1757                {
1758                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1759                }
1760                let status_code = inspect_ulcp_status(response.value)?;
1761                let status = umsh_ulcp::Status(status_code);
1762                raw_transmit_result = Some(UlcpRawTransmitResultRecord {
1763                    transaction_id: response.transaction_id,
1764                    status_code,
1765                    status_name: format!("{status:?}"),
1766                    disposition: if status == umsh_ulcp::Status::OK {
1767                        UlcpRawTransmitDisposition::Sent
1768                    } else if status == umsh_ulcp::Status::BUSY
1769                        || status == umsh_ulcp::Status::CCA_FAILURE
1770                    {
1771                        // Both are transient channel-contention refusals: the
1772                        // frame never left the radio, so retry with backoff.
1773                        UlcpRawTransmitDisposition::Retry
1774                    } else {
1775                        UlcpRawTransmitDisposition::Rejected
1776                    },
1777                });
1778            }
1779            ExpectedResponse::HostChannelInsert(mut remaining) => {
1780                if response.property_id == prop::LAST_STATUS {
1781                    let error = ulcp_operation_error(
1782                        "provision host channel key".to_owned(),
1783                        response.value.as_slice(),
1784                    )?;
1785                    // A channel key is its own item, so ALREADY asserts the
1786                    // state that was asked for. Anything else — NOMEM above
1787                    // all — stops the pass; the phone still runs its own MAC
1788                    // while attached, so this degrades radio-side filtering
1789                    // rather than the user's ability to use the channel.
1790                    if error.status_code != umsh_ulcp::Status::ALREADY.0 {
1791                        operation_error = Some(error);
1792                        state.refresh_attached_snapshot(None)?;
1793                        remaining.clear();
1794                    }
1795                } else if response.property_id != prop::HOST_CHANNEL_KEYS
1796                    || response.command != Cmd::PropInserted as u8
1797                {
1798                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1799                }
1800                if let Some(frame) = state.next_host_channel_insert(remaining) {
1801                    outbound.push(frame);
1802                } else {
1803                    state.refresh_attached_snapshot(None)?;
1804                }
1805            }
1806            ExpectedResponse::HostChannelReplace => {
1807                if response.property_id == prop::LAST_STATUS {
1808                    operation_error = Some(ulcp_operation_error(
1809                        "provision host channel keys".to_owned(),
1810                        response.value.as_slice(),
1811                    )?);
1812                } else if response.property_id != prop::HOST_CHANNEL_KEYS
1813                    || response.command != Cmd::PropIs as u8
1814                {
1815                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1816                }
1817                state.refresh_attached_snapshot(None)?;
1818            }
1819            ExpectedResponse::DevChannelInsert(id) => {
1820                if response.property_id == prop::LAST_STATUS {
1821                    let error = ulcp_operation_error(
1822                        "insert device channel key".to_owned(),
1823                        response.value.as_slice(),
1824                    )?;
1825                    // ALREADY is the device saying the channel is stored.
1826                    if error.status_code == umsh_ulcp::Status::ALREADY.0 {
1827                        state.patch_dev_channels(&id, true);
1828                        state.refresh_attached_snapshot(None)?;
1829                    }
1830                    operation_error = Some(error);
1831                } else {
1832                    if response.property_id != prop::DEV_CHANNEL_KEYS
1833                        || response.command != Cmd::PropInserted as u8
1834                        || response.value != id
1835                    {
1836                        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1837                    }
1838                    state.patch_dev_channels(&id, true);
1839                    if state.has_capability(cap::SAVE)? {
1840                        let tid = state.allocate_tid();
1841                        state
1842                            .expected
1843                            .insert(tid, ExpectedResponse::SaveDevChannels);
1844                        outbound.push(ulcp_save(tid)?);
1845                    }
1846                    state.refresh_attached_snapshot(None)?;
1847                }
1848            }
1849            ExpectedResponse::DevChannelRemove(id) => {
1850                if response.property_id == prop::LAST_STATUS {
1851                    let error = ulcp_operation_error(
1852                        "remove device channel key".to_owned(),
1853                        response.value.as_slice(),
1854                    )?;
1855                    if error.status_code == umsh_ulcp::Status::ITEM_NOT_FOUND.0 {
1856                        state.patch_dev_channels(&id, false);
1857                        state.refresh_attached_snapshot(None)?;
1858                    }
1859                    operation_error = Some(error);
1860                } else {
1861                    if response.property_id != prop::DEV_CHANNEL_KEYS
1862                        || response.command != Cmd::PropRemoved as u8
1863                        || response.value != id
1864                    {
1865                        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1866                    }
1867                    state.patch_dev_channels(&id, false);
1868                    if state.has_capability(cap::SAVE)? {
1869                        let tid = state.allocate_tid();
1870                        state
1871                            .expected
1872                            .insert(tid, ExpectedResponse::SaveDevChannels);
1873                        outbound.push(ulcp_save(tid)?);
1874                    }
1875                    state.refresh_attached_snapshot(None)?;
1876                }
1877            }
1878            ExpectedResponse::SaveDevChannels => {
1879                if response.property_id != prop::LAST_STATUS
1880                    || response.command != Cmd::PropIs as u8
1881                {
1882                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1883                }
1884                if inspect_ulcp_status(response.value.clone())? != 0 {
1885                    operation_error = Some(ulcp_operation_error(
1886                        "save device channel keys".to_owned(),
1887                        response.value.as_slice(),
1888                    )?);
1889                }
1890            }
1891            ExpectedResponse::DevKeyInsert { property, item } => {
1892                let table = dev_key_table_name(property);
1893                if response.property_id == prop::LAST_STATUS {
1894                    let error = ulcp_operation_error(
1895                        format!("insert device {table}"),
1896                        response.value.as_slice(),
1897                    )?;
1898                    // ALREADY is the device saying the key is stored; keep
1899                    // the cache truthful even though the operation "failed".
1900                    if error.status_code == umsh_ulcp::Status::ALREADY.0 {
1901                        state.patch_dev_keys(property, &item, true);
1902                        state.refresh_attached_snapshot(None)?;
1903                    }
1904                    operation_error = Some(error);
1905                } else {
1906                    if response.property_id != property
1907                        || response.command != Cmd::PropInserted as u8
1908                        || response.value != item
1909                    {
1910                        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1911                    }
1912                    state.patch_dev_keys(property, &item, true);
1913                    if state.has_capability(cap::SAVE)? {
1914                        let tid = state.allocate_tid();
1915                        state
1916                            .expected
1917                            .insert(tid, ExpectedResponse::SaveDevKeys { property });
1918                        outbound.push(ulcp_save(tid)?);
1919                    }
1920                    state.refresh_attached_snapshot(None)?;
1921                }
1922            }
1923            ExpectedResponse::DevKeyRemove { property, item } => {
1924                let table = dev_key_table_name(property);
1925                if response.property_id == prop::LAST_STATUS {
1926                    let error = ulcp_operation_error(
1927                        format!("remove device {table}"),
1928                        response.value.as_slice(),
1929                    )?;
1930                    // ITEM_NOT_FOUND means the key is not on the device,
1931                    // which is the state the caller asked for.
1932                    if error.status_code == umsh_ulcp::Status::ITEM_NOT_FOUND.0 {
1933                        state.patch_dev_keys(property, &item, false);
1934                        state.refresh_attached_snapshot(None)?;
1935                    }
1936                    operation_error = Some(error);
1937                } else {
1938                    if response.property_id != property
1939                        || response.command != Cmd::PropRemoved as u8
1940                        || response.value != item
1941                    {
1942                        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1943                    }
1944                    state.patch_dev_keys(property, &item, false);
1945                    if state.has_capability(cap::SAVE)? {
1946                        let tid = state.allocate_tid();
1947                        state
1948                            .expected
1949                            .insert(tid, ExpectedResponse::SaveDevKeys { property });
1950                        outbound.push(ulcp_save(tid)?);
1951                    }
1952                    state.refresh_attached_snapshot(None)?;
1953                }
1954            }
1955            ExpectedResponse::SaveDevKeys { property } => {
1956                if response.property_id != prop::LAST_STATUS
1957                    || response.command != Cmd::PropIs as u8
1958                {
1959                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1960                }
1961                if inspect_ulcp_status(response.value.clone())? != 0 {
1962                    // The live mutation stuck; only persistence failed. The
1963                    // session stays attached and the caller sees the same
1964                    // `saved` warning path a failed configuration save uses.
1965                    let table = dev_key_table_name(property);
1966                    operation_error = Some(ulcp_operation_error(
1967                        format!("save device {table}s"),
1968                        response.value.as_slice(),
1969                    )?);
1970                }
1971            }
1972            ExpectedResponse::ManagementGet(property) => {
1973                if response.property_id == prop::LAST_STATUS && property != prop::LAST_STATUS {
1974                    // A refusal is the device's whole answer about this
1975                    // property. Drop any stale cached value so the session
1976                    // snapshot agrees with what was just reported.
1977                    let status_code = inspect_ulcp_status(response.value.clone())?;
1978                    state.responses.remove(&property);
1979                    state.record_management_answer(MobileMeshManagementAnswerRecord {
1980                        property_id: property,
1981                        value: None,
1982                        status_code: Some(status_code),
1983                    });
1984                } else if response.property_id != property || response.command != Cmd::PropIs as u8
1985                {
1986                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
1987                } else {
1988                    state.responses.insert(property, response.clone());
1989                    state.apply_property(&response)?;
1990                    state.record_management_answer(MobileMeshManagementAnswerRecord {
1991                        property_id: property,
1992                        value: Some(response.value.clone()),
1993                        status_code: None,
1994                    });
1995                }
1996                state.continue_local_management(&mut outbound)?;
1997            }
1998            ExpectedResponse::ManagementSet(property) => {
1999                if response.property_id == prop::LAST_STATUS && property != prop::LAST_STATUS {
2000                    // A refused write leaves the device holding whatever it
2001                    // held. The answer records the refusal and the run
2002                    // continues — the caller decides per property, like the
2003                    // mesh path.
2004                    let status_code = inspect_ulcp_status(response.value.clone())?;
2005                    state.record_management_answer(MobileMeshManagementAnswerRecord {
2006                        property_id: property,
2007                        value: None,
2008                        status_code: Some(status_code),
2009                    });
2010                } else if response.property_id != property || response.command != Cmd::PropIs as u8
2011                {
2012                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
2013                } else {
2014                    // The echo is the device's authoritative value, whatever
2015                    // was written — see the ConfigurationProperty arm.
2016                    state.responses.insert(property, response.clone());
2017                    state.apply_property(&response)?;
2018                    state.record_management_answer(MobileMeshManagementAnswerRecord {
2019                        property_id: property,
2020                        value: Some(response.value.clone()),
2021                        status_code: None,
2022                    });
2023                }
2024                state.continue_local_management(&mut outbound)?;
2025            }
2026            ExpectedResponse::ManagementSave => {
2027                if response.property_id != prop::LAST_STATUS
2028                    || response.command != Cmd::PropIs as u8
2029                {
2030                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
2031                }
2032                let status_code = inspect_ulcp_status(response.value.clone())?;
2033                if let Some(op) = state.management.as_mut() {
2034                    op.save_status = Some(status_code);
2035                }
2036                state.continue_local_management(&mut outbound)?;
2037            }
2038        }
2039
2040        if state.expected.is_empty() {
2041            if state.stage_failure_pending {
2042                state.stage_failure_pending = false;
2043                state.recover_from_operation_failure(&mut outbound)?;
2044            } else {
2045                state.advance_completed_stage(&mut outbound)?;
2046            }
2047        }
2048        Ok(state.update_with(outbound, Vec::new(), raw_transmit_result, operation_error))
2049    }
2050
2051    /// Invalidate all outstanding transactions for a disconnected transport.
2052    pub fn reset(&self) -> UlcpSessionUpdateRecord {
2053        let mut state = self.inner.lock().expect("ULCP session mutex poisoned");
2054        let generation = state.generation.wrapping_add(1);
2055        *state = UlcpSessionState {
2056            generation,
2057            mode: self.mode,
2058            lazy_inspection: self.lazy_inspection,
2059            ..UlcpSessionState::default()
2060        };
2061        state.update(Vec::new())
2062    }
2063
2064    /// Abandon raw transactions whose GATT writes were rejected locally.
2065    /// Their late correlated responses are ignored once; the attachment and
2066    /// all non-raw session state remain intact.
2067    pub fn abandon_raw_transmits(&self, transaction_ids: Vec<u8>) -> UlcpSessionUpdateRecord {
2068        let mut state = self.inner.lock().expect("ULCP session mutex poisoned");
2069        for tid in transaction_ids {
2070            if matches!(
2071                state.expected.get(&tid),
2072                Some(ExpectedResponse::RawTransmit)
2073            ) {
2074                state.expected.remove(&tid);
2075            }
2076        }
2077        state.update(Vec::new())
2078    }
2079}
2080
2081impl MobileUlcpSession {
2082    fn with_mode(mode: UlcpAttachMode) -> Self {
2083        Self {
2084            inner: Mutex::new(UlcpSessionState {
2085                mode,
2086                ..UlcpSessionState::default()
2087            }),
2088            mode,
2089            lazy_inspection: false,
2090        }
2091    }
2092
2093    /// Add one key to a device-domain public-key table.
2094    fn insert_device_key(
2095        &self,
2096        capability: u32,
2097        property: u32,
2098        public_key: Vec<u8>,
2099    ) -> Result<UlcpSessionUpdateRecord, MobileError> {
2100        let public_key: [u8; 32] = public_key
2101            .try_into()
2102            .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidPublicKeyLength)?;
2103        let mut state = self.inner.lock().expect("ULCP session mutex poisoned");
2104        state.begin_device_domain_operation(capability)?;
2105        let tid = state.allocate_tid();
2106        state.expected.insert(
2107            tid,
2108            ExpectedResponse::DevKeyInsert {
2109                property,
2110                item: public_key.to_vec(),
2111            },
2112        );
2113        let frame = ulcp_prop_insert(tid, property, &public_key)?;
2114        Ok(state.update(vec![frame]))
2115    }
2116
2117    /// Take one key out of a device-domain public-key table.
2118    fn remove_device_key(
2119        &self,
2120        capability: u32,
2121        property: u32,
2122        public_key: Vec<u8>,
2123    ) -> Result<UlcpSessionUpdateRecord, MobileError> {
2124        let public_key: [u8; 32] = public_key
2125            .try_into()
2126            .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidPublicKeyLength)?;
2127        let mut state = self.inner.lock().expect("ULCP session mutex poisoned");
2128        state.begin_device_domain_operation(capability)?;
2129        let tid = state.allocate_tid();
2130        state.expected.insert(
2131            tid,
2132            ExpectedResponse::DevKeyRemove {
2133                property,
2134                item: public_key.to_vec(),
2135            },
2136        );
2137        let frame = ulcp_prop_remove(tid, property, &public_key)?;
2138        Ok(state.update(vec![frame]))
2139    }
2140}
2141
2142/// What a device-domain key table holds, for the operation names an error
2143/// carries.
2144fn dev_key_table_name(property: u32) -> &'static str {
2145    match property {
2146        prop::DEV_ADMINS => "administrator",
2147        _ => "peer",
2148    }
2149}
2150
2151impl UlcpSessionState {
2152    fn allocate_tid(&mut self) -> u8 {
2153        self.tids.allocate()
2154    }
2155
2156    fn get_property(&mut self, property: u32) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MobileError> {
2157        let tid = self.allocate_tid();
2158        self.expected
2159            .insert(tid, ExpectedResponse::Property(property));
2160        ulcp_prop_get(tid, property)
2161    }
2162
2163    fn phase(&self) -> UlcpSessionPhase {
2164        match self.stage {
2165            SessionStage::Idle => UlcpSessionPhase::Idle,
2166            SessionStage::Initial | SessionStage::Inspection | SessionStage::Saving => {
2167                UlcpSessionPhase::Synchronizing
2168            }
2169            // A refresh deliberately preserves the attached phase so live UI
2170            // does not disappear while fresh authoritative values are read.
2171            SessionStage::Refreshing => UlcpSessionPhase::Attached,
2172            SessionStage::AwaitingHost => UlcpSessionPhase::AwaitingHost,
2173            SessionStage::Claiming => UlcpSessionPhase::Claiming,
2174            SessionStage::Configuring | SessionStage::SavingConfiguration => {
2175                UlcpSessionPhase::Configuring
2176            }
2177            SessionStage::Attached => UlcpSessionPhase::Attached,
2178        }
2179    }
2180
2181    fn ownership(&self) -> UlcpHostOwnership {
2182        if self.host_key_unsupported {
2183            return UlcpHostOwnership::Unsupported;
2184        }
2185        let Some(radio_key) = self.radio_host_key.as_deref() else {
2186            return UlcpHostOwnership::Unknown;
2187        };
2188        if radio_key.is_empty() {
2189            return UlcpHostOwnership::Unclaimed;
2190        }
2191        match self.selected_host_key {
2192            None => UlcpHostOwnership::LocalIdentityUnavailable,
2193            Some(selected) if radio_key == selected => UlcpHostOwnership::Ours,
2194            Some(_) => UlcpHostOwnership::OtherHost,
2195        }
2196    }
2197
2198    fn update(&mut self, outbound_frames: Vec<Vec<u8>>) -> UlcpSessionUpdateRecord {
2199        self.update_with(outbound_frames, Vec::new(), None, None)
2200    }
2201
2202    fn update_with_received(
2203        &mut self,
2204        received_frames: Vec<UlcpReceivedFrameRecord>,
2205    ) -> UlcpSessionUpdateRecord {
2206        self.update_with(Vec::new(), received_frames, None, None)
2207    }
2208
2209    fn update_with(
2210        &mut self,
2211        outbound_frames: Vec<Vec<u8>>,
2212        received_frames: Vec<UlcpReceivedFrameRecord>,
2213        raw_transmit_result: Option<UlcpRawTransmitResultRecord>,
2214        operation_error: Option<UlcpOperationErrorRecord>,
2215    ) -> UlcpSessionUpdateRecord {
2216        let raw_transmit_pending = self
2217            .expected
2218            .values()
2219            .any(|expected| matches!(expected, ExpectedResponse::RawTransmit));
2220        UlcpSessionUpdateRecord {
2221            outbound_frames,
2222            received_frames,
2223            snapshot: UlcpSessionSnapshotRecord {
2224                generation: self.generation,
2225                phase: self.phase(),
2226                host_ownership: self.ownership(),
2227                device_key: self.device_key.clone(),
2228                device_name: self.device_name.clone(),
2229                // Taken, not cloned: reported once, on the update that
2230                // actually carries a new measurement.
2231                battery: self.battery.take(),
2232                alert: self.alert,
2233                time: self.time.take(),
2234                gnss: self.gnss.as_ref().map(gnss_record),
2235                provisioning: self.provisioning.clone(),
2236            },
2237            waiting_for_responses: !self.expected.is_empty(),
2238            raw_transmit_pending,
2239            raw_transmit_started_transaction_id: None,
2240            raw_transmit_result,
2241            operation_error,
2242            // Taken, like the battery: a completion is reported on the
2243            // one update that carries it.
2244            management_event: self.management_event.take(),
2245            pushed_properties: std::mem::take(&mut self.pushed_properties),
2246        }
2247    }
2248
2249    fn apply_property(&mut self, response: &UlcpPropertyFrameRecord) -> Result<(), MobileError> {
2250        if response.command != Cmd::PropIs as u8 {
2251            // Insert/remove notifications are valid protocol frames, but none
2252            // of the mobile snapshot fields are multi-value payloads.
2253            return Ok(());
2254        }
2255        match response.property_id {
2256            prop::DEV_KEY => {
2257                if response.value.is_empty() {
2258                    self.device_key = None;
2259                } else if response.value.len() == items::PUBLIC_KEY_LEN {
2260                    self.device_key = Some(response.value.clone());
2261                } else {
2262                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
2263                }
2264            }
2265            prop::DEV_NAME => {
2266                let name = core::str::from_utf8(&response.value)
2267                    .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
2268                self.device_name = (!name.is_empty()).then(|| name.to_owned());
2269            }
2270            prop::BATTERY => {
2271                self.battery = Some(inspect_ulcp_battery(response.value.clone())?);
2272            }
2273            prop::ALERT => {
2274                // Arrives both as the answer to a write and unsolicited,
2275                // when the radio ends the alert itself.
2276                self.alert = Some(inspect_ulcp_alert(response.value.clone())?);
2277            }
2278            prop::TIME => {
2279                // Announced when the clock goes from unknown to known and
2280                // whenever it steps, which is how a phone learns the
2281                // device found the time on its own.
2282                self.time = Some(UlcpTimeRecord {
2283                    epoch_seconds: decode_optional(&response.value, decode_u32)?,
2284                });
2285            }
2286            key if umsh_ulcp::gnss::is_positioning_property(key) => {
2287                // Read or announced, indifferently: a device is meant to
2288                // announce only the fix indicator and leave a position to
2289                // be sampled, but one that volunteers a position anyway is
2290                // carrying the value a read would have returned, so this
2291                // takes it. Folding rather than replacing is what lets a
2292                // single property arrive without erasing the others.
2293                self.gnss
2294                    .get_or_insert(GnssSnapshot::SEARCHING)
2295                    .absorb(key, &response.value)
2296                    .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
2297            }
2298            prop::HOST_KEY => {
2299                if !response.value.is_empty() && response.value.len() != items::PUBLIC_KEY_LEN {
2300                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
2301                }
2302                self.radio_host_key = Some(response.value.clone());
2303            }
2304            _ => {}
2305        }
2306        Ok(())
2307    }
2308
2309    /// Whether synchronization may proceed straight to inspection without
2310    /// pausing for the user to decide about host ownership.
2311    ///
2312    /// A tethered session must pause: it is about to become the radio's
2313    /// host, and taking a radio from another phone is a decision only the
2314    /// user can make. An administrative session never claims anything, so
2315    /// there is no decision to pause for — whose radio this is stays worth
2316    /// reporting, but only as information.
2317    fn attaches_without_host_decision(&self) -> bool {
2318        self.mode == UlcpAttachMode::Administrative
2319            || matches!(
2320                self.ownership(),
2321                UlcpHostOwnership::Ours | UlcpHostOwnership::Unsupported
2322            )
2323    }
2324
2325    /// Gate a device-domain mutation: attached, no other operation in
2326    /// flight, and the device advertising whatever capability puts the
2327    /// table being written within reach.
2328    fn begin_device_domain_operation(&mut self, capability: u32) -> Result<(), MobileError> {
2329        if self.stage != SessionStage::Attached || !self.expected.is_empty() {
2330            return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
2331        }
2332        if !self.has_capability(capability)? {
2333            return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
2334        }
2335        Ok(())
2336    }
2337
2338    /// Gate a local management operation: attached, nothing else in
2339    /// flight. One operation at a time is the same discipline the mesh
2340    /// path enforces, and what lets a completion be attributed to the one
2341    /// operation that could have produced it.
2342    ///
2343    /// Raw PHY transmissions are not "something else": a tethered radio
2344    /// carries mesh traffic continuously, each exchange is matched by its
2345    /// own transaction, and a settings screen that could only work on a
2346    /// quiet mesh would rarely work at all.
2347    fn begin_local_management(&mut self) -> Result<(), MobileError> {
2348        if self.stage != SessionStage::Attached || self.management.is_some() {
2349            return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
2350        }
2351        let busy = self
2352            .expected
2353            .values()
2354            .any(|expected| !matches!(expected, ExpectedResponse::RawTransmit));
2355        if busy {
2356            return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
2357        }
2358        Ok(())
2359    }
2360
2361    /// A transaction identifier no outstanding exchange is using.
2362    ///
2363    /// The allocator cycles blindly, which is safe for the staged bulk
2364    /// reads — they only run with nothing outstanding — but a management
2365    /// round can coexist with a live one-off like an alert write, and
2366    /// must not reuse its identifier.
2367    fn allocate_management_tid(&mut self) -> Result<u8, MobileError> {
2368        for _ in 0..usize::from(frame::TID_MAX) {
2369            let tid = self.tids.allocate();
2370            if !self.expected.contains_key(&tid) {
2371                return Ok(tid);
2372            }
2373        }
2374        Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
2375    }
2376
2377    /// Record one answer for the local management operation in flight.
2378    fn record_management_answer(&mut self, answer: MobileMeshManagementAnswerRecord) {
2379        if let Some(op) = self.management.as_mut() {
2380            op.answers.push(answer);
2381        }
2382    }
2383
2384    /// Issue the next round of the local management operation, or complete
2385    /// it. Called at the start of the operation and each time one of its
2386    /// answers arrives; does nothing while any of them remain outstanding.
2387    fn continue_local_management(
2388        &mut self,
2389        outbound: &mut Vec<Vec<u8>>,
2390    ) -> Result<(), MobileError> {
2391        if self.expected.values().any(ExpectedResponse::is_management) {
2392            return Ok(());
2393        }
2394        let Some(mut op) = self.management.take() else {
2395            return Ok(());
2396        };
2397        if let Some((property, value)) = op.write_queue.pop_front() {
2398            // One write at a time: the plan's order is load-bearing (the
2399            // PHY bracket), and a device applies what it is asked in the
2400            // order asked only if it is asked in that order.
2401            let tid = self.allocate_management_tid()?;
2402            self.expected
2403                .insert(tid, ExpectedResponse::ManagementSet(property));
2404            outbound.push(ulcp_prop_set(tid, property, value)?);
2405            self.management = Some(op);
2406        } else if !op.fetch_queue.is_empty() {
2407            let budget = usize::from(frame::TID_MAX).saturating_sub(self.expected.len());
2408            for _ in 0..budget {
2409                let Some(property) = op.fetch_queue.pop_front() else {
2410                    break;
2411                };
2412                let tid = self.allocate_management_tid()?;
2413                self.expected
2414                    .insert(tid, ExpectedResponse::ManagementGet(property));
2415                outbound.push(ulcp_prop_get(tid, property)?);
2416            }
2417            self.management = Some(op);
2418        } else {
2419            self.management_event = Some(UlcpLocalManagementEventRecord {
2420                answers: op.answers,
2421                status_code: op.save_status,
2422            });
2423            self.refresh_attached_snapshot(None)?;
2424        }
2425        Ok(())
2426    }
2427
2428    /// Queue the next host channel-key insert, if any remain. The cached
2429    /// digest is updated as each key is accepted.
2430    fn next_host_channel_insert(&mut self, mut remaining: VecDeque<Vec<u8>>) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
2431        let key = remaining.pop_front()?;
2432        let tid = self.allocate_tid();
2433        let frame = ulcp_prop_insert(tid, prop::HOST_CHANNEL_KEYS, &key).ok()?;
2434        self.expected
2435            .insert(tid, ExpectedResponse::HostChannelInsert(remaining));
2436        if let Ok(id) = dev_channel_id(&key) {
2437            self.push_host_channel_id(&id);
2438        }
2439        Some(frame)
2440    }
2441
2442    /// Replace the cached `PROP_HOST_CHANNEL_KEYS` digest wholesale.
2443    fn set_host_channel_ids(&mut self, ids: &[Vec<u8>]) {
2444        let value = ids.concat();
2445        self.host_channel_entry().value = value;
2446    }
2447
2448    fn push_host_channel_id(&mut self, id: &[u8]) {
2449        let entry = self.host_channel_entry();
2450        if !entry.value.chunks(items::CHANNEL_ID_LEN).any(|c| c == id) {
2451            entry.value.extend_from_slice(id);
2452        }
2453    }
2454
2455    fn host_channel_entry(&mut self) -> &mut UlcpPropertyFrameRecord {
2456        self.responses
2457            .entry(prop::HOST_CHANNEL_KEYS)
2458            .or_insert_with(|| UlcpPropertyFrameRecord {
2459                transaction_id: frame::TID_UNSOLICITED,
2460                command: Cmd::PropIs as u8,
2461                property_id: prop::HOST_CHANNEL_KEYS,
2462                value: Vec::new(),
2463            })
2464    }
2465
2466    /// Patch the cached `PROP_DEV_CHANNEL_KEYS` digest after a confirmed
2467    /// mutation. The cached value is a list of derived identifiers, so this
2468    /// tracks identifiers rather than key material.
2469    fn patch_dev_channels(&mut self, id: &[u8], present: bool) {
2470        let entry = self
2471            .responses
2472            .entry(prop::DEV_CHANNEL_KEYS)
2473            .or_insert_with(|| UlcpPropertyFrameRecord {
2474                transaction_id: frame::TID_UNSOLICITED,
2475                command: Cmd::PropIs as u8,
2476                property_id: prop::DEV_CHANNEL_KEYS,
2477                value: Vec::new(),
2478            });
2479        let mut value = Vec::with_capacity(entry.value.len() + id.len());
2480        let mut found = false;
2481        for chunk in entry.value.chunks(items::CHANNEL_ID_LEN) {
2482            if chunk == id {
2483                found = true;
2484                if !present {
2485                    continue;
2486                }
2487            }
2488            value.extend_from_slice(chunk);
2489        }
2490        if present && !found {
2491            value.extend_from_slice(id);
2492        }
2493        entry.value = value;
2494    }
2495
2496    /// Patch a cached device-domain public-key table after a confirmed
2497    /// mutation, keeping it lossless without a round-trip re-read.
2498    fn patch_dev_keys(&mut self, property: u32, key: &[u8], present: bool) {
2499        let entry = self
2500            .responses
2501            .entry(property)
2502            .or_insert_with(|| UlcpPropertyFrameRecord {
2503                transaction_id: frame::TID_UNSOLICITED,
2504                command: Cmd::PropIs as u8,
2505                property_id: property,
2506                value: Vec::new(),
2507            });
2508        let mut value = Vec::with_capacity(entry.value.len() + key.len());
2509        let mut found = false;
2510        for chunk in entry.value.chunks(items::PUBLIC_KEY_LEN) {
2511            if chunk == key {
2512                found = true;
2513                if !present {
2514                    continue;
2515                }
2516            }
2517            value.extend_from_slice(chunk);
2518        }
2519        if present && !found {
2520            value.extend_from_slice(key);
2521        }
2522        entry.value = value;
2523    }
2524
2525    fn has_capability(&self, capability: u32) -> Result<bool, MobileError> {
2526        let capabilities = self
2527            .responses
2528            .get(&prop::CAPS)
2529            .ok_or(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
2530        Ok(decode_capabilities(&capabilities.value)?.contains(&capability))
2531    }
2532
2533    fn writable(&self, values: Vec<(u32, Vec<u8>)>) -> VecDeque<(u32, Vec<u8>)> {
2534        let unreadable = self
2535            .provisioning
2536            .as_ref()
2537            .map(|sync| sync.unreadable_properties.as_slice())
2538            .unwrap_or_default();
2539        writable(values, unreadable).into()
2540    }
2541
2542    fn advance_completed_stage(&mut self, outbound: &mut Vec<Vec<u8>>) -> Result<(), MobileError> {
2543        match self.stage {
2544            SessionStage::Initial => {
2545                let version = self
2546                    .responses
2547                    .get(&prop::PROTOCOL_VERSION)
2548                    .ok_or(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
2549                if version.value != [PROTOCOL_MAJOR_VERSION, PROTOCOL_MINOR_VERSION] {
2550                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
2551                }
2552                let capabilities = self
2553                    .responses
2554                    .get(&prop::CAPS)
2555                    .ok_or(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
2556                self.inspection_queue = if self.lazy_inspection {
2557                    // Only what the sync reduction insists on: the
2558                    // interface check and the radio basics every device
2559                    // has. The rest is read on demand, which is the whole
2560                    // point of a lazy session.
2561                    VecDeque::from(vec![
2562                        prop::INTERFACE_TYPE,
2563                        prop::PHY_ENABLED,
2564                        prop::PHY_FREQ,
2565                        prop::PHY_TX_POWER,
2566                    ])
2567                } else {
2568                    ulcp_inspection_properties(capabilities.value.clone())?.into()
2569                };
2570                let advertises_host_filter = self.has_capability(cap::HOST_FILTER)?;
2571                if advertises_host_filter == self.host_key_unsupported {
2572                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
2573                }
2574                if self.attaches_without_host_decision() {
2575                    self.start_inspection(outbound)?;
2576                } else {
2577                    self.stage = SessionStage::AwaitingHost;
2578                }
2579            }
2580            SessionStage::Inspection => self.start_inspection(outbound)?,
2581            SessionStage::Refreshing => self.start_refresh(outbound)?,
2582            SessionStage::Configuring => {
2583                if !self.configuration_queue.is_empty() {
2584                    self.start_configuration(outbound)?;
2585                } else if self.has_capability(cap::SAVE)? {
2586                    self.stage = SessionStage::SavingConfiguration;
2587                    let tid = self.allocate_tid();
2588                    self.expected
2589                        .insert(tid, ExpectedResponse::SaveConfiguration);
2590                    outbound.push(ulcp_save(tid)?);
2591                } else {
2592                    self.finish_configuration()?;
2593                }
2594            }
2595            SessionStage::Claiming
2596            | SessionStage::Saving
2597            | SessionStage::AwaitingHost
2598            | SessionStage::Attached
2599            | SessionStage::SavingConfiguration
2600            | SessionStage::Idle => {}
2601        }
2602        Ok(())
2603    }
2604
2605    /// Abort only the failed operation stage. A correlated CRP status error
2606    /// never invalidates GATT framing and therefore never resets the session.
2607    fn recover_from_operation_failure(
2608        &mut self,
2609        outbound: &mut Vec<Vec<u8>>,
2610    ) -> Result<(), MobileError> {
2611        self.configuration_queue.clear();
2612        self.inspection_queue.clear();
2613        match self.stage {
2614            SessionStage::Claiming => self.stage = SessionStage::AwaitingHost,
2615            SessionStage::Saving => {
2616                // The host-key write succeeded even if persistence did not.
2617                // Continue attaching while reporting that SAVE failed.
2618                self.start_inspection(outbound)?;
2619            }
2620            SessionStage::Refreshing
2621            | SessionStage::Configuring
2622            | SessionStage::SavingConfiguration => {
2623                // Retain the last authoritative snapshot. Property echoes that
2624                // completed before the failed operation remain available for
2625                // the next explicit refresh.
2626                self.stage = SessionStage::Attached;
2627            }
2628            SessionStage::Inspection if self.provisioning.is_some() => {
2629                self.stage = SessionStage::Attached;
2630            }
2631            SessionStage::Initial | SessionStage::Inspection => {
2632                // The transport is healthy but the initial snapshot is not
2633                // trustworthy enough to attach. Stay connected and report the
2634                // operation error; reconnect/refresh may retry synchronization.
2635                self.stage = SessionStage::Initial;
2636            }
2637            SessionStage::Attached | SessionStage::AwaitingHost | SessionStage::Idle => {}
2638        }
2639        Ok(())
2640    }
2641
2642    fn finish_configuration(&mut self) -> Result<(), MobileError> {
2643        let responses = self.responses.values().cloned().collect();
2644        self.provisioning = Some(inspect_ulcp_sync(responses)?);
2645        self.stage = SessionStage::Attached;
2646        Ok(())
2647    }
2648
2649    fn start_configuration(&mut self, outbound: &mut Vec<Vec<u8>>) -> Result<(), MobileError> {
2650        self.stage = SessionStage::Configuring;
2651        for _ in 0..usize::from(frame::TID_MAX) {
2652            let Some((property, value)) = self.configuration_queue.pop_front() else {
2653                break;
2654            };
2655            let tid = self.allocate_tid();
2656            self.expected
2657                .insert(tid, ExpectedResponse::ConfigurationProperty(property));
2658            outbound.push(ulcp_prop_set(tid, property, value)?);
2659        }
2660        Ok(())
2661    }
2662
2663    fn start_inspection(&mut self, outbound: &mut Vec<Vec<u8>>) -> Result<(), MobileError> {
2664        self.stage = SessionStage::Inspection;
2665        if self.inspection_queue.is_empty() {
2666            let responses = self.responses.values().cloned().collect();
2667            self.provisioning = Some(inspect_ulcp_sync(responses)?);
2668            self.stage = SessionStage::Attached;
2669            return Ok(());
2670        }
2671        for _ in 0..usize::from(frame::TID_MAX) {
2672            let Some(property) = self.inspection_queue.pop_front() else {
2673                break;
2674            };
2675            outbound.push(self.get_property(property)?);
2676        }
2677        Ok(())
2678    }
2679
2680    fn start_refresh(&mut self, outbound: &mut Vec<Vec<u8>>) -> Result<(), MobileError> {
2681        self.stage = SessionStage::Refreshing;
2682        if self.inspection_queue.is_empty() {
2683            let responses = self.responses.values().cloned().collect();
2684            self.provisioning = Some(inspect_ulcp_sync(responses)?);
2685            self.stage = SessionStage::Attached;
2686            return Ok(());
2687        }
2688        for _ in 0..usize::from(frame::TID_MAX) {
2689            let Some(property) = self.inspection_queue.pop_front() else {
2690                break;
2691            };
2692            outbound.push(self.get_property(property)?);
2693        }
2694        Ok(())
2695    }
2696
2697    /// Recompute the attached provisioning snapshot after device state
2698    /// changed under an established session.
2699    ///
2700    /// `changed_property` is the property the triggering notification
2701    /// carried, or `None` for a change this session made itself.
2702    ///
2703    /// Re-opening the host decision is deliberately limited to a
2704    /// `PROP_HOST_KEY` change. Another phone claiming the radio out from
2705    /// under an attached session is a question only the user can answer,
2706    /// so that case still returns to the host prompt. Every *other*
2707    /// published value is news, not a decision: a session attached to a
2708    /// radio owned by another identity (a tethered claim that did not
2709    /// take, which attaches deliberately — see the `Claim` arm) would
2710    /// otherwise be thrown back to the prompt by any unsolicited update at
2711    /// all. `PROP_BATTERY` makes that concrete, being the one notification
2712    /// that arrives on its own schedule for the life of the session.
2713    fn refresh_attached_snapshot(
2714        &mut self,
2715        changed_property: Option<u32>,
2716    ) -> Result<(), MobileError> {
2717        if self.stage != SessionStage::Attached {
2718            return Ok(());
2719        }
2720        let responses = self.responses.values().cloned().collect();
2721        self.provisioning = Some(inspect_ulcp_sync(responses)?);
2722        if changed_property == Some(prop::HOST_KEY) && !self.attaches_without_host_decision() {
2723            self.stage = SessionStage::AwaitingHost;
2724        }
2725        Ok(())
2726    }
2727}
2728
2729/// Return the authoritative properties needed for the read-only post-attach
2730/// inspection, gated by the supplied `PROP_CAPS` value.
2731#[uniffi::export]
2732pub fn ulcp_inspection_properties(capabilities: Vec<u8>) -> Result<Vec<u32>, MobileError> {
2733    let capabilities = decode_capabilities(&capabilities)?;
2734    validate_capability_dependencies(&capabilities)?;
2735    let has = |capability| capabilities.contains(&capability);
2736
2737    let mut properties = vec![
2738        prop::INTERFACE_TYPE,
2739        prop::PHY_ENABLED,
2740        prop::PHY_FREQ,
2741        prop::PHY_TX_POWER,
2742    ];
2743    if has(cap::PHY_LORA) {
2744        properties.extend([prop::PHY_LORA_BW, prop::PHY_LORA_SF, prop::PHY_LORA_CR]);
2745    }
2746    if has(cap::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT) {
2747        properties.extend([prop::PHY_DUTY_NOW, prop::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT]);
2748    }
2749    if has(cap::SAVE) {
2750        properties.push(prop::SAVED);
2751    }
2752    if has(cap::HOST_FILTER) {
2753        properties.push(prop::HOST_RX_FILTERS);
2754    }
2755    if has(cap::HOST_KEYS) {
2756        properties.extend([prop::HOST_CHANNEL_KEYS, prop::HOST_PEER_KEYS]);
2757    }
2758    if has(cap::HOST_RX_QUEUE) {
2759        properties.extend([prop::HOST_RX_QUEUE_COUNT, prop::HOST_RX_QUEUE_DROPPED]);
2760    }
2761    if has(cap::HOST_AUTO_ACK) {
2762        properties.push(prop::HOST_AUTO_ACK);
2763    }
2764    if has(cap::REPEATER) {
2765        properties.extend([
2766            prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED,
2767            prop::MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS,
2768            prop::MAC_REPEATER_DEFAULT_REGION,
2769            prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI,
2770            prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_SNR,
2771        ]);
2772    }
2773    if has(cap::IDENT) {
2774        properties.extend([
2775            prop::IDENT_ROLE,
2776            prop::IDENT_MOBILE,
2777            // Where the device says it is. Read at attach because it is
2778            // the position a region proposal starts from, and a phone at
2779            // a bench cannot ask for it separately: the local link's
2780            // snapshot is the whole of what a setup sheet knows.
2781            prop::IDENT_LOCATION,
2782            prop::IDENT_ALTITUDE,
2783        ]);
2784    }
2785    if has(cap::DEV_IDENTITY) {
2786        properties.extend([
2787            prop::DEV_PEERS,
2788            prop::DEV_CHANNEL_KEYS,
2789            prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE,
2790        ]);
2791    }
2792    if has(cap::ADMIN) {
2793        properties.push(prop::DEV_ADMINS);
2794    }
2795    if has(cap::ALERT) {
2796        // Read at attach so a phone reconnecting mid-search finds the
2797        // alert it left running rather than a stale "off".
2798        properties.push(prop::ALERT);
2799    }
2800    if has(cap::TIME) {
2801        // The clock is live rather than configuration, but it is read
2802        // here for the same reason the alert is: a phone that just
2803        // attached should know whether the device knows the time, not
2804        // wait for the next announcement to find out.
2805        properties.extend([prop::TIME, prop::TZ_OFFSET]);
2806    }
2807    if has(cap::GNSS) {
2808        properties.extend([
2809            prop::GNSS_ENABLED,
2810            prop::GNSS_LOCATION,
2811            prop::GNSS_ALTITUDE,
2812            prop::GNSS_FIX,
2813            prop::GNSS_PRECISION,
2814            prop::GNSS_SATELLITES,
2815            prop::GNSS_IDENT_UPDATE,
2816            prop::GNSS_IDENT_PRECISION,
2817            prop::GNSS_TIME_TRUST,
2818        ]);
2819    }
2820    if has(cap::ADVERT) {
2821        properties.extend([
2822            prop::ADVERT_INTERVAL,
2823            prop::BEACON_INTERVAL,
2824            prop::STARTUP_BEACON,
2825        ]);
2826    }
2827    Ok(properties)
2828}
2829
2830pub(crate) fn ulcp_refresh_properties(capabilities: Vec<u8>) -> Result<Vec<u32>, MobileError> {
2831    let decoded = decode_capabilities(&capabilities)?;
2832    validate_capability_dependencies(&decoded)?;
2833    let has = |capability| decoded.contains(&capability);
2834    let mut properties = Vec::new();
2835    if has(cap::DEV_IDENTITY) {
2836        properties.push(prop::DEV_KEY);
2837    }
2838    if has(cap::DEV_NAME) {
2839        properties.push(prop::DEV_NAME);
2840    }
2841    if has(cap::BATTERY) {
2842        properties.push(prop::BATTERY);
2843    }
2844    if has(cap::HOST_FILTER) {
2845        properties.push(prop::HOST_KEY);
2846    }
2847    properties.extend(ulcp_inspection_properties(capabilities)?);
2848    Ok(properties)
2849}
2850
2851/// Validate and reduce the property responses from the read-only post-attach
2852/// inspection.
2853///
2854/// The four properties every ULCP device must answer — the interface type
2855/// and the live PHY triple — are required: without them there is no radio
2856/// to describe. Everything else is capability-gated and merely *expected*,
2857/// so a device that refuses one, or answers it with something undecodable,
2858/// yields a snapshot with that setting absent and named in
2859/// `unreadable_properties` rather than no snapshot at all.
2860#[uniffi::export]
2861pub fn inspect_ulcp_sync(
2862    responses: Vec<UlcpPropertyFrameRecord>,
2863) -> Result<UlcpSyncRecord, MobileError> {
2864    let value = |key| property_value(&responses, key);
2865    let capabilities = decode_capabilities(value(prop::CAPS)?)?;
2866    validate_capability_dependencies(&capabilities)?;
2867    let has = |capability| capabilities.contains(&capability);
2868
2869    let interface = decode_exact_pui(value(prop::INTERFACE_TYPE)?)?;
2870    if interface != INTERFACE_TYPE {
2871        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
2872    }
2873    let phy_enabled = decode_bool(value(prop::PHY_ENABLED)?)?;
2874    let frequency_khz = decode_u32(value(prop::PHY_FREQ)?)?;
2875    let transmit_power_dbm = decode_i8(value(prop::PHY_TX_POWER)?)?;
2876
2877    let mut expected = ExpectedProperties {
2878        responses: &responses,
2879        unreadable: Vec::new(),
2880    };
2881    let lora = has(cap::PHY_LORA);
2882    let bandwidth_hz = expected.read(lora, prop::PHY_LORA_BW, decode_u32);
2883    let spreading_factor = expected.read(lora, prop::PHY_LORA_SF, decode_u8);
2884    let coding_rate_denom = expected.read(lora, prop::PHY_LORA_CR, decode_u8);
2885    let duty = has(cap::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT);
2886    let duty_cycle_now = expected.read(duty, prop::PHY_DUTY_NOW, decode_u16);
2887    let duty_cycle_limit = expected.read(duty, prop::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT, decode_u16);
2888    let saved = expected.read(has(cap::SAVE), prop::SAVED, decode_saved);
2889    let queue = has(cap::HOST_RX_QUEUE);
2890    let queued_frames = expected.read(queue, prop::HOST_RX_QUEUE_COUNT, decode_u16);
2891    let dropped_frames = expected.read(queue, prop::HOST_RX_QUEUE_DROPPED, decode_u32);
2892    let filter_count = expected.read(
2893        has(cap::HOST_FILTER),
2894        prop::HOST_RX_FILTERS,
2895        decode_filter_count,
2896    );
2897    let host_keys = has(cap::HOST_KEYS);
2898    let host_channel_count = expected.read(
2899        host_keys,
2900        prop::HOST_CHANNEL_KEYS,
2901        decode_fixed_count::<{ items::CHANNEL_ID_LEN }>,
2902    );
2903    let host_peer_count = expected.read(
2904        host_keys,
2905        prop::HOST_PEER_KEYS,
2906        decode_fixed_count::<{ items::PUBLIC_KEY_LEN }>,
2907    );
2908    let auto_ack = expected.read(has(cap::HOST_AUTO_ACK), prop::HOST_AUTO_ACK, decode_bool);
2909    let dev_identity = has(cap::DEV_IDENTITY);
2910    let dev_peer_keys = expected.read(
2911        dev_identity,
2912        prop::DEV_PEERS,
2913        decode_fixed_list::<{ items::PUBLIC_KEY_LEN }>,
2914    );
2915    let dev_channel_ids = expected.read(
2916        dev_identity,
2917        prop::DEV_CHANNEL_KEYS,
2918        decode_fixed_list::<{ items::CHANNEL_ID_LEN }>,
2919    );
2920    let manageable = has(cap::ADMIN);
2921    let dev_admin_keys = expected.read(
2922        manageable,
2923        prop::DEV_ADMINS,
2924        decode_fixed_list::<{ items::PUBLIC_KEY_LEN }>,
2925    );
2926    // Read here as well as onto the session snapshot, because a phone
2927    // reading a device across the mesh has no session with it and this
2928    // record is the whole of what it learned.
2929    let device_name = expected
2930        .read(has(cap::DEV_NAME), prop::DEV_NAME, decode_device_name)
2931        .flatten();
2932
2933    // Readings, not settings, so they go around `expected`: a device that
2934    // has not reported its battery yet has not withheld a setting, and
2935    // saying it had would put "its battery" in a notice about configuration
2936    // this phone is about to write over. On the local link neither is even
2937    // asked for at attach — the battery arrives unsolicited — while a
2938    // reading across the mesh asks for both and gets an answer or none.
2939    let battery = reported_value(&responses, has(cap::BATTERY), prop::BATTERY, |value| {
2940        inspect_ulcp_battery(value.to_vec())
2941    });
2942    let alert = reported_value(&responses, has(cap::ALERT), prop::ALERT, |value| {
2943        inspect_ulcp_alert(value.to_vec())
2944    });
2945
2946    // Every part of the policy is read before any of it is required, so one
2947    // unreadable property does not hide the others behind it.
2948    let forwards = has(cap::REPEATER);
2949    let repeater_enabled = expected.read(forwards, prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED, decode_bool);
2950    let regions = expected.read(forwards, prop::MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS, decode_region_list);
2951    let default_region = expected.read(
2952        forwards,
2953        prop::MAC_REPEATER_DEFAULT_REGION,
2954        decode_optional_region,
2955    );
2956    let min_rssi_dbm = expected.read(forwards, prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI, |value| {
2957        decode_optional(value, decode_i16)
2958    });
2959    let min_snr_db = expected.read(forwards, prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_SNR, |value| {
2960        decode_optional(value, decode_i8)
2961    });
2962    let repeater = (|| {
2963        Some(UlcpRepeaterSettingsRecord {
2964            enabled: repeater_enabled?,
2965            regions: regions?,
2966            default_region: default_region?,
2967            min_rssi_dbm: min_rssi_dbm?,
2968            min_snr_db: min_snr_db?,
2969        })
2970    })();
2971
2972    // An empty PROP_IDENT_ROLE is the device saying it derives its own
2973    // role, which is the same `None` a device without CAP_IDENT reports.
2974    let ident = has(cap::IDENT);
2975    let ident_role = expected
2976        .read(ident, prop::IDENT_ROLE, |value| {
2977            decode_optional(value, decode_u8)
2978        })
2979        .flatten();
2980    let ident_mobile = expected.read(ident, prop::IDENT_MOBILE, decode_bool);
2981    // Read as a pair, like the policies above: the cell and the height
2982    // are one statement of where the device is, and a device keeping its
2983    // own position refuses both together.
2984    let ident_location = expected.read(ident, prop::IDENT_LOCATION, |value| {
2985        Ok::<Vec<u8>, MobileError>(value.to_vec())
2986    });
2987    let ident_altitude = expected.read(ident, prop::IDENT_ALTITUDE, decode_optional_altitude);
2988    let ident_position = (|| {
2989        let location = ident_location?;
2990        let placed = (!location.is_empty()).then(|| NodeLocation::from_bytes(&location).center());
2991        Some(UlcpIdentPositionRecord {
2992            latitude_deg: placed.map(|(latitude, _)| latitude.into()),
2993            longitude_deg: placed.map(|(_, longitude)| longitude.into()),
2994            cell_meters: (!location.is_empty())
2995                .then(|| ulcp_location_cell_meters(location.len() as u8))
2996                .flatten(),
2997            altitude_m: ident_altitude?,
2998            location,
2999        })
3000    })();
3001    let dev_discoverable = expected.read(dev_identity, prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE, decode_bool);
3002
3003    let tz_offset_min = expected.read(has(cap::TIME), prop::TZ_OFFSET, decode_i16);
3004
3005    // Read whole, like the forwarding policy above and for the same
3006    // reason: this is written as a set.
3007    let positioning = has(cap::GNSS);
3008    let gnss_enabled = expected.read(positioning, prop::GNSS_ENABLED, decode_bool);
3009    let ident_update = expected.read(positioning, prop::GNSS_IDENT_UPDATE, decode_bool);
3010    let ident_precision = expected.read(positioning, prop::GNSS_IDENT_PRECISION, decode_precision);
3011    let time_trust = expected.read(positioning, prop::GNSS_TIME_TRUST, decode_bool);
3012    let gnss = (|| {
3013        Some(UlcpGnssSettingsRecord {
3014            enabled: gnss_enabled?,
3015            ident_update: ident_update?,
3016            ident_precision: ident_precision?,
3017            time_trust: time_trust?,
3018        })
3019    })();
3020
3021    // Also read whole: the two schedules together are how much airtime
3022    // this device claims, which is one decision.
3023    let announces = has(cap::ADVERT);
3024    let advert_interval = expected.read(announces, prop::ADVERT_INTERVAL, decode_u32);
3025    let beacon_interval = expected.read(announces, prop::BEACON_INTERVAL, decode_u32);
3026    let startup_beacon = expected.read(announces, prop::STARTUP_BEACON, decode_bool);
3027    let advert = (|| {
3028        Some(UlcpAdvertSettingsRecord {
3029            advert_interval_seconds: advert_interval?,
3030            beacon_interval_seconds: beacon_interval?,
3031            startup_beacon: startup_beacon?,
3032        })
3033    })();
3034
3035    let mut unreadable_properties = expected.unreadable;
3036    unreadable_properties.sort_unstable();
3037
3038    Ok(UlcpSyncRecord {
3039        capability_count: capabilities
3040            .len()
3041            .try_into()
3042            .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?,
3043        has_host_filtering: has(cap::HOST_FILTER),
3044        supports_offline_queue: has(cap::HOST_RX_QUEUE),
3045        supports_delegated_ack: has(cap::HOST_AUTO_ACK),
3046        supports_device_name: has(cap::DEV_NAME),
3047        device_name,
3048        supports_lora: has(cap::PHY_LORA),
3049        supports_duty_cycle_limit: has(cap::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT),
3050        supports_battery: has(cap::BATTERY),
3051        battery,
3052        supports_repeater: has(cap::REPEATER),
3053        supports_ident: has(cap::IDENT),
3054        supports_device_identity: has(cap::DEV_IDENTITY),
3055        supports_time: has(cap::TIME),
3056        supports_gnss: positioning,
3057        supports_advert: announces,
3058        supports_admin: manageable,
3059        supports_alert: has(cap::ALERT),
3060        supports_reboot: has(cap::REBOOT),
3061        alert,
3062        phy_enabled,
3063        frequency_khz,
3064        transmit_power_dbm,
3065        bandwidth_hz,
3066        spreading_factor,
3067        coding_rate_denom,
3068        duty_cycle_now,
3069        duty_cycle_limit,
3070        saved,
3071        queued_frames,
3072        dropped_frames,
3073        filter_count,
3074        host_channel_count,
3075        host_peer_count,
3076        auto_ack,
3077        repeater,
3078        dev_peer_keys,
3079        dev_admin_keys,
3080        dev_channel_ids,
3081        ident_role,
3082        ident_mobile,
3083        ident_position,
3084        dev_discoverable,
3085        tz_offset_min,
3086        gnss,
3087        advert,
3088        unreadable_properties,
3089    })
3090}
3091
3092// ─── Managing one device, a screen at a time ─────────────────────────────
3093
3094/// One screenful of a device's settings.
3095///
3096/// Reading a device whole costs tens of properties and several round
3097/// trips, which over a link a few hops deep is the difference between a
3098/// screen that opens and one that is waited on. A category is what one
3099/// screen shows, and asking for exactly that is normally one exchange.
3100#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, uniffi::Enum)]
3101pub enum UlcpManageCategory {
3102    /// What the battery reports. Read-only.
3103    Power,
3104    /// The radio: frequency, power, the modem profile, the duty ledger.
3105    Radio,
3106    /// What the device advertises about itself, including where it is.
3107    Identity,
3108    /// The receiver, and what it currently sees. The fix is read-only.
3109    Gnss,
3110    /// The wall clock: what time the device holds, where it is meant to
3111    /// be, and whether the receiver may set the clock.
3112    Time,
3113    /// The forwarding policy.
3114    Repeater,
3115    /// Who this device talks to, and who may manage it.
3116    PeerNodes,
3117}
3118
3119/// The property numbers the management screens name.
3120///
3121/// A screen has to say which fields the operator edited, and it caches
3122/// values under the number the device answered for, so the numbers cross
3123/// the boundary whether or not anyone likes it. Handing them over once,
3124/// from the same constants everything else here is built on, is what
3125/// keeps a second copy from being written down somewhere in Swift.
3126#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, uniffi::Record)]
3127pub struct UlcpManagedPropertyIds {
3128    pub caps: u32,
3129    pub device_version: u32,
3130    pub device_model: u32,
3131    pub device_name: u32,
3132    pub battery: u32,
3133    pub phy_enabled: u32,
3134    pub frequency: u32,
3135    pub transmit_power: u32,
3136    pub lora_bandwidth: u32,
3137    pub lora_spreading_factor: u32,
3138    pub lora_coding_rate: u32,
3139    pub duty_cycle_now: u32,
3140    pub duty_cycle_limit: u32,
3141    pub ident_role: u32,
3142    pub ident_mobile: u32,
3143    pub ident_location: u32,
3144    pub ident_altitude: u32,
3145    pub dev_discoverable: u32,
3146    pub gnss_ident_update: u32,
3147    pub gnss_ident_precision: u32,
3148    pub uptime: u32,
3149    pub advert_interval: u32,
3150    pub beacon_interval: u32,
3151    pub startup_beacon: u32,
3152    pub gnss_enabled: u32,
3153    pub gnss_time_trust: u32,
3154    pub time: u32,
3155    pub tz_offset: u32,
3156    pub alert: u32,
3157    pub repeater_enabled: u32,
3158    pub repeater_regions: u32,
3159    pub repeater_default_region: u32,
3160    pub repeater_min_rssi: u32,
3161    pub repeater_min_snr: u32,
3162    pub dev_peers: u32,
3163    pub dev_admins: u32,
3164}
3165

3169    UlcpManagedPropertyIds {
