3177        transmit_power: prop::PHY_TX_POWER,
3178        lora_bandwidth: prop::PHY_LORA_BW,
3179        lora_spreading_factor: prop::PHY_LORA_SF,
3180        lora_coding_rate: prop::PHY_LORA_CR,
3181        duty_cycle_now: prop::PHY_DUTY_NOW,
3182        duty_cycle_limit: prop::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT,
3183        ident_role: prop::IDENT_ROLE,
3184        ident_mobile: prop::IDENT_MOBILE,
3185        ident_location: prop::IDENT_LOCATION,
3186        ident_altitude: prop::IDENT_ALTITUDE,
3187        dev_discoverable: prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE,
3188        gnss_ident_update: prop::GNSS_IDENT_UPDATE,
3189        gnss_ident_precision: prop::GNSS_IDENT_PRECISION,
3190        uptime: prop::UPTIME,
3191        advert_interval: prop::ADVERT_INTERVAL,
3192        beacon_interval: prop::BEACON_INTERVAL,
3193        startup_beacon: prop::STARTUP_BEACON,
3194        gnss_enabled: prop::GNSS_ENABLED,
3195        gnss_time_trust: prop::GNSS_TIME_TRUST,
3196        time: prop::TIME,
3197        tz_offset: prop::TZ_OFFSET,
3198        alert: prop::ALERT,
3199        repeater_enabled: prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED,
3200        repeater_regions: prop::MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS,
3201        repeater_default_region: prop::MAC_REPEATER_DEFAULT_REGION,
3202        repeater_min_rssi: prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI,
3203        repeater_min_snr: prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_SNR,
3204        dev_peers: prop::DEV_PEERS,
3205        dev_admins: prop::DEV_ADMINS,
3206    }
3207}
3208
3209/// The four properties a device is identified by, asked for together.
3210///
3211/// Capabilities first, so a device that declines the batch teaches the
3212/// crawl to ask one at a time before the rest.
3213#[uniffi::export]
3214pub fn ulcp_card_properties() -> Vec<u32> {
3215    vec![
3216        prop::CAPS,
3217        prop::DEV_VERSION,
3218        prop::DEV_MODEL,
3219        prop::DEV_NAME,
3220    ]
3221}
3222
3223/// What one category asks for, given what the device says it can do.
3224///
3225/// Capability-gated so a screen never spends airtime on a property the
3226/// device does not have, and filtered to what an administrator may
3227/// reach.
3228#[uniffi::export]
3229pub fn ulcp_category_properties(
3230    category: UlcpManageCategory,
3231    capabilities: Vec<u8>,
3232) -> Result<Vec<u32>, MobileError> {
3233    let capabilities = decode_capabilities(&capabilities)?;
3234    validate_capability_dependencies(&capabilities)?;
3235    let has = |capability| capabilities.contains(&capability);
3236    let mut properties = Vec::new();
3237    let mut when = |gate: bool, keys: &[u32]| {
3238        if gate {
3239            properties.extend_from_slice(keys);
3240        }
3241    };
3242
3243    match category {
3244        UlcpManageCategory::Power => when(has(cap::BATTERY), &[prop::BATTERY]),
3245        UlcpManageCategory::Radio => {
3246            when(
3247                true,
3248                &[prop::PHY_ENABLED, prop::PHY_FREQ, prop::PHY_TX_POWER],
3249            );
3250            when(
3251                has(cap::PHY_LORA),
3252                &[prop::PHY_LORA_BW, prop::PHY_LORA_SF, prop::PHY_LORA_CR],
3253            );
3254            when(
3255                has(cap::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT),
3256                &[prop::PHY_DUTY_NOW, prop::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT],
3257            );
3258        }
3259        UlcpManageCategory::Identity => {
3260            when(has(cap::DEV_NAME), &[prop::DEV_NAME]);
3261            when(
3262                has(cap::IDENT),
3263                &[
3264                    prop::IDENT_ROLE,
3265                    prop::IDENT_MOBILE,
3266                    prop::IDENT_LOCATION,
3267                    prop::IDENT_ALTITUDE,
3268                ],
3269            );
3270            when(has(cap::DEV_IDENTITY), &[prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE]);
3271            // Whether the device maintains its own position decides
3272            // whether the location rows above are editable at all, so
3273            // this screen has to know even though the receiver has its
3274            // own.
3275            when(
3276                has(cap::GNSS),
3277                &[prop::GNSS_IDENT_UPDATE, prop::GNSS_IDENT_PRECISION],
3278            );
3279            when(
3280                has(cap::ADVERT),
3281                &[
3282                    prop::ADVERT_INTERVAL,
3283                    prop::BEACON_INTERVAL,
3284                    prop::STARTUP_BEACON,
3285                ],
3286            );
3287        }
3288        UlcpManageCategory::Gnss => when(
3289            has(cap::GNSS),
3290            &[
3291                prop::GNSS_ENABLED,
3292                prop::GNSS_LOCATION,
3293                prop::GNSS_ALTITUDE,
3294                prop::GNSS_FIX,
3295                prop::GNSS_PRECISION,
3296                prop::GNSS_SATELLITES,
3297            ],
3298        ),
3299        UlcpManageCategory::Time => {
3300            // Ungated: a device with no wall clock may still know how
3301            // long it has been up, and a refusal costs one slot.
3302            when(true, &[prop::UPTIME]);
3303            when(has(cap::TIME), &[prop::TIME, prop::TZ_OFFSET]);
3304            // Whether the receiver may set the clock is the clock's
3305            // business, so it lives here rather than with the receiver.
3306            when(has(cap::GNSS), &[prop::GNSS_TIME_TRUST]);
3307        }
3308        UlcpManageCategory::Repeater => when(
3309            has(cap::REPEATER),
3310            &[
3311                prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED,
3312                prop::MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS,
3313                prop::MAC_REPEATER_DEFAULT_REGION,
3314                prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI,
3315                prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_SNR,
3316            ],
3317        ),
3318        UlcpManageCategory::PeerNodes => {
3319            when(has(cap::DEV_IDENTITY), &[prop::DEV_PEERS]);
3320            when(has(cap::ADMIN), &[prop::DEV_ADMINS]);
3321        }
3322    }
3323    properties.retain(|&key| umsh_ulcp::ids::admin_reachable(key));
3324    Ok(properties)
3325}
3326
3327/// What a device is, as opposed to how it is configured.
3328///
3329/// The four properties worth learning once and keeping: capabilities and
3330/// firmware version change only when the firmware does, the model never,
3331/// and the name rarely. Cached against the version, this is what lets
3332/// opening a device's settings cost nothing at all.
3333#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, uniffi::Record)]
3334pub struct UlcpDeviceCardRecord {
3335    /// `PROP_CAPS` verbatim, to plan later reads against without asking
3336    /// the device again.
3337    pub capabilities: Vec<u8>,
3338    /// `PROP_DEV_VERSION`: what firmware it is running. The natural key
3339    /// for everything cached about it — capabilities cannot change
3340    /// without this changing too.
3341    pub device_version: Option<String>,
3342    /// `PROP_DEV_MODEL`: the hardware, when the device names it.
3343    pub device_model: Option<String>,
3344    pub device_name: Option<String>,
3345    pub supports_device_name: bool,
3346    pub supports_battery: bool,
3347    pub supports_lora: bool,
3348    pub supports_duty_cycle_limit: bool,
3349    pub supports_repeater: bool,
3350    pub supports_ident: bool,
3351    pub supports_device_identity: bool,
3352    pub supports_gnss: bool,
3353    pub supports_advert: bool,
3354    pub supports_admin: bool,
3355    pub supports_alert: bool,
3356    /// Whether a Restart control is worth offering (`CAP_REBOOT`).
3357    pub supports_reboot: bool,
3358    pub supports_save: bool,
3359    /// Whether batched property reads are worth trying. A device that
3360    /// declines one is asked again a property at a time, so this is a
3361    /// hint rather than a contract.
3362    pub supports_multi: bool,
3363}
3364
3365/// Reduce the answers to a card read into what a device is.
3366///
3367/// Capabilities are required — without them there is nothing to plan the
3368/// rest against. Everything else is absent rather than fatal: a device
3369/// that will not name its hardware is a device that does not name its
3370/// hardware.
3371#[uniffi::export]
3372pub fn inspect_ulcp_device_card(
3373    responses: Vec<UlcpPropertyFrameRecord>,
3374) -> Result<UlcpDeviceCardRecord, MobileError> {
3375    let raw = property_value(&responses, prop::CAPS)?.to_vec();
3376    let capabilities = decode_capabilities(&raw)?;
3377    validate_capability_dependencies(&capabilities)?;
3378    let has = |capability| capabilities.contains(&capability);
3379    let text = |key| {
3380        property_value(&responses, key)
3381            .and_then(decode_device_name)
3382            .ok()
3383            .flatten()
3384    };
3385
3386    Ok(UlcpDeviceCardRecord {
3387        capabilities: raw,
3388        device_version: text(prop::DEV_VERSION),
3389        device_model: text(prop::DEV_MODEL),
3390        device_name: text(prop::DEV_NAME),
3391        supports_device_name: has(cap::DEV_NAME),
3392        supports_battery: has(cap::BATTERY),
3393        supports_lora: has(cap::PHY_LORA),
3394        supports_duty_cycle_limit: has(cap::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT),
3395        supports_repeater: has(cap::REPEATER),
3396        supports_ident: has(cap::IDENT),
3397        supports_device_identity: has(cap::DEV_IDENTITY),
3398        supports_gnss: has(cap::GNSS),
3399        supports_advert: has(cap::ADVERT),
3400        supports_admin: has(cap::ADMIN),
3401        supports_alert: has(cap::ALERT),
3402        supports_reboot: has(cap::REBOOT),
3403        supports_save: has(cap::SAVE),
3404        supports_multi: has(cap::CMD_MULTI),
3405    })
3406}
3407
3408/// Everything the six management screens show, all of it optional.
3409///
3410/// A category read answers a handful of properties, so anything outside
3411/// it is simply absent — this record says what the last read of *some*
3412/// category found, and a screen fills in from it whatever it recognizes.
3413/// The counterpart to [`UlcpSyncRecord`], which describes a whole device
3414/// and can insist on the properties every device must answer.
3415#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, uniffi::Record)]
3416pub struct UlcpDevicePropertiesRecord {
3417    pub battery: Option<UlcpBatteryRecord>,
3418    pub phy_enabled: Option<bool>,
3419    pub frequency_khz: Option<u32>,
3420    pub transmit_power_dbm: Option<i8>,
3421    pub bandwidth_hz: Option<u32>,
3422    pub spreading_factor: Option<u8>,
3423    pub coding_rate_denom: Option<u8>,
3424    pub duty_cycle_now: Option<u16>,
3425    pub duty_cycle_limit: Option<u16>,
3426    pub device_name: Option<String>,
3427    /// `None` is the device deriving its own role, which reads the same
3428    /// as never having been told.
3429    pub ident_role: Option<u8>,
3430    pub ident_mobile: Option<bool>,
3431    /// `PROP_IDENT_LOCATION` verbatim. Empty is a device advertising no
3432    /// position, which is different from not having been asked.
3433    pub ident_location: Option<Vec<u8>>,
3434    pub ident_latitude_deg: Option<f64>,
3435    pub ident_longitude_deg: Option<f64>,
3436    /// Width of the advertised cell at the equator, in meters — what the
3437    /// length of the location actually discloses.
3438    pub ident_location_cell_meters: Option<f64>,
3439    pub ident_altitude_m: Option<i32>,
3440    pub dev_discoverable: Option<bool>,
3441    /// Whether the device maintains its own advertised position. Set,
3442    /// the location and altitude above are the device's to write and a
3443    /// host's write is refused.
3444    pub gnss_ident_update: Option<bool>,
3445    pub gnss_ident_precision: Option<u8>,
3446    /// `PROP_UPTIME`: seconds since the device booted. Absent on a
3447    /// device that does not report it.
3448    pub uptime_seconds: Option<u32>,
3449    pub advert_interval_seconds: Option<u32>,
3450    pub beacon_interval_seconds: Option<u32>,
3451    pub startup_beacon: Option<bool>,
3452    pub gnss_enabled: Option<bool>,
3453    /// What the receiver currently sees. Read-only, and absent on a
3454    /// device with no receiver.
3455    pub gnss: Option<UlcpGnssRecord>,
3456    pub gnss_time_trust: Option<bool>,
3457    /// What the device's clock read when it answered. Present when the
3458    /// clock was asked about; the inner epoch is absent on a device that
3459    /// has not found the time.
3460    pub time: Option<UlcpTimeRecord>,
3461    pub tz_offset_min: Option<i16>,
3462    pub repeater_enabled: Option<bool>,
3463    pub repeater_regions: Option<Vec<String>>,
3464    pub repeater_default_region: Option<Vec<u8>>,
3465    pub repeater_min_rssi_dbm: Option<i16>,
3466    pub repeater_min_snr_db: Option<i8>,
3467    pub dev_peer_keys: Option<Vec<Vec<u8>>>,
3468    pub dev_admin_keys: Option<Vec<Vec<u8>>>,
3469}
3470
3471/// Encode a place as the cell a device advertises it from.
3472///
3473/// The counterpart to the latitude and longitude an inspection reports:
3474/// what goes on the air is a cell rather than a point, and how large that
3475/// cell is — the precision, which is also the value's length — is what the
3476/// device discloses. See [`ulcp_location_cell_meters`] for what each
3477/// precision is worth in meters.
3478///
3479/// Precision must be 1 through 7. A coordinate outside its own range is
3480/// refused rather than wrapped: a longitude of 200° is a typo, not a
3481/// place.
3482#[uniffi::export]
3483pub fn ulcp_encode_location(
3484    latitude_deg: f64,
3485    longitude_deg: f64,
3486    precision: u8,
3487) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MobileError> {
3488    if !(1..=MAX_PRECISION).contains(&precision)
3489        || !(-90.0..=90.0).contains(&latitude_deg)
3490        || !(-180.0..=180.0).contains(&longitude_deg)
3491    {
3492        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
3493    }
3494    // Through the exact constructor rather than the float one: at the
3495    // finest precisions, rounding a typed-in coordinate into a binary
3496    // float can land it in the neighboring cell.
3497    let e7 = |degrees: f64| (degrees * 1e7).round() as i32;
3498    Ok(
3499        NodeLocation::from_e7(e7(latitude_deg), e7(longitude_deg), precision)
3500            .as_bytes()
3501            .to_vec(),
3502    )
3503}
3504
3505/// Present one remembered value as the property frame the inspectors read.
3506///
3507/// What [`ulcp_records_from_answers`] does for a fresh answer, for a value
3508/// that came out of a cache instead. Cached octets and answered octets are
3509/// the same octets, so they decode through the same path — and a caller
3510/// never has to know which command byte a reported value wears.
3511#[uniffi::export]
3512pub fn ulcp_property_record(property_id: u32, value: Vec<u8>) -> UlcpPropertyFrameRecord {
3513    UlcpPropertyFrameRecord {
3514        // A management exchange is correlated by its envelope token, so
3515        // every request on one carries transaction zero.
3516        transaction_id: 0,
3517        command: Cmd::PropIs as u8,
3518        property_id,
3519        value,
3520    }
3521}
3522
3523/// Decode whatever a category read answered.
3524///
3525/// Nothing is required and nothing fails: a property that did not come
3526/// back, or came back undecodable, is left absent. Which properties were
3527/// *refused* is a separate question, and the answers to the read say so
3528/// directly.
3529#[uniffi::export]
3530pub fn inspect_ulcp_properties(
3531    responses: Vec<UlcpPropertyFrameRecord>,
3532) -> UlcpDevicePropertiesRecord {
3533    let at = &responses;
3534    let location = optional_value(at, prop::IDENT_LOCATION, |value| {
3535        Ok::<Vec<u8>, MobileError>(value.to_vec())
3536    });
3537    let placed = location
3538        .as_ref()
3539        .filter(|bytes| !bytes.is_empty())
3540        .map(|bytes| NodeLocation::from_bytes(bytes).center());
3541
3542    UlcpDevicePropertiesRecord {
3543        battery: optional_value(at, prop::BATTERY, |value| {
3544            inspect_ulcp_battery(value.to_vec())
3545        }),
3546        phy_enabled: optional_value(at, prop::PHY_ENABLED, decode_bool),
3547        frequency_khz: optional_value(at, prop::PHY_FREQ, decode_u32),
3548        transmit_power_dbm: optional_value(at, prop::PHY_TX_POWER, decode_i8),
3549        bandwidth_hz: optional_value(at, prop::PHY_LORA_BW, decode_u32),
3550        spreading_factor: optional_value(at, prop::PHY_LORA_SF, decode_u8),
3551        coding_rate_denom: optional_value(at, prop::PHY_LORA_CR, decode_u8),
3552        duty_cycle_now: optional_value(at, prop::PHY_DUTY_NOW, decode_u16),
3553        duty_cycle_limit: optional_value(at, prop::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT, decode_u16),
3554        device_name: optional_value(at, prop::DEV_NAME, decode_device_name).flatten(),
3555        ident_role: optional_value(at, prop::IDENT_ROLE, |value| {
3556            decode_optional(value, decode_u8)
3557        })
3558        .flatten(),
3559        ident_mobile: optional_value(at, prop::IDENT_MOBILE, decode_bool),
3560        ident_latitude_deg: placed.map(|(latitude, _)| latitude.into()),
3561        ident_longitude_deg: placed.map(|(_, longitude)| longitude.into()),
3562        ident_location_cell_meters: location
3563            .as_ref()
3564            .filter(|bytes| !bytes.is_empty())
3565            .and_then(|bytes| ulcp_location_cell_meters(bytes.len() as u8)),
3566        ident_location: location,
3567        ident_altitude_m: optional_value(at, prop::IDENT_ALTITUDE, decode_optional_altitude)
3568            .flatten(),
3569        dev_discoverable: optional_value(at, prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE, decode_bool),
3570        gnss_ident_update: optional_value(at, prop::GNSS_IDENT_UPDATE, decode_bool),
3571        gnss_ident_precision: optional_value(at, prop::GNSS_IDENT_PRECISION, decode_precision),
3572        uptime_seconds: optional_value(at, prop::UPTIME, decode_u32),
3573        advert_interval_seconds: optional_value(at, prop::ADVERT_INTERVAL, decode_u32),
3574        beacon_interval_seconds: optional_value(at, prop::BEACON_INTERVAL, decode_u32),
3575        startup_beacon: optional_value(at, prop::STARTUP_BEACON, decode_bool),
3576        gnss_enabled: optional_value(at, prop::GNSS_ENABLED, decode_bool),
3577        gnss: gnss_readout(at),
3578        gnss_time_trust: optional_value(at, prop::GNSS_TIME_TRUST, decode_bool),
3579        time: optional_value(at, prop::TIME, |value| {
3580            Ok::<UlcpTimeRecord, MobileError>(UlcpTimeRecord {
3581                epoch_seconds: decode_optional(value, decode_u32)?,
3582            })
3583        }),
3584        tz_offset_min: optional_value(at, prop::TZ_OFFSET, decode_i16),
3585        repeater_enabled: optional_value(at, prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED, decode_bool),
3586        repeater_regions: optional_value(at, prop::MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS, decode_region_list),
3587        repeater_default_region: optional_value(
3588            at,
3589            prop::MAC_REPEATER_DEFAULT_REGION,
3590            decode_optional_region,
3591        )
3592        .flatten(),
3593        repeater_min_rssi_dbm: optional_value(at, prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI, |value| {
3594            decode_optional(value, decode_i16)
3595        })
3596        .flatten(),
3597        repeater_min_snr_db: optional_value(at, prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_SNR, |value| {
3598            decode_optional(value, decode_i8)
3599        })
3600        .flatten(),
3601        dev_peer_keys: optional_value(
3602            at,
3603            prop::DEV_PEERS,
3604            decode_fixed_list::<{ items::PUBLIC_KEY_LEN }>,
3605        ),
3606        dev_admin_keys: optional_value(
3607            at,
3608            prop::DEV_ADMINS,
3609            decode_fixed_list::<{ items::PUBLIC_KEY_LEN }>,
3610        ),
3611    }
3612}
3613
3614/// Fold whatever positioning properties came back into one readout.
3615///
3616/// `None` unless the fix indicator arrived: without it there is nothing
3617/// to say whether the rest describes a position or the absence of one.
3618fn gnss_readout(responses: &[UlcpPropertyFrameRecord]) -> Option<UlcpGnssRecord> {
3619    let mut snapshot = GnssSnapshot::SEARCHING;
3620    property_value(responses, prop::GNSS_FIX)
3621        .ok()
3622        .and_then(|value| snapshot.absorb(prop::GNSS_FIX, value).ok())?;
3623    for key in [
3624        prop::GNSS_LOCATION,
3625        prop::GNSS_ALTITUDE,
3626        prop::GNSS_PRECISION,
3627        prop::GNSS_SATELLITES,
3628    ] {
3629        if let Ok(value) = property_value(responses, key) {
3630            let _ = snapshot.absorb(key, value);
3631        }
3632    }
3633    Some(gnss_record(&snapshot))
3634}
3635
3636/// `PROP_IDENT_ALTITUDE`: empty, or a minimal-length signed integer.
3637fn decode_optional_altitude(value: &[u8]) -> Result<Option<i32>, MobileError> {
3638    match value {
3639        [] => Ok(None),
3640        bytes => umsh_ulcp::sint::decode(bytes)
3641            .map(Some)
3642            .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame),
3643    }
3644}
3645
3646/// Encode only the properties the operator actually changed.
3647///
3648/// The whole point of the category screens: a device several hops away
3649/// takes one write for one edit, rather than a restatement of its entire
3650/// configuration. `dirty_property_ids` names what was edited; anything
3651/// else in `desired` is ignored, so a record filled in from a stale
3652/// cache cannot write stale values back.
3653///
3654/// The radio is bracketed when any of its parameters move: the PHY goes
3655/// down first and comes back up last, so a device is never asked to
3656/// change the frequency it is transmitting on.
3657#[uniffi::export]
3658pub fn ulcp_dirty_writes(
3659    desired: UlcpDevicePropertiesRecord,
3660    dirty_property_ids: Vec<u32>,
3661) -> Result<Vec<MobileMeshPropertyWriteRecord>, MobileError> {
3662    let mut dirty: Vec<u32> = dirty_property_ids;
3663    dirty.sort_unstable();
3664    dirty.dedup();
3665
3666    let mut values: Vec<(u32, Vec<u8>)> = Vec::new();
3667    for key in &dirty {
3668        // A property named as edited whose value is absent is a caller
3669        // mistake: there is nothing to write, and silently skipping it
3670        // would report success for an edit that never happened.
3671        let missing = || MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame;
3672        let value = match *key {
3673            prop::PHY_ENABLED => vec![desired.phy_enabled.ok_or_else(missing)? as u8],
3674            prop::PHY_FREQ => desired
3675                .frequency_khz
3676                .ok_or_else(missing)?
3677                .to_le_bytes()
3678                .to_vec(),
3679            prop::PHY_TX_POWER => vec![desired.transmit_power_dbm.ok_or_else(missing)? as u8],
3680            prop::PHY_LORA_BW => desired
3681                .bandwidth_hz
3682                .ok_or_else(missing)?
3683                .to_le_bytes()
3684                .to_vec(),
3685            prop::PHY_LORA_SF => vec![desired.spreading_factor.ok_or_else(missing)?],
3686            prop::PHY_LORA_CR => vec![desired.coding_rate_denom.ok_or_else(missing)?],
3687            prop::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT => desired
3688                .duty_cycle_limit
3689                .ok_or_else(missing)?
3690                .to_le_bytes()
3691                .to_vec(),
3692            prop::DEV_NAME => desired
3693                .device_name
3694                .clone()
3695                .ok_or_else(missing)?
3696                .into_bytes(),
3697            // Empty is a legitimate value for both: the device derives
3698            // its own role, and advertises no position.
3699            prop::IDENT_ROLE => desired
3700                .ident_role
3701                .map(|role| vec![role])
3702                .unwrap_or_default(),
3703            prop::IDENT_MOBILE => vec![desired.ident_mobile.ok_or_else(missing)? as u8],
3704            prop::IDENT_LOCATION => {
3705                let location = desired.ident_location.clone().unwrap_or_default();
3706                if location.len() > MAX_PRECISION as usize {
3707                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
3708                }
3709                location
3710            }
3711            prop::IDENT_ALTITUDE => match desired.ident_altitude_m {
3712                Some(meters) => {
3713                    let mut buf = [0u8; umsh_ulcp::sint::MAX_LEN];
3714                    let len = umsh_ulcp::sint::encode(meters, &mut buf)
3715                        .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
3716                    buf[..len].to_vec()
3717                }
3718                None => Vec::new(),
3719            },
3720            prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE => vec![desired.dev_discoverable.ok_or_else(missing)? as u8],
3721            prop::GNSS_ENABLED => vec![desired.gnss_enabled.ok_or_else(missing)? as u8],
3722            prop::GNSS_IDENT_UPDATE => vec![desired.gnss_ident_update.ok_or_else(missing)? as u8],
3723            prop::GNSS_IDENT_PRECISION => {
3724                let precision = desired.gnss_ident_precision.ok_or_else(missing)?;
3725                if !(1..=MAX_PRECISION).contains(&precision) {
3726                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
3727                }
3728                vec![precision]
3729            }
3730            prop::GNSS_TIME_TRUST => vec![desired.gnss_time_trust.ok_or_else(missing)? as u8],
3731            // Empty clears the clock back to unknown, which is what a
3732            // device reports before its first fix.
3733            prop::TIME => desired
3734                .time
3735                .ok_or_else(missing)?
3736                .epoch_seconds
3737                .map(|epoch| epoch.to_le_bytes().to_vec())
3738                .unwrap_or_default(),
3739            prop::TZ_OFFSET => desired
3740                .tz_offset_min
3741                .ok_or_else(missing)?
3742                .to_le_bytes()
3743                .to_vec(),
3744            prop::ADVERT_INTERVAL => desired
3745                .advert_interval_seconds
3746                .ok_or_else(missing)?
3747                .to_le_bytes()
3748                .to_vec(),
3749            prop::BEACON_INTERVAL => desired
3750                .beacon_interval_seconds
3751                .ok_or_else(missing)?
3752                .to_le_bytes()
3753                .to_vec(),
3754            prop::STARTUP_BEACON => vec![desired.startup_beacon.ok_or_else(missing)? as u8],
3755            prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED => vec![desired.repeater_enabled.ok_or_else(missing)? as u8],
3756            prop::MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS => encode_region_list(
3757                desired
3758                    .repeater_regions
3759                    .clone()
3760                    .ok_or_else(missing)?
3761                    .as_slice(),
3762            )?,
3763            // Empty is "never tag" and "no threshold" respectively.
3764            prop::MAC_REPEATER_DEFAULT_REGION => {
3765                let region = desired.repeater_default_region.clone().unwrap_or_default();
3766                if !region.is_empty() && region.len() != items::REGION_CODE_LEN {
3767                    return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
3768                }
3769                region
3770            }
3771            prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI => desired
3772                .repeater_min_rssi_dbm
3773                .map(|rssi| rssi.to_le_bytes().to_vec())
3774                .unwrap_or_default(),
3775            prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_SNR => desired
3776                .repeater_min_snr_db
3777                .map(|snr| vec![snr as u8])
3778                .unwrap_or_default(),
3779            // Everything else is either read-only or edited as a table,
3780            // one entry at a time.
3781            _ => return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame),
3782        };
3783        values.push((*key, value));
3784    }
3785
3786    // Bracket the radio when anything it is transmitting under moves.
3787    const RADIO: [u32; 6] = [
3788        prop::PHY_FREQ,
3789        prop::PHY_TX_POWER,
3790        prop::PHY_LORA_BW,
3791        prop::PHY_LORA_SF,
3792        prop::PHY_LORA_CR,
3793        prop::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT,
3794    ];
3795    if values.iter().any(|(key, _)| RADIO.contains(key)) {
3796        let ends_enabled = match values.iter().find(|(key, _)| *key == prop::PHY_ENABLED) {
3797            Some((_, value)) => value.first() == Some(&1),
3798            // Not edited, so it ends however it started — which the
3799            // caller states by filling this in from the last read.
3800            None => desired.phy_enabled.unwrap_or(true),
3801        };
3802        values.retain(|(key, _)| *key != prop::PHY_ENABLED);
3803        values.insert(0, (prop::PHY_ENABLED, vec![0]));
3804        values.push((prop::PHY_ENABLED, vec![ends_enabled as u8]));
3805    }
3806
3807    Ok(values
3808        .into_iter()
3809        .map(|(property_id, value)| MobileMeshPropertyWriteRecord { property_id, value })
3810        .collect())
3811}
3812
3813/// Properties only ever written as a set. Writing part of a modem profile
3814/// or part of a forwarding policy leaves the device running a configuration
3815/// nobody asked for, so one unreadable member withdraws the whole group.
3816/// These are the same groupings the reduction reports as a unit.
3817const WHOLE_WRITE_GROUPS: [&[u32]; 4] = [
3818    &[prop::PHY_LORA_BW, prop::PHY_LORA_SF, prop::PHY_LORA_CR],
3819    &[
3820        prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED,
3821        prop::MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS,
3822        prop::MAC_REPEATER_DEFAULT_REGION,
3823        prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI,
3824        prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_SNR,
3825    ],
3826    &[
3827        prop::GNSS_ENABLED,
3828        prop::GNSS_IDENT_UPDATE,
3829        prop::GNSS_IDENT_PRECISION,
3830        prop::GNSS_TIME_TRUST,
3831    ],
3832    &[
3833        prop::ADVERT_INTERVAL,
3834        prop::BEACON_INTERVAL,
3835        prop::STARTUP_BEACON,
3836    ],
3837];
3838
3839/// The capability-gated half of an inspection: properties the device is
3840/// expected to answer, each of which it may nevertheless decline.
3841struct ExpectedProperties<'a> {
3842    responses: &'a [UlcpPropertyFrameRecord],
3843    unreadable: Vec<u32>,
3844}
3845
3846impl ExpectedProperties<'_> {
3847    /// Decode `key` when the device advertises the capability that gates it.
3848    ///
3849    /// A missing or undecodable value is recorded and reported as `None`
3850    /// rather than failing the whole reduction: the setting is unknown, which
3851    /// is a fact about one property and not about the device as a whole.
3852    fn read<T>(
3853        &mut self,
3854        gated_on: bool,
3855        key: u32,
3856        decode: impl FnOnce(&[u8]) -> Result<T, MobileError>,
3857    ) -> Option<T> {
3858        if !gated_on {
3859            return None;
3860        }
3861        match property_value(self.responses, key).and_then(decode) {
3862            Ok(value) => Some(value),
3863            Err(_) => {
3864                self.unreadable.push(key);
3865                None
3866            }
3867        }
3868    }
3869}
3870
3871/// Decode a live reading the device may or may not have reported.
3872///
3873/// The counterpart to [`ExpectedProperties::read`] for values that are not
3874/// configuration: absence is ordinary rather than a withheld setting, so
3875/// nothing is recorded and the reduction reports what it has.
3876fn reported_value<T>(
3877    responses: &[UlcpPropertyFrameRecord],
3878    gated_on: bool,
3879    key: u32,
3880    decode: impl FnOnce(&[u8]) -> Result<T, MobileError>,
3881) -> Option<T> {
3882    if !gated_on {
3883        return None;
3884    }
3885    optional_value(responses, key, decode)
3886}
3887
3888/// Decode a property a reply need not contain at all.
3889///
3890/// What [`reported_value`] does once a capability says the property
3891/// should be there, for the reductions that make no such demand: a
3892/// category read answers a handful of properties and says nothing about
3893/// the rest.
3894fn optional_value<T>(
3895    responses: &[UlcpPropertyFrameRecord],
3896    key: u32,
3897    decode: impl FnOnce(&[u8]) -> Result<T, MobileError>,
3898) -> Option<T> {
3899    property_value(responses, key).and_then(decode).ok()
3900}
3901
3902fn property_value(responses: &[UlcpPropertyFrameRecord], key: u32) -> Result<&[u8], MobileError> {
3903    let mut matching = responses
3904        .iter()
3905        .filter(|response| response.property_id == key);
3906    let response = matching.next().ok_or(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
3907    if matching.next().is_some() || response.command != Cmd::PropIs as u8 {
3908        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
3909    }
3910    Ok(&response.value)
3911}
3912
3913pub(crate) fn decode_capabilities(value: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u32>, MobileError> {
3914    let mut capabilities = Vec::new();
3915    let mut rest = value;
3916    while !rest.is_empty() {
3917        let (capability, used) = pui::decode(rest).map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
3918        if capabilities.contains(&capability) {
3919            return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
3920        }
3921        capabilities.push(capability);
3922        rest = &rest[used..];
3923    }
3924    Ok(capabilities)
3925}
3926
3927fn validate_capability_dependencies(capabilities: &[u32]) -> Result<(), MobileError> {
3928    let has = |capability| capabilities.contains(&capability);
3929    if has(cap::HOST_RX_QUEUE) && !has(cap::HOST_FILTER)
3930        || has(cap::HOST_KEYS) && !has(cap::HOST_FILTER)
3931        || has(cap::HOST_AUTO_ACK) && (!has(cap::HOST_KEYS) || !has(cap::HOST_RX_QUEUE))
3932        // A device with no identity of its own has nothing to forward for
3933        // and nothing to advertise.
3934        || has(cap::REPEATER) && !has(cap::DEV_IDENTITY)
3935        || has(cap::IDENT) && !has(cap::DEV_IDENTITY)
3936        // An administrator is authorized against the device identity and
3937        // reaches the device domain, so there is nothing to manage without
3938        // one.
3939        || has(cap::ADMIN) && !has(cap::DEV_IDENTITY)
3940        // What a scheduled advertisement carries *is* the device identity.
3941        || has(cap::ADVERT) && !has(cap::DEV_IDENTITY)
3942        // A receiver that cannot set a clock is still a receiver, but the
3943        // device also dates its fixes, so CAP_GNSS implies CAP_TIME.
3944        || has(cap::GNSS) && !has(cap::TIME)
3945    {
3946        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
3947    }
3948    Ok(())
3949}
3950
3951fn decode_exact_pui(value: &[u8]) -> Result<u32, MobileError> {
3952    let (decoded, used) = pui::decode(value).map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
3953    (used == value.len())
3954        .then_some(decoded)
3955        .ok_or(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
3956}
3957
3958fn decode_bool(value: &[u8]) -> Result<bool, MobileError> {
3959    match value {
3960        [0] => Ok(false),
3961        [1] => Ok(true),
3962        _ => Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame),
3963    }
3964}
3965
3966fn decode_saved(value: &[u8]) -> Result<SavedSnapshotRecord, MobileError> {
3967    match value {
3968        [saved::NONE] => Ok(SavedSnapshotRecord::None),
3969        [saved::CURRENT] => Ok(SavedSnapshotRecord::Current),
3970        [saved::FALLBACK] => Ok(SavedSnapshotRecord::Fallback),
3971        [saved::UNREADABLE] => Ok(SavedSnapshotRecord::Unreadable),
3972        _ => Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame),
3973    }
3974}
3975
3976fn decode_u16(value: &[u8]) -> Result<u16, MobileError> {
3977    value
3978        .try_into()
3979        .map(u16::from_le_bytes)
3980        .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
3981}
3982
3983fn decode_u8(value: &[u8]) -> Result<u8, MobileError> {
3984    value
3985        .first()
3986        .copied()
3987        .filter(|_| value.len() == 1)
3988        .ok_or(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
3989}
3990
3991fn decode_i8(value: &[u8]) -> Result<i8, MobileError> {
3992    decode_u8(value).map(|value| value as i8)
3993}
3994
3995/// A location precision, which is only ever 1–7 bytes. A device
3996/// reporting anything else is reporting a setting this phone cannot
3997/// present, so it is recorded as unreadable rather than shown.
3998fn decode_precision(value: &[u8]) -> Result<u8, MobileError> {
3999    decode_u8(value)
4000        .ok()
4001        .filter(|bytes| (1..=MAX_PRECISION).contains(bytes))
4002        .ok_or(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
4003}
4004
4005fn decode_i16(value: &[u8]) -> Result<i16, MobileError> {
4006    value
4007        .try_into()
4008        .map(i16::from_le_bytes)
4009        .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
4010}
4011
4012/// Decode a property whose empty value means "unset" rather than zero.
4013fn decode_optional<T>(
4014    value: &[u8],
4015    decode: impl Fn(&[u8]) -> Result<T, MobileError>,
4016) -> Result<Option<T>, MobileError> {
4017    if value.is_empty() {
4018        return Ok(None);
4019    }
4020    decode(value).map(Some)
4021}
4022
4023/// Split a `PROP_MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS` value into its region strings.
4024///
4025/// Deliberately imposes no upper bound: how many regions a device holds
4026/// is its own business, and a device reporting more than this phone would
4027/// ever write is not a malformed frame.
4028fn decode_region_list(value: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<String>, MobileError> {
4029    let mut regions = Vec::new();
4030    for item in items::prefixed_items(value) {
4031        let item = item.map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
4032        let text = core::str::from_utf8(item).map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
4033        regions.push(text.to_owned());
4034    }
4035    Ok(regions)
4036}
4037
4038/// Pack region strings back into a `PROP_MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS` value.
4039///
4040/// Over-long names are refused here rather than on the air: the device
4041/// rejects them outright, and one rejected write abandons everything
4042/// after it.
4043fn encode_region_list(regions: &[String]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MobileError> {
4044    let mut value = Vec::new();
4045    for region in regions {
4046        if !(1..=items::REGION_STRING_MAX_LEN).contains(&region.len()) {
4047            return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4048        }
4049        let mut item = vec![0u8; region.len() + 4];
4050        let len = items::encode_prefixed_item(region.as_bytes(), &mut item)
4051            .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
4052        value.extend_from_slice(&item[..len]);
4053    }
4054    Ok(value)
4055}
4056
4057fn decode_optional_region(value: &[u8]) -> Result<Option<Vec<u8>>, MobileError> {
4058    match value.len() {
4059        0 => Ok(None),
4060        items::REGION_CODE_LEN => Ok(Some(value.to_vec())),
4061        _ => Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame),
4062    }
4063}
4064
4065/// Drop the writes the device has already refused to answer for.
4066///
4067/// A capability-gated property that would not read is one the device does
4068/// not implement, so writing it fails — and one rejected write abandons
4069/// the whole configuration pass. The caller still states a complete
4070/// configuration; what cannot land is left out here, where the device's
4071/// own answers are known, rather than in the form.
4072fn writable(values: Vec<(u32, Vec<u8>)>, unreadable: &[u32]) -> Vec<(u32, Vec<u8>)> {
4073    if unreadable.is_empty() {
4074        return values;
4075    }
4076    let dropped = |property: u32| {
4077        unreadable.contains(&property)
4078            || WHOLE_WRITE_GROUPS.iter().any(|group| {
4079                group.contains(&property) && group.iter().any(|part| unreadable.contains(part))
4080            })
4081    };
4082    values
4083        .into_iter()
4084        .filter(|(property, _)| !dropped(*property))
4085        .collect()
4086}
4087
4088/// Reduce a whole device configuration to the property writes that state
4089/// it, in the order they must be sent, against a device known only by
4090/// what a completed read reported.
4091///
4092/// This is [`MobileUlcpSession::configure_device`] with the session taken
4093/// out of it: an administrator on the mesh writes the same properties, in
4094/// the same order, and drops the same unreadable ones — it just has no
4095/// attached device to ask, only the record it read.
4096pub(crate) fn device_config_writes(
4097    configuration: UlcpDeviceConfigRecord,
4098    reported: &UlcpSyncRecord,
4099) -> Result<Vec<(u32, Vec<u8>)>, MobileError> {
4100    let capabilities = DeviceCapabilities::reported(reported);
4101    validate_radio_settings(&configuration.radio, capabilities)?;
4102    let device_values = validate_device_settings(&configuration, capabilities)?;
4103    Ok(writable(
4104        configuration_values(configuration.radio, device_values),
4105        &reported.unreadable_properties,
4106    ))
4107}
4108
4109/// What a device can be told, as either half of the app learns it.
4110///
4111/// A bench session reads the capability list off the device it is holding
4112/// open; a mesh administrator reads it out of the record a whole-device
4113/// read produced. The configuration reduces to the same writes either
4114/// way, so both build one of these and nothing below has to ask which
4115/// side it came from.
4116#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
4117struct DeviceCapabilities {
4118    device_name: bool,
4119    lora: bool,
4120    duty_cycle_limit: bool,
4121    ident: bool,
4122    dev_identity: bool,
4123    repeater: bool,
4124    time: bool,
4125    gnss: bool,
4126    advert: bool,
4127}
4128
4129impl DeviceCapabilities {
4130    /// What the attached device answered `PROP_CAPS` with.
4131    fn read(state: &UlcpSessionState) -> Result<Self, MobileError> {
4132        Ok(Self {
4133            device_name: state.has_capability(cap::DEV_NAME)?,
4134            lora: state.has_capability(cap::PHY_LORA)?,
4135            duty_cycle_limit: state.has_capability(cap::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT)?,
4136            ident: state.has_capability(cap::IDENT)?,
4137            dev_identity: state.has_capability(cap::DEV_IDENTITY)?,
4138            repeater: state.has_capability(cap::REPEATER)?,
4139            time: state.has_capability(cap::TIME)?,
4140            gnss: state.has_capability(cap::GNSS)?,
4141            advert: state.has_capability(cap::ADVERT)?,
4142        })
4143    }
4144
4145    /// The same list as a completed read reports it.
4146    fn reported(sync: &UlcpSyncRecord) -> Self {
4147        Self {
4148            device_name: sync.supports_device_name,
4149            lora: sync.supports_lora,
4150            duty_cycle_limit: sync.supports_duty_cycle_limit,
4151            ident: sync.supports_ident,
4152            dev_identity: sync.supports_device_identity,
4153            repeater: sync.supports_repeater,
4154            time: sync.supports_time,
4155            gnss: sync.supports_gnss,
4156            advert: sync.supports_advert,
4157        }
4158    }
4159}
4160
4161/// Order one configuration pass: everything that changes live PHY
4162/// behavior happens with the radio down, and the radio comes back up only
4163/// once the complete new profile is in place.
4164///
4165/// `device_values` are the device-domain writes, which ride between the
4166/// two PHY_ENABLED writes for the same reason the PHY parameters do — a
4167/// repeater must not start forwarding under half of its new policy.
4168fn configuration_values(
4169    settings: UlcpRadioSettingsRecord,
4170    device_values: Vec<(u32, Vec<u8>)>,
4171) -> Vec<(u32, Vec<u8>)> {
4172    let mut values = Vec::new();
4173    if !settings.phy_enabled {
4174        values.push((prop::PHY_ENABLED, vec![0]));
4175    }
4176    if let Some(name) = settings.device_name {
4177        values.push((prop::DEV_NAME, name.into_bytes()));
4178    }
4179    values.extend([
4180        (
4181            prop::PHY_FREQ,
4182            settings.frequency_khz.to_le_bytes().to_vec(),
4183        ),
4184        (prop::PHY_TX_POWER, vec![settings.transmit_power_dbm as u8]),
4185    ]);
4186    if let (Some(bandwidth), Some(sf), Some(cr)) = (
4187        settings.bandwidth_hz,
4188        settings.spreading_factor,
4189        settings.coding_rate_denom,
4190    ) {
4191        values.extend([
4192            (prop::PHY_LORA_BW, bandwidth.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
4193            (prop::PHY_LORA_SF, vec![sf]),
4194            (prop::PHY_LORA_CR, vec![cr]),
4195        ]);
4196    }
4197    if let Some(limit) = settings.duty_cycle_limit {
4198        values.push((prop::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT, limit.to_le_bytes().to_vec()));
4199    }
4200    values.extend(device_values);
4201    if settings.phy_enabled {
4202        values.push((prop::PHY_ENABLED, vec![1]));
4203    }
4204    values
4205}
4206
4207/// Check the device-domain half of a commissioning record against what
4208/// the device says it can do, and reduce it to property writes.
4209///
4210/// Capability-gated fields must be present exactly when the capability
4211/// is: the record states a whole desired configuration, so a field the
4212/// device cannot honor is a caller mistake rather than something to
4213/// silently drop.
4214fn validate_device_settings(
4215    configuration: &UlcpDeviceConfigRecord,
4216    capabilities: DeviceCapabilities,
4217) -> Result<Vec<(u32, Vec<u8>)>, MobileError> {
4218    let mut values = Vec::new();
4219
4220    let supports_ident = capabilities.ident;
4221    if configuration.ident_mobile.is_some() != supports_ident
4222        || (configuration.ident_role.is_some() && !supports_ident)
4223    {
4224        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4225    }
4226    if supports_ident {
4227        // An empty PROP_IDENT_ROLE hands the choice back to the device.
4228        values.push((
4229            prop::IDENT_ROLE,
4230            configuration
4231                .ident_role
4232                .map(|role| vec![role])
4233                .unwrap_or_default(),
4234        ));
4235        values.push((
4236            prop::IDENT_MOBILE,
4237            vec![configuration.ident_mobile.unwrap_or(false) as u8],
4238        ));
4239    }
4240
4241    let supports_dev_identity = capabilities.dev_identity;
4242    if configuration.dev_discoverable.is_some() != supports_dev_identity {
4243        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4244    }
4245    if let Some(discoverable) = configuration.dev_discoverable {
4246        values.push((prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE, vec![discoverable as u8]));
4247    }
4248
4249    let supports_repeater = capabilities.repeater;
4250    if configuration.repeater.is_some() != supports_repeater {
4251        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4252    }
4253    if let Some(repeater) = &configuration.repeater {
4254        let regions = encode_region_list(&repeater.regions)?;
4255        if let Some(default_region) = &repeater.default_region {
4256            if default_region.len() != items::REGION_CODE_LEN {
4257                return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4258            }
4259        }
4260        // Enabling last means the forwarding policy is already whole by
4261        // the time the device starts acting on it. The device does not
4262        // cross-check the default region against the forwarding list —
4263        // that is a SHOULD the presenting UI is better placed to warn on.
4264        values.extend([
4265            (prop::MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS, regions),
4266            (
4267                prop::MAC_REPEATER_DEFAULT_REGION,
4268                repeater.default_region.clone().unwrap_or_default(),
4269            ),
4270            (
4271                prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI,
4272                repeater
4273                    .min_rssi_dbm
4274                    .map(|rssi| rssi.to_le_bytes().to_vec())
4275                    .unwrap_or_default(),
4276            ),
4277            (
4278                prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_SNR,
4279                repeater
4280                    .min_snr_db
4281                    .map(|snr| vec![snr as u8])
4282                    .unwrap_or_default(),
4283            ),
4284            (prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED, vec![repeater.enabled as u8]),
4285        ]);
4286    }
4287
4288    values.extend(positioning_values(
4289        configuration.gnss,
4290        configuration.tz_offset_min,
4291        capabilities,
4292    )?);
4293    values.extend(advert_values(configuration.advert, capabilities)?);
4294    Ok(values)
4295}
4296
4297/// Reduce the advertisement policy to property writes.
4298///
4299/// Split out for the same reason [`positioning_values`] is: a tethered
4300/// phone changes these on its companion radio without commissioning it,
4301/// and both paths have to produce the same writes.
4302fn advert_values(
4303    advert: Option<UlcpAdvertSettingsRecord>,
4304    capabilities: DeviceCapabilities,
4305) -> Result<Vec<(u32, Vec<u8>)>, MobileError> {
4306    let announces = capabilities.advert;
4307    if advert.is_some() != announces {
4308        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4309    }
4310    let Some(advert) = advert else {
4311        return Ok(Vec::new());
4312    };
4313    // The device refuses these too. Catching them here means an
4314    // out-of-range interval fails before any of the group has been
4315    // written, rather than leaving the schedule half-changed.
4316    for interval in [
4317        advert.advert_interval_seconds,
4318        advert.beacon_interval_seconds,
4319    ] {
4320        if interval != 0
4321            && !(MIN_AUTO_ANNOUNCE_INTERVAL_S..=MAX_AUTO_ANNOUNCE_INTERVAL_S).contains(&interval)
4322        {
4323            return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4324        }
4325    }
4326    Ok(vec![
4327        (
4328            prop::ADVERT_INTERVAL,
4329            advert.advert_interval_seconds.to_le_bytes().to_vec(),
4330        ),
4331        (
4332            prop::BEACON_INTERVAL,
4333            advert.beacon_interval_seconds.to_le_bytes().to_vec(),
4334        ),
4335        (prop::STARTUP_BEACON, vec![advert.startup_beacon as u8]),
4336    ])
4337}
4338
4339/// Reduce the zone and the positioning policy to property writes.
4340///
4341/// Split out because these are the one part of a device's own domain a
4342/// phone changes on its *companion* radio without commissioning it —
4343/// [`MobileUlcpSession::configure_positioning`] writes exactly this list
4344/// and nothing else, where [`validate_device_settings`] folds it into a
4345/// whole-domain write. Same values either way, so the two paths cannot
4346/// drift apart.
4347///
4348/// Each field must be present exactly when its capability is: these
4349/// state a whole desired setting rather than a patch.
4350fn positioning_values(
4351    gnss: Option<UlcpGnssSettingsRecord>,
4352    tz_offset_min: Option<i16>,
4353    capabilities: DeviceCapabilities,
4354) -> Result<Vec<(u32, Vec<u8>)>, MobileError> {
4355    let mut values = Vec::new();
4356
4357    let keeps_time = capabilities.time;
4358    if tz_offset_min.is_some() != keeps_time {
4359        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4360    }
4361    if let Some(minutes) = tz_offset_min {
4362        // The extremes of the zone database, not of the encoding: a
4363        // fourteen-hour offset is Kiritimati, and anything past it is a
4364        // caller mistake rather than a place.
4365        if !(-12 * 60..=14 * 60).contains(&minutes) {
4366            return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4367        }
4368        values.push((prop::TZ_OFFSET, minutes.to_le_bytes().to_vec()));
4369    }
4370
4371    let positioning = capabilities.gnss;
4372    if gnss.is_some() != positioning {
4373        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4374    }
4375    if let Some(gnss) = gnss {
4376        if !(1..=MAX_PRECISION).contains(&gnss.ident_precision) {
4377            return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4378        }
4379        // Enabled last, so a receiver that starts looking does it under
4380        // the disclosure and trust policy just written rather than the
4381        // one it happened to be holding.
4382        values.extend([
4383            (prop::GNSS_IDENT_UPDATE, vec![gnss.ident_update as u8]),
4384            (prop::GNSS_IDENT_PRECISION, vec![gnss.ident_precision]),
4385            (prop::GNSS_TIME_TRUST, vec![gnss.time_trust as u8]),
4386            (prop::GNSS_ENABLED, vec![gnss.enabled as u8]),
4387        ]);
4388    }
4389    Ok(values)
4390}
4391
4392fn validate_radio_settings(
4393    settings: &UlcpRadioSettingsRecord,
4394    capabilities: DeviceCapabilities,
4395) -> Result<(), MobileError> {
4396    if settings.frequency_khz == 0 {
4397        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4398    }
4399    if let Some(name) = &settings.device_name {
4400        if !capabilities.device_name
4401            || name.is_empty()
4402            || name.len() > 64
4403            || name.as_bytes().contains(&0)
4404        {
4405            return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4406        }
4407    }
4408    let lora = (
4409        settings.bandwidth_hz,
4410        settings.spreading_factor,
4411        settings.coding_rate_denom,
4412    );
4413    match lora {
4414        (None, None, None) if !capabilities.lora => {}
4415        (Some(bandwidth), Some(sf), Some(cr))
4416            if capabilities.lora
4417                && bandwidth > 0
4418                && (5..=12).contains(&sf)
4419                && (5..=8).contains(&cr) => {}
4420        _ => return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame),
4421    }
4422    if settings.duty_cycle_limit.is_some() != capabilities.duty_cycle_limit {
4423        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4424    }
4425    Ok(())
4426}
4427
4428/// A device name, which is UTF-8 and may be empty — a device that has not
4429/// been named, rather than one named nothing.
4430fn decode_device_name(value: &[u8]) -> Result<Option<String>, MobileError> {
4431    let name = core::str::from_utf8(value).map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
4432    Ok((!name.is_empty()).then(|| name.to_owned()))
4433}
4434
4435fn decode_u32(value: &[u8]) -> Result<u32, MobileError> {
4436    value
4437        .try_into()
4438        .map(u32::from_le_bytes)
4439        .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
4440}
4441
4442/// Split a concatenation of fixed-width items into the items themselves.
4443/// The lossless counterpart of [`decode_fixed_count`], for properties whose
4444/// GET form reads back full values rather than digests.
4445fn decode_fixed_list<const N: usize>(value: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>, MobileError> {
4446    items::fixed_items::<N>(value)
4447        .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?
4448        .map(|item| Ok(item.to_vec()))
4449        .collect()
4450}
4451
4452fn decode_fixed_count<const N: usize>(value: &[u8]) -> Result<u32, MobileError> {
4453    let count = items::fixed_items::<N>(value)
4454        .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?
4455        .count();
4456    count.try_into().map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
4457}
4458
4459fn decode_filter_count(value: &[u8]) -> Result<u32, MobileError> {
4460    let mut count = 0u32;
4461    for item in items::prefixed_items(value) {
4462        let item = item.map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
4463        Filter::decode(item).map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
4464        count = count.checked_add(1).ok_or(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
4465    }
4466    Ok(count)
4467}
4468
4469/// Split a ULCP frame into ATT values using the negotiated maximum write
4470/// length. The returned values include the one-octet SAR header.
4471#[uniffi::export]
4472pub fn ulcp_gatt_segments(
4473    frame: Vec<u8>,
4474    maximum_value_length: u16,
4475) -> Result<Vec<GattSegmentRecord>, MobileError> {
4476    let segment_payload = usize::from(maximum_value_length)
4477        .checked_sub(1)
4478        .filter(|length| *length > 0)
4479        .ok_or(MobileError::InvalidGattSegment)?;
4480    if frame.len() > MAX_FRAME {
4481        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4482    }
4483
4484    Ok(gatt::segments(&frame, segment_payload)
4485        .map(|segment| {
4486            let mut value = vec![0; segment.payload().len() + 1];
4487            let length = segment
4488                .write_to(&mut value)
4489                .expect("sized from the segment payload");
4490            value.truncate(length);
4491            GattSegmentRecord { value }
4492        })
4493        .collect())
4494}
4495
4496/// Frame a ULCP frame for an HDLC-Lite byte stream — a serial port, or
4497/// a socket standing in for one — including both delimiting flags.
4498///
4499/// The byte-stream counterpart of [`ulcp_gatt_segments`]: a stream has
4500/// no segmentation, so one frame encodes to one write.
4501#[uniffi::export]
4502pub fn ulcp_hdlc_encode(frame: Vec<u8>) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MobileError> {
4503    if frame.len() > MAX_FRAME {
4504        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4505    }
4506    let mut wire = vec![0; hdlc::max_encoded_len(frame.len())];
4507    let length =
4508        hdlc::encode_frame(&frame, &mut wire).map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
4509    wire.truncate(length);
4510    Ok(wire)
4511}
4512
4513/// Encode a `CMD_PROP_GET` request with the shared ULCP codec.
4514#[uniffi::export]
4515pub fn ulcp_prop_get(transaction_id: u8, property_id: u32) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MobileError> {
4516    let mut output = [0; 8];
4517    let length = frame::prop_get(&mut output, transaction_id, property_id)
4518        .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
4519    Ok(output[..length].to_vec())
4520}
4521
4522/// Encode a `CMD_PROP_SET` request with the shared ULCP codec.
4523#[uniffi::export]
4524pub fn ulcp_prop_set(
4525    transaction_id: u8,
4526    property_id: u32,
4527    value: Vec<u8>,
4528) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MobileError> {
4529    if value.len() > MAX_FRAME {
4530        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4531    }
4532    let mut output = vec![0; MAX_FRAME];
4533    let length = frame::prop_set(&mut output, transaction_id, property_id, &value)
4534        .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
4535    output.truncate(length);
4536    Ok(output)
4537}
4538
4539/// Encode a `CMD_PROP_INSERT` request. Deliberately not exported: typed
4540/// session operations own multi-value mutations.
4541fn ulcp_prop_insert(
4542    transaction_id: u8,
4543    property_id: u32,
4544    item: &[u8],
4545) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MobileError> {
4546    let mut output = vec![0; MAX_FRAME];
4547    let length = frame::prop_insert(&mut output, transaction_id, property_id, item)
4548        .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
4549    output.truncate(length);
4550    Ok(output)
4551}
4552
4553/// Encode a `CMD_PROP_REMOVE` request. Deliberately not exported, like
4554/// [`ulcp_prop_insert`].
4555fn ulcp_prop_remove(
4556    transaction_id: u8,
4557    property_id: u32,
4558    selector: &[u8],
4559) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MobileError> {
4560    let mut output = vec![0; MAX_FRAME];
4561    let length = frame::prop_remove(&mut output, transaction_id, property_id, selector)
4562        .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
4563    output.truncate(length);
4564    Ok(output)
4565}

4593/// One vetted PHY profile, as a preset picker consumes it.
4594///
4595/// A crossing of `umsh_ulcp::profiles::PhyProfile`. The bindings carry
4596/// no constants, so the table travels as a function.
4597#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, uniffi::Record)]
4598pub struct RadioPresetRecord {
4599    pub id: String,
4600    pub name: String,
4601    pub frequency_khz: u32,
4602    pub bandwidth_hz: u32,
4603    pub spreading_factor: u8,
4604    pub coding_rate_denom: u8,
4605    /// Absent where the profile has no vetted power, in which case
4606    /// adopting it leaves a device's configured power alone.
4607    pub transmit_power_dbm: Option<i8>,
4608    pub duty_cycle_limit: u16,
4609    pub sync_word: u16,
4610    pub tx_preamble_symbols: u16,
4611}
4612
4613/// Every vetted radio profile, in the order to offer them: the profile
4614/// a device ships on first.
4615#[uniffi::export]
4616pub fn ulcp_radio_presets() -> Vec<RadioPresetRecord> {
4617    umsh_ulcp::profiles::VETTED
4618        .iter()
4619        .map(|profile| RadioPresetRecord {
4620            id: profile.id.to_string(),
4621            name: profile.name.to_string(),
4622            frequency_khz: profile.freq_khz,
4623            bandwidth_hz: profile.bw_hz,
4624            spreading_factor: profile.sf,
4625            coding_rate_denom: profile.cr_denom,
4626            transmit_power_dbm: profile.tx_power_dbm,
4627            duty_cycle_limit: profile.duty_limit,
4628            sync_word: profile.sync_word,
4629            tx_preamble_symbols: profile.tx_preamble_symbols,
4630        })
4631        .collect()
4632}

4641fn dev_channel_id(channel_key: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MobileError> {

4643        .try_into()
4644        .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidChannelKeyLength)?;

4655}

4674
4675/// What a status code is called.
4676///
4677/// A device answering across the mesh reports a bare code, where a device
4678/// on the local link reports one already named in
4679/// [`UlcpOperationErrorRecord`]. Both are the same statuses, so both are
4680/// named the same way here rather than by a table on the other side of
4681/// the bindings that would have to be kept in step with this one.
4682#[uniffi::export]
4683pub fn ulcp_status_name(status: u32) -> String {
4684    format!("{:?}", umsh_ulcp::Status(status))
4685}
4686
4687/// Decode an exact packed status value from `PROP_LAST_STATUS`.
4688#[uniffi::export]
4689pub fn inspect_ulcp_status(value: Vec<u8>) -> Result<u32, MobileError> {
4690    decode_exact_pui(&value)
4691}
4692
4693fn ulcp_operation_error(
4694    operation: String,
4695    value: &[u8],
4696) -> Result<UlcpOperationErrorRecord, MobileError> {
4697    let status_code = inspect_ulcp_status(value.to_vec())?;
4698    let status = umsh_ulcp::Status(status_code);
4699    if status == umsh_ulcp::Status::OK {
4700        // A property operation that promised an echoed value cannot silently
4701        // substitute status-only success. That is a real session violation,
4702        // not a reported operation error.
4703        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4704    }
4705    Ok(UlcpOperationErrorRecord {
4706        operation,
4707        status_code,
4708        status_name: format!("{status:?}"),
4709    })
4710}
4711
4712/// Parse and validate a property notification or response.
4713#[uniffi::export]
4714pub fn inspect_ulcp_property_frame(bytes: Vec<u8>) -> Result<UlcpPropertyFrameRecord, MobileError> {
4715    let parsed = PropertyNotification::parse(&bytes).map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
4716    Ok(UlcpPropertyFrameRecord {
4717        transaction_id: parsed.tid,
4718        command: parsed.kind.command() as u8,
4719        property_id: parsed.key,
4720        value: parsed.value.to_vec(),
4721    })
4722}
4723
4724/// Validate and reduce a `PROP_BATTERY` value to fields used by mobile UI.
4725#[uniffi::export]
4726pub fn inspect_ulcp_battery(value: Vec<u8>) -> Result<UlcpBatteryRecord, MobileError> {
4727    let battery = BatteryStatus::decode(&value).map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
4728    Ok(UlcpBatteryRecord {
4729        percentage: battery.level_percent,
4730        voltage_mv: battery.voltage_mv,
4731        charge_state: battery.charge_state.map(UlcpChargeState::from_wire),
4732    })
4733}
4734
4735/// Encode a `PROP_ALERT` value.
4736///
4737/// Shared by the local link and by a mesh administrator so an alert is one
4738/// encoding rather than two that have to agree.
4739pub(crate) fn encode_alert_state(state: UlcpAlertState) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MobileError> {
4740    let mut value = [0u8; pui::MAX_LEN];
4741    let len = pui::encode(state.to_wire().code(), &mut value)
4742        .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
4743    Ok(value[..len].to_vec())
4744}
4745
4746/// Validate and reduce a `PROP_ALERT` value.
4747#[uniffi::export]
4748pub fn inspect_ulcp_alert(value: Vec<u8>) -> Result<UlcpAlertState, MobileError> {
4749    let (code, consumed) = pui::decode(&value).map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)?;
4750    if consumed != value.len() {
4751        return Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame);
4752    }
4753    AlertState::from_code(code)
4754        .map(UlcpAlertState::from_wire)
4755        .ok_or(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
4756}
4757
4758/// Read a region code from what someone typed, yielding the two wire
4759/// octets used everywhere else in the ULCP and mesh surfaces.
4760///
4761/// One to three ASCII letters or digits are a short code — an airport,
4762/// a country, a state — `0xXXXX` is a literal code, and anything else is a
4763/// region *name* hashed into a part of the code space disjoint from the
4764/// all-letter short codes. So "SJC" and "San Jose" are deliberately
4765/// different regions, and no name can ever collide with a letter code.
4766#[uniffi::export]
4767pub fn region_code_from_string(text: String) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MobileError> {
4768    text.parse::<RegionCode>()
4769        .map(|code| code.to_bytes().to_vec())
4770        .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidRegionCode)
4771}
4772
4773/// Render a region code for display. Codes derived from an all-letter
4774/// short code come back as those letters; everything else as `0xXXXX`,
4775/// which [`region_code_from_string`] reads back.
4776#[uniffi::export]
4777pub fn region_code_description(code: Vec<u8>) -> Result<String, MobileError> {
4778    let bytes: [u8; items::REGION_CODE_LEN] = code
4779        .try_into()
4780        .map_err(|_| MobileError::InvalidRegionCode)?;
4781    Ok(RegionCode::from_bytes(bytes).to_string())
4782}
4783
4784/// Stateful, bounded receiver for Frame Out notifications.
4785#[derive(uniffi::Object)]
4786pub struct MobileGattReassembler {
4787    inner: Mutex<Reassembler<MAX_FRAME>>,
4788}
4789
4790#[uniffi::export]
4791impl MobileGattReassembler {
4792    #[uniffi::constructor]
4793    pub fn new() -> Arc<Self> {
4794        Arc::new(Self {
4795            inner: Mutex::new(Reassembler::new()),
4796        })
4797    }
4798
4799    /// Consume one ATT value, returning a complete ULCP frame when the
4800    /// segment ends one. Invalid input resets the shared reassembly state.
4801    pub fn push(&self, segment: Vec<u8>) -> Result<Option<Vec<u8>>, MobileError> {
4802        let mut reassembler = self.inner.lock().expect("GATT reassembler mutex poisoned");
4803        match reassembler.push(&segment) {
4804            None => Ok(None),
4805            Some(Ok(frame)) => Ok(Some(frame.to_vec())),
4806            Some(Err(_)) => Err(MobileError::InvalidGattSegment),
4807        }
4808    }
4809
4810    pub fn reset(&self) {
4811        self.inner
4812            .lock()
4813            .expect("GATT reassembler mutex poisoned")
4814            .reset();
4815    }
4816}
4817
4818/// Stateful, bounded receiver for an HDLC-Lite byte stream.
4819///
4820/// Sized to admit exactly the frames [`MobileGattReassembler`] does:
4821/// the decoder's bound counts the two FCS octets, which a ULCP frame's
4822/// own length does not.
4823#[derive(uniffi::Object)]
4824pub struct MobileHdlcDecoder {
4825    inner: Mutex<hdlc::Decoder<{ MAX_FRAME + 2 }>>,
4826}
4827
4828#[uniffi::export]
4829impl MobileHdlcDecoder {
4830    #[uniffi::constructor]
4831    pub fn new() -> Arc<Self> {
4832        Arc::new(Self {
4833            inner: Mutex::new(hdlc::Decoder::new()),
4834        })
4835    }
4836
4837    /// Consume received bytes, returning every frame they completed.
4838    ///
4839    /// A stream delivers arbitrary chunks rather than whole frames, so
4840    /// one call can complete none or several. Corrupt and oversized
4841    /// frames are discarded rather than reported: the decoder
4842    /// resynchronizes on the next flag, and a byte stream can carry
4843    /// line noise that belongs to nobody — a bridge opening a serial
4844    /// port mid-transmission, most commonly. This is the one place the
4845    /// two transports differ, GATT being reliable enough that a bad
4846    /// segment is a protocol violation worth surfacing.
4847    pub fn push(&self, bytes: Vec<u8>) -> Vec<Vec<u8>> {
4848        let mut decoder = self.inner.lock().expect("HDLC decoder mutex poisoned");
4849        let mut frames = Vec::new();
4850        for byte in bytes {
4851            if let Some(Ok(frame)) = decoder.push(byte) {
4852                frames.push(frame.to_vec());
4853            }
4854        }
4855        frames
4856    }
4857
4858    /// Discard any partially received frame. Used when the link comes
4859    /// up, so a half-frame from a previous connection cannot merge into
4860    /// the first frame of this one.
4861    pub fn reset(&self) {
4862        self.inner
4863            .lock()
4864            .expect("HDLC decoder mutex poisoned")
4865            .reset();
4866    }
4867}
4868
4869#[cfg(test)]
4870mod tests {
4871    use super::*;
4872    use umsh_ulcp::PropPayload;
4873
4874    fn response(property_id: u32, value: &[u8]) -> UlcpPropertyFrameRecord {
4875        UlcpPropertyFrameRecord {
4876            transaction_id: 1,
4877            command: Cmd::PropIs as u8,
4878            property_id,
4879            value: value.to_vec(),
4880        }
4881    }
4882
4883    fn encoded_capabilities(values: &[u32]) -> Vec<u8> {
4884        let mut encoded = Vec::new();
4885        for value in values {
4886            let mut bytes = [0; pui::MAX_LEN];
4887            let len = pui::encode(*value, &mut bytes).unwrap();
4888            encoded.extend_from_slice(&bytes[..len]);
4889        }
4890        encoded
4891    }
4892
4893    fn property_request(bytes: &[u8]) -> (u8, u32) {
4894        let parsed = Frame::parse(bytes).unwrap();
4895        assert_eq!(parsed.command(), Some(Cmd::PropGet));
4896        let (property, used) = pui::decode(parsed.payload).unwrap();
4897        assert_eq!(used, parsed.payload.len());
4898        (parsed.header.tid(), property)
4899    }
4900
4901    fn property_response(tid: u8, property: u32, value: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8> {
4902        let mut bytes = vec![0; MAX_FRAME];
4903        let length = frame::prop_is(&mut bytes, tid, property, value).unwrap();
4904        bytes.truncate(length);
4905        bytes
4906    }
4907
4908    fn answer_requests(
4909        session: &MobileUlcpSession,
4910        requests: Vec<Vec<u8>>,
4911        value: impl Fn(u32) -> (u32, Vec<u8>),
4912    ) -> UlcpSessionUpdateRecord {
4913        let mut last = None;
4914        for request in requests {
4915            let (tid, requested) = property_request(&request);
4916            let (returned, bytes) = value(requested);
4917            last = Some(
4918                session
4919                    .consume(property_response(tid, returned, &bytes))
4920                    .unwrap(),
4921            );
4922        }
4923        last.unwrap()
4924    }
4925
4926    /// Capabilities of a device that is a full mesh citizen in its own
4927    /// right: it has an identity, advertises one, can forward, and answers
4928    /// to the administrators it lists.
4929    fn commissionable_capabilities() -> Vec<u32> {
4930        vec![
4931            cap::HOST_FILTER,
4932            cap::SAVE,
4933            cap::DEV_NAME,
4934            cap::DEV_IDENTITY,
4935            cap::REPEATER,
4936            cap::IDENT,
4937            cap::ADMIN,
4938        ]
4939    }
4940
4941    /// Answer whatever the session asks for, for a device with the
4942    /// capabilities above and a factory-default device domain.
4943    fn commissionable_value(property: u32) -> (u32, Vec<u8>) {
4944        let value = match property {
4945            prop::LAST_STATUS => vec![0],
4946            prop::PROTOCOL_VERSION => vec![6, 0],
4947            prop::CAPS => encoded_capabilities(&commissionable_capabilities()),
4948            prop::DEV_NAME => b"Ridge repeater".to_vec(),
4949            prop::DEV_KEY => vec![0x5A; 32],
4950            prop::BATTERY => Vec::new(),
4951            prop::INTERFACE_TYPE => vec![INTERFACE_TYPE as u8],
4952            prop::PHY_ENABLED => vec![1],
4953            prop::PHY_FREQ => 915_000u32.to_le_bytes().to_vec(),
4954            prop::PHY_TX_POWER => vec![14],
4955            prop::SAVED => vec![saved::CURRENT],
4956            prop::HOST_RX_FILTERS => Vec::new(),
4957            prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED => vec![0],
4958            prop::MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS
4959            | prop::MAC_REPEATER_DEFAULT_REGION
4960            | prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI
4961            | prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_SNR
4962            | prop::IDENT_ROLE
4963            // A factory-default device states no position and no height.
4964            | prop::IDENT_LOCATION
4965            | prop::IDENT_ALTITUDE
4966            | prop::DEV_PEERS
4967            | prop::DEV_ADMINS
4968            | prop::DEV_CHANNEL_KEYS => Vec::new(),
4969            prop::IDENT_MOBILE => vec![0],
4970            prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE => vec![1],
4971            other => unreachable!("unexpected property {other}"),
4972        };
4973        (property, value)
4974    }
4975
4976    /// Answer every bounded read batch until the session stops asking.
4977    fn drive_reads(
4978        session: &MobileUlcpSession,
4979        requests: Vec<Vec<u8>>,
4980        value: impl Fn(u32) -> (u32, Vec<u8>),
4981    ) -> UlcpSessionUpdateRecord {
4982        let mut pending = requests;
4983        let mut last = None;
4984        while !pending.is_empty() {
4985            let update = answer_requests(session, pending, &value);
4986            pending = update.outbound_frames.clone();
4987            last = Some(update);
4988        }
4989        last.expect("at least one batch")
4990    }
4991
4992    /// Bring a session to `Attached` against a commissionable device that
4993    /// reports `host_key` as its tethered host.
4994    fn attach_commissionable(
4995        session: &MobileUlcpSession,
4996        selected_host_key: Option<Vec<u8>>,
4997        host_key: Vec<u8>,
4998    ) -> UlcpSessionUpdateRecord {
4999        let begin = session.begin(selected_host_key).unwrap();
5000        drive_reads(session, begin.outbound_frames, move |property| {
5001            if property == prop::HOST_KEY {
5002                (property, host_key.clone())
5003            } else {
5004                commissionable_value(property)
5005            }
5006        })
5007    }
5008
5009    /// Bring a session to `Attached` against a device that also offers the
5010    /// host key tables, which the commissionable fixture deliberately does
5011    /// not.
5012    fn attach_host_keys_capable(session: &MobileUlcpSession) -> UlcpSessionUpdateRecord {
5013        let mut capabilities = commissionable_capabilities();
5014        capabilities.push(cap::HOST_KEYS);
5015        let begin = session.begin(Some(vec![0xAA; 32])).unwrap();
5016        drive_reads(
5017            session,
5018            begin.outbound_frames,
5019            move |property| match property {
5020                prop::CAPS => (property, encoded_capabilities(&capabilities)),
5021                prop::HOST_KEY => (property, vec![0xAA; 32]),
5022                prop::HOST_CHANNEL_KEYS | prop::HOST_PEER_KEYS => (property, Vec::new()),
5023                _ => commissionable_value(property),
5024            },
5025        )
5026    }
5027
5028    /// Consume a configuration batch, returning the writes it made as a
5029    /// property map, the order they were issued in, and the `CMD_SAVE`
5030    /// transaction that closed it.
5031    fn drive_configuration(
5032        session: &MobileUlcpSession,
5033        first_batch: Vec<Vec<u8>>,
5034    ) -> (HashMap<u32, Vec<u8>>, Vec<u32>, u8) {
5035        let mut pending = VecDeque::from(first_batch);
5036        let mut written = HashMap::new();
5037        let mut order = Vec::new();
5038        loop {
5039            let request = pending.pop_front().expect("configuration ends in a save");
5040            let parsed = Frame::parse(&request).unwrap();
5041            if parsed.command() == Some(Cmd::Save) {
5042                return (written, order, parsed.header.tid());
5043            }
5044            assert_eq!(parsed.command(), Some(Cmd::PropSet));
5045            let payload = PropPayload::parse(parsed.payload).unwrap();
5046            order.push(payload.key);
5047            written.insert(payload.key, payload.value.to_vec());
5048            let update = session
5049                .consume(property_response(
5050                    parsed.header.tid(),
5051                    payload.key,
5052                    payload.value,
5053                ))
5054                .unwrap_or_else(|error| {
5055                    panic!("write of property {} failed: {error:?}", payload.key)
5056                });
5057            pending.extend(update.outbound_frames);
5058        }
5059    }
5060
5061    #[test]
5062    fn exported_gatt_round_trip_uses_shared_codec() {
5063        let frame = ulcp_prop_get(3, 4_864).unwrap();
5064        let segments = ulcp_gatt_segments(frame.clone(), 4).unwrap();
5065        let receiver = MobileGattReassembler::new();
5066        let mut completed = None;
5067        for segment in segments {
5068            if let Some(value) = receiver.push(segment.value).unwrap() {
5069                completed = Some(value);
5070            }
5071        }
5072        assert_eq!(completed, Some(frame));
5073    }
5074
5075    #[test]
5076    fn exported_hdlc_round_trip_uses_shared_codec() {
5077        let frame = ulcp_prop_get(3, 4_864).unwrap();
5078        let wire = ulcp_hdlc_encode(frame.clone()).unwrap();
5079
5080        // The export must produce the bytes the shared encoder does,
5081        // or a phone and a serial host would disagree on the wire.
5082        let mut expected = vec![0; hdlc::max_encoded_len(frame.len())];
5083        let length = hdlc::encode_frame(&frame, &mut expected).unwrap();
5084        expected.truncate(length);
5085        assert_eq!(wire, expected);
5086
5087        let decoder = MobileHdlcDecoder::new();
5088        assert_eq!(decoder.push(wire), vec![frame]);
5089    }
5090
5091    #[test]
5092    fn hdlc_decoding_spans_chunk_boundaries_and_batches() {
5093        // A stream hands over arbitrary chunks: a frame can arrive in
5094        // pieces, and one read can carry several frames.
5095        let first = ulcp_prop_get(1, 4_864).unwrap();
5096        let second = ulcp_prop_get(2, 4_865).unwrap();
5097        let mut wire = ulcp_hdlc_encode(first.clone()).unwrap();
5098        wire.extend(ulcp_hdlc_encode(second.clone()).unwrap());
5099
5100        let decoder = MobileHdlcDecoder::new();
5101        let split = wire.len() / 3;
5102        assert!(decoder.push(wire[..split].to_vec()).is_empty());
5103        assert_eq!(decoder.push(wire[split..].to_vec()), vec![first, second]);
5104    }

5106    #[test]
5107    fn hdlc_decoding_escapes_the_framing_bytes() {
5108        // A frame whose payload spells the delimiters must survive.
5109        let frame = vec![hdlc::FLAG, hdlc::ESCAPE, 0x11, 0x13, 0x00, 0xFF];
5110        let decoder = MobileHdlcDecoder::new();
5111        assert_eq!(
5112            decoder.push(ulcp_hdlc_encode(frame.clone()).unwrap()),
5113            vec![frame]
5114        );
5115    }
