5117    #[test]
5118    fn hdlc_decoding_resynchronizes_past_noise() {
5119        // Opening a bridged port mid-transmission leaves a partial
5120        // frame in the stream; the next good one must still arrive.
5121        let frame = ulcp_prop_get(7, 4_864).unwrap();
5122        let mut wire = vec![0x01, 0x02, hdlc::FLAG, 0xDE, 0xAD];
5123        wire.extend(ulcp_hdlc_encode(frame.clone()).unwrap());
5124
5125        let decoder = MobileHdlcDecoder::new();
5126        assert_eq!(decoder.push(wire), vec![frame]);
5127    }

5129    #[test]
5130    fn hdlc_encoding_admits_exactly_what_gatt_does() {
5131        // Both transports carry the same frames, so a phone cannot
5132        // build one it could send over BLE but not over a socket.
5133        let largest = vec![0xA5; MAX_FRAME];
5134        let decoder = MobileHdlcDecoder::new();
5135        assert_eq!(
5136            decoder.push(ulcp_hdlc_encode(largest.clone()).unwrap()),
5137            vec![largest]
5138        );
5139        assert!(matches!(
5140            ulcp_hdlc_encode(vec![0xA5; MAX_FRAME + 1]),
5141            Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
5142        ));
5143    }

5145    #[test]
5146    fn a_reset_decoder_drops_the_partial_frame() {
5147        let frame = ulcp_prop_get(5, 4_864).unwrap();
5148        let wire = ulcp_hdlc_encode(frame.clone()).unwrap();
5149
5150        let decoder = MobileHdlcDecoder::new();
5151        assert!(decoder.push(wire[..wire.len() - 2].to_vec()).is_empty());
5152        // Without the reset the tail would finish the stale frame.
5153        decoder.reset();
5154        assert!(decoder.push(wire[wire.len() - 2..].to_vec()).is_empty());
5155        assert_eq!(decoder.push(wire), vec![frame]);
5156    }

5243    #[test]
5244    fn a_reading_is_carried_when_reported_and_missed_quietly_when_not() {
5245        let capabilities = encoded_capabilities(&[cap::BATTERY, cap::ALERT]);
5246        let base = |extra: Vec<UlcpPropertyFrameRecord>| {
5247            let mut responses = vec![
5248                response(prop::CAPS, &capabilities),
5249                response(prop::INTERFACE_TYPE, &[INTERFACE_TYPE as u8]),
5250                response(prop::PHY_ENABLED, &[1]),
5251                response(prop::PHY_FREQ, &915_000u32.to_le_bytes()),
5252                response(prop::PHY_TX_POWER, &[14]),
5253            ];
5254            responses.extend(extra);
5255            inspect_ulcp_sync(responses).unwrap()
5256        };
5257
5258        let reported = base(vec![
5259            response(prop::BATTERY, &[0b110, 82, 1]),
5260            response(prop::ALERT, &[AlertState::Locate.code() as u8]),
5261        ]);
5262        assert_eq!(reported.battery.unwrap().percentage, Some(82));
5263        assert_eq!(reported.alert, Some(UlcpAlertState::Locate));
5264
5265        // The case that matters: a device that has said nothing about
5266        // either is not a device withholding settings. Both are absent, and
5267        // neither shows up in the notice about configuration this phone
5268        // would write over.
5269        let silent = base(Vec::new());
5270        assert!(silent.supports_battery && silent.supports_alert);
5271        assert_eq!(silent.battery, None);
5272        assert_eq!(silent.alert, None);
5273        assert!(silent.unreadable_properties.is_empty());
5274    }

5302    #[test]
5303    fn full_inspection_reports_only_digest_counts() {
5304        let capabilities = (cap::HOST_FILTER..=cap::BATTERY)
5305            .map(|capability| capability as u8)
5306            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
5307        let properties = ulcp_inspection_properties(capabilities.clone()).unwrap();
5308        assert!(properties.contains(&prop::HOST_RX_FILTERS));
5309        assert!(properties.contains(&prop::HOST_RX_QUEUE_COUNT));
5310        assert!(properties.contains(&prop::HOST_AUTO_ACK));
5311
5312        let sync = inspect_ulcp_sync(vec![
5313            response(prop::CAPS, &capabilities),
5314            response(prop::INTERFACE_TYPE, &[INTERFACE_TYPE as u8]),
5315            response(prop::PHY_ENABLED, &[1]),
5316            response(prop::PHY_FREQ, &868_100u32.to_le_bytes()),
5317            response(prop::PHY_TX_POWER, &[22]),
5318            response(prop::SAVED, &[1]),
5319            response(prop::HOST_RX_FILTERS, &[]),
5320            response(prop::HOST_CHANNEL_KEYS, &[1, 2, 3, 4]),
5321            response(prop::HOST_PEER_KEYS, &[7; 32]),
5322            response(prop::HOST_RX_QUEUE_COUNT, &3u16.to_le_bytes()),
5323            response(prop::HOST_RX_QUEUE_DROPPED, &4u32.to_le_bytes()),
5324            response(prop::HOST_AUTO_ACK, &[1]),
5325            response(prop::DEV_PEERS, &[7; 64]),
5326            response(prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE, &[1]),
5327        ])
5328        .unwrap();
5329        assert_eq!(sync.saved, Some(SavedSnapshotRecord::Current));
5330        assert_eq!(sync.queued_frames, Some(3));
5331        assert_eq!(sync.dropped_frames, Some(4));
5332        assert_eq!(sync.filter_count, Some(0));
5333        assert_eq!(sync.host_channel_count, Some(2));
5334        assert_eq!(sync.host_peer_count, Some(1));
5335        assert_eq!(sync.auto_ack, Some(true));
5336        // The device-identity peer list is the one key table read back
5337        // losslessly rather than as a digest count.
5338        assert!(sync.supports_device_identity);
5339        assert_eq!(sync.dev_peer_keys, Some(vec![vec![7; 32], vec![7; 32]]));
5340    }

5359    #[test]
5360    fn mobile_session_owns_sync_tids_and_attaches_transparent_radio() {
5361        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
5362        let begin = session.begin(Some(vec![0xAA; 32])).unwrap();
5363        assert_eq!(begin.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Synchronizing);
5364        assert_eq!(begin.outbound_frames.len(), 7);
5365        assert_eq!(
5366            begin
5367                .outbound_frames
5368                .iter()
5369                .map(|request| property_request(request).0)
5370                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
5371            [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7]
5372        );
5373
5374        let inspection =
5375            answer_requests(&session, begin.outbound_frames, |property| match property {
5376                prop::LAST_STATUS => (property, vec![0]),
5377                prop::PROTOCOL_VERSION => (property, vec![6, 0]),
5378                prop::CAPS => (property, vec![cap::WRITABLE_RAW_STREAM as u8]),
5379                prop::DEV_KEY => (property, Vec::new()),
5380                prop::DEV_NAME => (property, b"Transparent".to_vec()),
5381                prop::BATTERY => (property, Vec::new()),
5382                prop::HOST_KEY => (prop::LAST_STATUS, vec![2]),
5383                _ => unreachable!(),
5384            });
5385        assert_eq!(inspection.outbound_frames.len(), 4);
5386        assert_eq!(
5387            inspection.snapshot.host_ownership,
5388            UlcpHostOwnership::Unsupported
5389        );
5390
5391        let attached =
5392            answer_requests(
5393                &session,
5394                inspection.outbound_frames,
5395                |property| match property {
5396                    prop::INTERFACE_TYPE => (property, vec![INTERFACE_TYPE as u8]),
5397                    prop::PHY_ENABLED => (property, vec![1]),
5398                    prop::PHY_FREQ => (property, 915_000u32.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
5399                    prop::PHY_TX_POWER => (property, vec![14]),
5400                    _ => unreachable!(),
5401                },
5402            );
5403        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
5404        assert_eq!(
5405            attached.snapshot.device_name.as_deref(),
5406            Some("Transparent")
5407        );
5408        assert_eq!(
5409            attached.snapshot.provisioning.unwrap().frequency_khz,
5410            915_000
5411        );
5412    }

5414    #[test]
5415    fn mobile_session_owns_claim_then_save_choreography() {
5416        let host_key = vec![0xAA; 32];
5417        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
5418        let begin = session.begin(Some(host_key.clone())).unwrap();
5419        let awaiting =
5420            answer_requests(&session, begin.outbound_frames, |property| match property {
5421                prop::LAST_STATUS => (property, vec![0]),
5422                prop::PROTOCOL_VERSION => (property, vec![6, 0]),
5423                prop::CAPS => (property, vec![cap::HOST_FILTER as u8, cap::SAVE as u8]),
5424                prop::DEV_KEY | prop::DEV_NAME | prop::BATTERY | prop::HOST_KEY => {
5425                    (property, Vec::new())
5426                }
5427                _ => unreachable!(),
5428            });
5429        assert_eq!(awaiting.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::AwaitingHost);
5430        assert_eq!(
5431            awaiting.snapshot.host_ownership,
5432            UlcpHostOwnership::Unclaimed
5433        );
5434
5435        let claim = session.claim(host_key.clone()).unwrap();
5436        assert_eq!(claim.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Claiming);
5437        assert_eq!(claim.outbound_frames.len(), 1);
5438        let parsed_claim = Frame::parse(&claim.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
5439        assert_eq!(parsed_claim.command(), Some(Cmd::PropSet));
5440        let payload = PropPayload::parse(parsed_claim.payload).unwrap();
5441        assert_eq!(payload.key, prop::HOST_KEY);
5442        assert_eq!(payload.value, host_key);
5443
5444        let save = session
5445            .consume(property_response(
5446                parsed_claim.header.tid(),
5447                prop::HOST_KEY,
5448                &host_key,
5449            ))
5450            .unwrap();
5451        assert_eq!(save.outbound_frames.len(), 1);
5452        let parsed_save = Frame::parse(&save.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
5453        assert_eq!(parsed_save.command(), Some(Cmd::Save));
5454
5455        let inspection = session
5456            .consume(property_response(
5457                parsed_save.header.tid(),
5458                prop::LAST_STATUS,
5459                &[0],
5460            ))
5461            .unwrap();
5462        assert_eq!(inspection.outbound_frames.len(), 6);
5463        let attached =
5464            answer_requests(
5465                &session,
5466                inspection.outbound_frames,
5467                |property| match property {
5468                    prop::INTERFACE_TYPE => (property, vec![INTERFACE_TYPE as u8]),
5469                    prop::PHY_ENABLED => (property, vec![1]),
5470                    prop::PHY_FREQ => (property, 868_100u32.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
5471                    prop::PHY_TX_POWER => (property, vec![14]),
5472                    prop::SAVED => (property, vec![1]),
5473                    prop::HOST_RX_FILTERS => (property, Vec::new()),
5474                    _ => unreachable!(),
5475                },
5476            );
5477        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
5478        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.host_ownership, UlcpHostOwnership::Ours);
5479        assert_eq!(
5480            attached.snapshot.provisioning.unwrap().saved,
5481            Some(SavedSnapshotRecord::Current)
5482        );
5483
5484        let changed_host = session
5485            .consume(property_response(
5486                frame::TID_UNSOLICITED,
5487                prop::HOST_KEY,
5488                &[0xBB; 32],
5489            ))
5490            .unwrap();
5491        assert_eq!(changed_host.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::AwaitingHost);
5492        assert_eq!(
5493            changed_host.snapshot.host_ownership,
5494            UlcpHostOwnership::OtherHost
5495        );
5496    }

5498    #[test]
5499    fn administrative_session_attaches_without_claiming_anyones_radio() {
5500        let phone = vec![0xAA; 32];
5501        let other_phone = vec![0xBB; 32];
5502
5503        // A radio someone else tethered. An administrative session has no
5504        // decision to put to the user, so it attaches — and still reports
5505        // whose radio it is, because that is worth showing.
5506        let session = MobileUlcpSession::administrative();
5507        let attached = attach_commissionable(&session, Some(phone.clone()), other_phone.clone());
5508        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
5509        assert_eq!(
5510            attached.snapshot.host_ownership,
5511            UlcpHostOwnership::OtherHost
5512        );
5513        assert_eq!(
5514            session.claim(phone.clone()),
5515            Err(MobileError::AdministrativeSession)
5516        );
5517
5518        // An unclaimed radio likewise: commissioning ten repeaters must
5519        // not leave this phone's host key on any of them.
5520        let unclaimed = MobileUlcpSession::administrative();
5521        let attached = attach_commissionable(&unclaimed, Some(phone.clone()), Vec::new());
5522        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
5523        assert_eq!(
5524            attached.snapshot.host_ownership,
5525            UlcpHostOwnership::Unclaimed
5526        );
5527
5528        // The tethered session is the one that must pause: it is about to
5529        // take the radio from the other phone.
5530        let tethered = MobileUlcpSession::new();
5531        let begin = tethered.begin(Some(phone.clone())).unwrap();
5532        let awaiting = answer_requests(&tethered, begin.outbound_frames, move |property| {
5533            if property == prop::HOST_KEY {
5534                (property, other_phone.clone())
5535            } else {
5536                commissionable_value(property)
5537            }
5538        });
5539        assert_eq!(awaiting.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::AwaitingHost);
5540
5541        // A host-key change pushed mid-session does not evict an
5542        // administrative session either.
5543        let pushed = session
5544            .consume(property_response(
5545                frame::TID_UNSOLICITED,
5546                prop::HOST_KEY,
5547                &[0xCC; 32],
5548            ))
5549            .unwrap();
5550        assert_eq!(pushed.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
5551        assert_eq!(pushed.snapshot.host_ownership, UlcpHostOwnership::OtherHost);
5552    }

5590    #[test]
5591    fn repeater_policy_round_trips_through_the_sync_reducer() {
5592        let capabilities = encoded_capabilities(&commissionable_capabilities());
5593        let sync = inspect_ulcp_sync(vec![
5594            response(prop::CAPS, &capabilities),
5595            response(prop::INTERFACE_TYPE, &[INTERFACE_TYPE as u8]),
5596            response(prop::PHY_ENABLED, &[1]),
5597            response(prop::PHY_FREQ, &915_000u32.to_le_bytes()),
5598            response(prop::PHY_TX_POWER, &[14]),
5599            response(prop::SAVED, &[saved::CURRENT]),
5600            response(prop::HOST_RX_FILTERS, &[]),
5601            response(prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED, &[1]),
5602            // SJC and SFO, the strings exactly as they were written.
5603            response(
5604                prop::MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS,
5605                &[3, b'S', b'J', b'C', 3, b'S', b'F', b'O'],
5606            ),
5607            response(prop::MAC_REPEATER_DEFAULT_REGION, &[0x78, 0x53]),
5608            response(prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI, &(-115i16).to_le_bytes()),
5609            response(prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_SNR, &[(-7i8) as u8]),
5610            response(prop::IDENT_ROLE, &[3]),
5611            response(prop::IDENT_MOBILE, &[1]),
5612            response(prop::DEV_PEERS, &[]),
5613            response(prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE, &[1]),
5614        ])
5615        .unwrap();
5616        assert_eq!(
5617            sync.repeater,
5618            Some(UlcpRepeaterSettingsRecord {
5619                enabled: true,
5620                regions: vec!["SJC".to_owned(), "SFO".to_owned()],
5621                default_region: Some(vec![0x78, 0x53]),
5622                min_rssi_dbm: Some(-115),
5623                min_snr_db: Some(-7),
5624            })
5625        );
5626        assert_eq!(sync.ident_role, Some(3));
5627        assert_eq!(sync.ident_mobile, Some(true));
5628
5629        // An odd-length region list is not a set of region codes, so the
5630        // policy is not reported — but the device still is. A repeater
5631        // without an identity of its own, on the other hand, is not a
5632        // repeater, and that is a malformed capability set.
5633        let malformed = |property, value: &[u8]| {
5634            let mut responses = vec![
5635                response(prop::CAPS, &capabilities),
5636                response(prop::INTERFACE_TYPE, &[INTERFACE_TYPE as u8]),
5637                response(prop::PHY_ENABLED, &[1]),
5638                response(prop::PHY_FREQ, &915_000u32.to_le_bytes()),
5639                response(prop::PHY_TX_POWER, &[14]),
5640                response(prop::SAVED, &[saved::CURRENT]),
5641                response(prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED, &[0]),
5642                response(prop::MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS, &[]),
5643                response(prop::MAC_REPEATER_DEFAULT_REGION, &[]),
5644                response(prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI, &[]),
5645                response(prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_SNR, &[]),
5646                response(prop::IDENT_ROLE, &[]),
5647                response(prop::IDENT_MOBILE, &[0]),
5648                response(prop::DEV_PEERS, &[]),
5649                response(prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE, &[1]),
5650            ];
5651            responses.retain(|entry| entry.property_id != property);
5652            responses.push(response(property, value));
5653            inspect_ulcp_sync(responses)
5654        };
5655        for property in [
5656            prop::MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS,
5657            prop::MAC_REPEATER_DEFAULT_REGION,
5658            prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI,
5659        ] {
5660            let sync = malformed(property, &[0x8D, 0x53, 0x7C]).expect("device still described");
5661            assert_eq!(sync.repeater, None, "property {property}");
5662            assert!(sync.supports_repeater, "property {property}");
5663            assert!(
5664                sync.unreadable_properties.contains(&property),
5665                "property {property}"
5666            );
5667        }
5668        assert_eq!(
5669            ulcp_inspection_properties(encoded_capabilities(&[cap::REPEATER])),
5670            Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
5671        );
5672    }
5673
5674    /// A device that refuses one capability-gated property — firmware
5675    /// older than the capability it advertises — is a device with one
5676    /// unknown setting, not one this phone cannot administer. It attaches,
5677    /// it describes itself, and it stays configurable; the refused setting
5678    /// is absent, named, and left out of the write that follows.
5679    #[test]
5680    fn a_refused_property_still_yields_an_administrable_device() {
5681        let session = MobileUlcpSession::administrative();
5682        let begin = session.begin(Some(vec![0xAA; 32])).unwrap();
5683        let attached = drive_reads(&session, begin.outbound_frames, |property| match property {
5684            // One property of its own, and one part of a policy that is
5685            // only meaningful whole.
5686            prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE | prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI => (
5687                prop::LAST_STATUS,
5688                vec![umsh_ulcp::Status::PROP_NOT_FOUND.0 as u8],
5689            ),
5690            prop::HOST_KEY => (property, vec![0xBB; 32]),
5691            other => commissionable_value(other),
5692        });
5693
5694        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
5695        // Nobody asked to read that property in particular; they asked to
5696        // attach, and the attach succeeded. Reporting it as a failed
5697        // operation is what used to strand the caller with no snapshot.
5698        assert_eq!(attached.operation_error, None);
5699        let sync = attached.snapshot.provisioning.expect("device described");
5700        assert!(sync.supports_device_identity);
5701        assert_eq!(sync.dev_discoverable, None);
5702        assert_eq!(
5703            sync.unreadable_properties,
5704            vec![prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI, prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE]
5705        );
5706        // The rest of the same capability is unaffected.
5707        assert_eq!(sync.dev_peer_keys, Some(Vec::new()));
5708        // A policy missing one part is not a policy, so none of it is
5709        // reported — the device still says it can forward.
5710        assert!(sync.supports_repeater);
5711        assert_eq!(sync.repeater, None);
5712
5713        let configured = session
5714            .configure_device(UlcpDeviceConfigRecord {
5715                radio: UlcpRadioSettingsRecord {
5716                    device_name: None,
5717                    phy_enabled: true,
5718                    frequency_khz: 906_875,
5719                    transmit_power_dbm: 20,
5720                    bandwidth_hz: None,
5721                    spreading_factor: None,
5722                    coding_rate_denom: None,
5723                    duty_cycle_limit: None,
5724                },
5725                ident_role: None,
5726                ident_mobile: Some(true),
5727                dev_discoverable: Some(true),
5728                repeater: Some(UlcpRepeaterSettingsRecord {
5729                    enabled: false,
5730                    regions: Vec::new(),
5731                    default_region: None,
5732                    min_rssi_dbm: None,
5733                    min_snr_db: None,
5734                }),
5735                tz_offset_min: None,
5736                gnss: None,
5737                advert: None,
5738            })
5739            .unwrap();
5740        let (written, _, _) = drive_configuration(&session, configured.outbound_frames);
5741        // The write the device would have rejected — failing the whole
5742        // pass over a setting nobody can even see — is never sent, and
5743        // neither is the rest of the policy it belongs to: a device left
5744        // forwarding under half a policy is worse than one left alone.
5745        assert!(!written.contains_key(&prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE));
5746        for property in [
5747            prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED,
5748            prop::MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS,
5749            prop::MAC_REPEATER_DEFAULT_REGION,
5750            prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI,
5751            prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_SNR,
5752        ] {
5753            assert!(!written.contains_key(&property), "property {property}");
5754        }
5755        assert_eq!(written.get(&prop::IDENT_MOBILE), Some(&vec![1]));
5756        assert_eq!(
5757            written.get(&prop::PHY_FREQ),
5758            Some(&906_875u32.to_le_bytes().to_vec())
5759        );
5760    }
5761
5762    #[test]
5763    fn configuring_a_device_writes_its_whole_domain_as_a_property_map() {
5764        let session = MobileUlcpSession::administrative();
5765        let attached = attach_commissionable(&session, Some(vec![0xAA; 32]), vec![0xBB; 32]);
5766        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
5767
5768        let configured = session
5769            .configure_device(UlcpDeviceConfigRecord {
5770                radio: UlcpRadioSettingsRecord {
5771                    device_name: Some("Ridge repeater".into()),
5772                    phy_enabled: true,
5773                    frequency_khz: 906_875,
5774                    transmit_power_dbm: 22,
5775                    bandwidth_hz: None,
5776                    spreading_factor: None,
5777                    coding_rate_denom: None,
5778                    duty_cycle_limit: None,
5779                },
5780                ident_role: Some(3),
5781                ident_mobile: Some(false),
5782                dev_discoverable: Some(false),
5783                repeater: Some(UlcpRepeaterSettingsRecord {
5784                    enabled: true,
5785                    regions: vec!["SJC".to_owned()],
5786                    default_region: Some(vec![0x78, 0x53]),
5787                    min_rssi_dbm: Some(-115),
5788                    min_snr_db: Some(-7),
5789                }),
5790                tz_offset_min: None,
5791                gnss: None,
5792                advert: None,
5793            })
5794            .unwrap();
5795        assert_eq!(configured.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Configuring);
5796
5797        let (written, order, save_tid) = drive_configuration(&session, configured.outbound_frames);
5798
5799        // The whole configuration is a property -> value map with nothing
5800        // else in it. A template feature that produces such a map has
5801        // everything it needs; nothing here is shaped around this record.
5802        assert_eq!(
5803            written,
5804            HashMap::from([
5805                (prop::DEV_NAME, b"Ridge repeater".to_vec()),
5806                (prop::PHY_FREQ, 906_875u32.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
5807                (prop::PHY_TX_POWER, vec![22]),
5808                (prop::IDENT_ROLE, vec![3]),
5809                (prop::IDENT_MOBILE, vec![0]),
5810                (prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE, vec![0]),
5811                (prop::MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS, vec![3, b'S', b'J', b'C']),
5812                (prop::MAC_REPEATER_DEFAULT_REGION, vec![0x78, 0x53]),
5813                (
5814                    prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI,
5815                    (-115i16).to_le_bytes().to_vec()
5816                ),
5817                (prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_SNR, vec![(-7i8) as u8]),
5818                (prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED, vec![1]),
5819                (prop::PHY_ENABLED, vec![1]),
5820            ])
5821        );
5822        // Forwarding starts only once the whole policy — and the radio it
5823        // forwards over — is in place.
5824        assert_eq!(
5825            &order[order.len() - 2..],
5826            &[prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED, prop::PHY_ENABLED]
5827        );
5828
5829        let attached = session
5830            .consume(property_response(save_tid, prop::LAST_STATUS, &[0]))
5831            .unwrap();
5832        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
5833        assert_eq!(
5834            attached.snapshot.host_ownership,
5835            UlcpHostOwnership::OtherHost
5836        );
5837        let provisioning = attached.snapshot.provisioning.unwrap();
5838        assert_eq!(provisioning.frequency_khz, 906_875);
5839        assert_eq!(provisioning.ident_role, Some(3));
5840        assert_eq!(
5841            provisioning.repeater,
5842            Some(UlcpRepeaterSettingsRecord {
5843                enabled: true,
5844                regions: vec!["SJC".to_owned()],
5845                default_region: Some(vec![0x78, 0x53]),
5846                min_rssi_dbm: Some(-115),
5847                min_snr_db: Some(-7),
5848            })
5849        );
5850    }

5852    #[test]
5853    fn device_configuration_must_match_what_the_device_can_do() {
5854        let session = MobileUlcpSession::administrative();
5855        attach_commissionable(&session, None, Vec::new());
5856
5857        let radio = UlcpRadioSettingsRecord {
5858            device_name: None,
5859            phy_enabled: true,
5860            frequency_khz: 915_000,
5861            transmit_power_dbm: 14,
5862            bandwidth_hz: None,
5863            spreading_factor: None,
5864            coding_rate_denom: None,
5865            duty_cycle_limit: None,
5866        };
5867        let repeater = UlcpRepeaterSettingsRecord {
5868            enabled: true,
5869            regions: Vec::new(),
5870            default_region: None,
5871            min_rssi_dbm: None,
5872            min_snr_db: None,
5873        };
5874        let configure = |ident_role, ident_mobile, dev_discoverable, repeater| {
5875            session.configure_device(UlcpDeviceConfigRecord {
5876                radio: radio.clone(),
5877                ident_role,
5878                ident_mobile,
5879                dev_discoverable,
5880                repeater,
5881                tz_offset_min: None,
5882                gnss: None,
5883                advert: None,
5884            })
5885        };
5886
5887        // Every capability-gated field is required, because the record
5888        // states a whole desired configuration rather than a patch.
5889        assert_eq!(
5890            configure(Some(3), None, Some(true), Some(repeater.clone())),
5891            Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
5892        );
5893        assert_eq!(
5894            configure(None, Some(false), Some(true), None),
5895            Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
5896        );
5897        assert_eq!(
5898            configure(None, Some(false), None, Some(repeater.clone())),
5899            Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
5900        );
5901        // A region string is 1 to 24 octets; outside that the device
5902        // refuses it, so the write never leaves the phone.
5903        for bad in ["", &"A".repeat(items::REGION_STRING_MAX_LEN + 1)] {
5904            assert_eq!(
5905                configure(
5906                    None,
5907                    Some(false),
5908                    Some(true),
5909                    Some(UlcpRepeaterSettingsRecord {
5910                        regions: vec![bad.to_owned()],
5911                        ..repeater.clone()
5912                    })
5913                ),
5914                Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
5915            );
5916        }
5917        assert_eq!(
5918            configure(
5919                None,
5920                Some(false),
5921                Some(true),
5922                Some(UlcpRepeaterSettingsRecord {
5923                    default_region: Some(vec![0x78, 0x53, 0x00]),
5924                    ..repeater.clone()
5925                })
5926            ),
5927            Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
5928        );
5929
5930        // An omitted role is the device deriving its own, written as an
5931        // empty value rather than skipped.
5932        let configured = configure(None, Some(true), Some(true), Some(repeater)).unwrap();
5933        let (written, ..) = drive_configuration(&session, configured.outbound_frames);
5934        assert_eq!(written.get(&prop::IDENT_ROLE), Some(&Vec::new()));
5935        assert_eq!(written.get(&prop::IDENT_MOBILE), Some(&vec![1]));
5936        assert_eq!(written.get(&prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE), Some(&vec![1]));
5937        assert_eq!(written.get(&prop::MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS), Some(&Vec::new()));
5938        assert_eq!(written.get(&prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI), Some(&Vec::new()));
5939    }
5940
5941    #[test]
5942    fn region_codes_convert_between_text_and_wire_octets() {
5943        assert_eq!(region_code_from_string("SJC".into()).unwrap(), [0x78, 0x53]);
5944        assert_eq!(region_code_description(vec![0x78, 0x53]).unwrap(), "SJC");
5945        // A two-letter short code is a region in its own right.
5946        assert_eq!(region_code_from_string("WA".into()).unwrap(), [0x8F, 0xE8]);
5947        assert_eq!(region_code_description(vec![0x8F, 0xE8]).unwrap(), "WA");
5948        // A name lands outside the letter space, so it never renders as
5949        // letters and round-trips through hex.
5950        let named = region_code_from_string("Rogue Valley".into()).unwrap();
5951        assert_eq!(named, [0xC0, 0xF9]);
5952        let described = region_code_description(named.clone()).unwrap();
5953        assert_eq!(described, "0xC0F9");
5954        assert_eq!(region_code_from_string(described).unwrap(), named);
5955
5956        // Case is not part of a region's identity, on either derivation.
5957        assert_eq!(region_code_from_string("sjc".into()).unwrap(), [0x78, 0x53]);
5958        assert_eq!(
5959            region_code_from_string("rogue valley".into()).unwrap(),
5960            named
5961        );
5962
5963        assert_eq!(
5964            region_code_from_string("  ".into()),
5965            Err(MobileError::InvalidRegionCode)
5966        );
5967        assert_eq!(
5968            region_code_description(vec![0x78]),
5969            Err(MobileError::InvalidRegionCode)
5970        );
5971    }

5984    #[test]
5985    fn mobile_session_emits_typed_raw_receive_during_sync() {
5986        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
5987        session.begin(None).unwrap();
5988
5989        let metadata = BufferedRxMeta {
5990            rx: umsh_ulcp::RxMeta {
5991                rssi_dbm: Some(-87),
5992                lqi: core::num::NonZeroU8::new(42),
5993                snr_cb: Some(125),
5994            },
5995            flags: RX_FLAG_BUFFERED | RX_FLAG_ACKED,
5996            age_s: 9,
5997        };
5998        let mut metadata_bytes = [0; BufferedRxMeta::WIRE_LEN];
5999        metadata.encode(&mut metadata_bytes).unwrap();
6000        let mut bytes = vec![0; MAX_FRAME];
6001        let len = frame::str_recv(
6002            &mut bytes,
6003            umsh_ulcp::ids::stream::PHY_RAW,
6004            &[1, 2, 3],
6005            &metadata_bytes,
6006        )
6007        .unwrap();
6008        bytes.truncate(len);
6009
6010        let update = session.consume(bytes).unwrap();
6011        assert_eq!(update.received_frames.len(), 1);
6012        assert_eq!(
6013            update.received_frames[0],
6014            UlcpReceivedFrameRecord {
6015                data: vec![1, 2, 3],
6016                rssi_dbm: Some(-87),
6017                lqi: Some(42),
6018                snr_cb: Some(125),
6019                was_buffered: true,
6020                was_acknowledged: true,
6021                age_seconds: 9,
6022            }
6023        );
6024        assert!(update.outbound_frames.is_empty());
6025        assert!(update.waiting_for_responses);
6026    }

6028    #[test]
6029    fn mobile_session_reports_raw_transmit_rejection_without_ending_session() {
6030        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
6031        let begin = session.begin(None).unwrap();
6032        let inspection =
6033            answer_requests(&session, begin.outbound_frames, |property| match property {
6034                prop::LAST_STATUS => (property, vec![0]),
6035                prop::PROTOCOL_VERSION => (property, vec![6, 0]),
6036                prop::CAPS => (property, vec![cap::WRITABLE_RAW_STREAM as u8]),
6037                prop::DEV_KEY | prop::DEV_NAME | prop::BATTERY => (property, Vec::new()),
6038                prop::HOST_KEY => (prop::LAST_STATUS, vec![2]),
6039                _ => unreachable!(),
6040            });
6041        let attached =
6042            answer_requests(
6043                &session,
6044                inspection.outbound_frames,
6045                |property| match property {
6046                    prop::INTERFACE_TYPE => (property, vec![INTERFACE_TYPE as u8]),
6047                    prop::PHY_ENABLED => (property, vec![1]),
6048                    prop::PHY_FREQ => (property, 915_000u32.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
6049                    prop::PHY_TX_POWER => (property, vec![14]),
6050                    _ => unreachable!(),
6051                },
6052            );
6053        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
6054
6055        let transmit = session.transmit_raw(vec![1, 2, 3], false).unwrap();
6056        assert!(transmit.raw_transmit_pending);
6057        assert_eq!(transmit.raw_transmit_result, None);
6058        assert_eq!(transmit.outbound_frames.len(), 1);
6059        let second_transmit = session.transmit_raw(vec![4], false).unwrap();
6060        assert_ne!(
6061            transmit.raw_transmit_started_transaction_id,
6062            second_transmit.raw_transmit_started_transaction_id
6063        );
6064
6065        let request = Frame::parse(&transmit.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6066        let rejected = session
6067            .consume(property_response(
6068                request.header.tid(),
6069                prop::LAST_STATUS,
6070                &[umsh_ulcp::Status::INVALID_STATE.0 as u8],
6071            ))
6072            .unwrap();
6073        assert_eq!(rejected.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
6074        assert!(rejected.raw_transmit_pending);
6075        assert_eq!(
6076            rejected.raw_transmit_result,
6077            Some(UlcpRawTransmitResultRecord {
6078                transaction_id: request.header.tid(),
6079                status_code: umsh_ulcp::Status::INVALID_STATE.0,
6080                status_name: "Status::INVALID_STATE".into(),
6081                disposition: UlcpRawTransmitDisposition::Rejected,
6082            })
6083        );
6084        let second_request = Frame::parse(&second_transmit.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6085        let completed = session
6086            .consume(property_response(
6087                second_request.header.tid(),
6088                prop::LAST_STATUS,
6089                &[umsh_ulcp::Status::OK.0 as u8],
6090            ))
6091            .unwrap();
6092        assert!(!completed.raw_transmit_pending);
6093
6094        // A radio-level rejection completes only that send; the attached
6095        // session remains usable for the next raw frame.
6096        let retryable = session.transmit_raw(vec![5], false).unwrap();
6097        let request = Frame::parse(&retryable.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6098        let busy = session
6099            .consume(property_response(
6100                request.header.tid(),
6101                prop::LAST_STATUS,
6102                &[umsh_ulcp::Status::BUSY.0 as u8],
6103            ))
6104            .unwrap();
6105        assert_eq!(
6106            busy.raw_transmit_result.unwrap().disposition,
6107            UlcpRawTransmitDisposition::Retry
6108        );
6109
6110        let abandoned = session.transmit_raw(vec![6], false).unwrap();
6111        let abandoned_request = Frame::parse(&abandoned.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6112        assert!(
6113            !session
6114                .abandon_raw_transmits(vec![abandoned_request.header.tid()])
6115                .raw_transmit_pending
6116        );
6117
6118        // A status error for an ordinary property operation is also
6119        // nonfatal. Finish the rest of the bounded batch, recover to Attached,
6120        // and prove the same session can issue another raw transmission.
6121        let configured = session
6122            .configure(UlcpRadioSettingsRecord {
6123                device_name: None,
6124                phy_enabled: true,
6125                frequency_khz: 915_000,
6126                transmit_power_dbm: 14,
6127                bandwidth_hz: None,
6128                spreading_factor: None,
6129                coding_rate_denom: None,
6130                duty_cycle_limit: None,
6131            })
6132            .unwrap();
6133        let mut final_update = None;
6134        for (index, request) in configured.outbound_frames.into_iter().enumerate() {
6135            let parsed = Frame::parse(&request).unwrap();
6136            let payload = PropPayload::parse(parsed.payload).unwrap();
6137            let response = if index == 0 {
6138                property_response(
6139                    parsed.header.tid(),
6140                    prop::LAST_STATUS,
6141                    &[umsh_ulcp::Status::INVALID_ARGUMENT.0 as u8],
6142                )
6143            } else {
6144                property_response(parsed.header.tid(), payload.key, payload.value)
6145            };
6146            let update = session.consume(response).unwrap();
6147            if index == 0 {
6148                assert_eq!(
6149                    update.operation_error,
6150                    Some(UlcpOperationErrorRecord {
6151                        operation: format!("set property {}", payload.key),
6152                        status_code: umsh_ulcp::Status::INVALID_ARGUMENT.0,
6153                        status_name: "Status::INVALID_ARGUMENT".into(),
6154                    })
6155                );
6156            }
6157            final_update = Some(update);
6158        }
6159        assert_eq!(
6160            final_update.unwrap().snapshot.phase,
6161            UlcpSessionPhase::Attached
6162        );
6163        assert!(session.transmit_raw(vec![6], false).is_ok());
6164    }

6166    #[test]
6167    fn mobile_session_verifies_radio_configuration_then_saves() {
6168        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
6169        let begin = session.begin(None).unwrap();
6170        let inspection =
6171            answer_requests(&session, begin.outbound_frames, |property| match property {
6172                prop::LAST_STATUS => (property, vec![0]),
6173                prop::PROTOCOL_VERSION => (property, vec![6, 0]),
6174                prop::CAPS => (
6175                    property,
6176                    encoded_capabilities(&[
6177                        cap::SAVE,
6178                        cap::DEV_NAME,
6179                        cap::PHY_LORA,
6180                        cap::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT,
6181                    ]),
6182                ),
6183                prop::DEV_NAME => (property, b"Old name".to_vec()),
6184                prop::DEV_KEY | prop::BATTERY => (property, Vec::new()),
6185                prop::HOST_KEY => (prop::LAST_STATUS, vec![2]),
6186                _ => unreachable!(),
6187            });
6188        let partial =
6189            answer_requests(
6190                &session,
6191                inspection.outbound_frames,
6192                |property| match property {
6193                    prop::INTERFACE_TYPE => (property, vec![INTERFACE_TYPE as u8]),
6194                    prop::PHY_ENABLED => (property, vec![1]),
6195                    prop::PHY_FREQ => (property, 915_000u32.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
6196                    prop::PHY_TX_POWER => (property, vec![14]),
6197                    prop::PHY_LORA_BW => (property, 125_000u32.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
6198                    prop::PHY_LORA_SF => (property, vec![9]),
6199                    prop::PHY_LORA_CR => (property, vec![5]),
6200                    _ => unreachable!(),
6201                },
6202            );
6203        let attached = answer_requests(
6204            &session,
6205            partial.outbound_frames,
6206            |property| match property {
6207                prop::PHY_DUTY_NOW => (property, 65u16.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
6208                prop::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT => (property, 655u16.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
6209                prop::SAVED => (property, vec![1]),
6210                _ => unreachable!(),
6211            },
6212        );
6213        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
6214
6215        let configured = session
6216            .configure(UlcpRadioSettingsRecord {
6217                device_name: Some("Trail radio".into()),
6218                phy_enabled: true,
6219                frequency_khz: 868_100,
6220                transmit_power_dbm: 20,
6221                bandwidth_hz: Some(250_000),
6222                spreading_factor: Some(10),
6223                coding_rate_denom: Some(6),
6224                duty_cycle_limit: Some(6_553),
6225            })
6226            .unwrap();
6227        assert_eq!(configured.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Configuring);
6228        assert_eq!(
6229            configured.outbound_frames.len(),
6230            usize::from(frame::TID_MAX)
6231        );
6232        let mut pending = VecDeque::from(configured.outbound_frames);
6233        let mut configured_properties = Vec::new();
6234        let save_tid = loop {
6235            let request = pending.pop_front().unwrap();
6236            let parsed = Frame::parse(&request).unwrap();
6237            if parsed.command() == Some(Cmd::Save) {
6238                break parsed.header.tid();
6239            }
6240            assert_eq!(parsed.command(), Some(Cmd::PropSet));
6241            let payload = PropPayload::parse(parsed.payload).unwrap();
6242            configured_properties.push(payload.key);
6243            // This radio tops out below the 20 dBm asked for and answers
6244            // with the power it will actually use. A `CMD_PROP_IS` is the
6245            // device's word on the property, so the session takes it.
6246            let answer: &[u8] = match payload.key {
6247                prop::PHY_TX_POWER => &[17],
6248                _ => payload.value,
6249            };
6250            let update = session
6251                .consume(property_response(parsed.header.tid(), payload.key, answer))
6252                .unwrap_or_else(|error| {
6253                    panic!(
6254                        "configuration response for property {} failed: {error:?}",
6255                        payload.key
6256                    )
6257                });
6258            pending.extend(update.outbound_frames);
6259        };
6260        assert_eq!(configured_properties.last(), Some(&prop::PHY_ENABLED));
6261        let attached = session
6262            .consume(property_response(save_tid, prop::LAST_STATUS, &[0]))
6263            .unwrap();
6264        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
6265        assert_eq!(
6266            attached.snapshot.device_name.as_deref(),
6267            Some("Trail radio")
6268        );
6269        let provisioning = attached.snapshot.provisioning.unwrap();
6270        assert_eq!(provisioning.frequency_khz, 868_100);
6271        // 20 dBm was written; the device said 17, so 17 is what the phone
6272        // shows.
6273        assert_eq!(provisioning.transmit_power_dbm, 17);
6274        assert_eq!(provisioning.bandwidth_hz, Some(250_000));
6275        assert_eq!(provisioning.spreading_factor, Some(10));
6276        assert_eq!(provisioning.coding_rate_denom, Some(6));
6277        assert_eq!(provisioning.duty_cycle_now, Some(65));
6278        assert_eq!(provisioning.duty_cycle_limit, Some(6_553));
6279
6280        let pushed = session
6281            .consume(property_response(
6282                frame::TID_UNSOLICITED,
6283                prop::PHY_DUTY_NOW,
6284                &131u16.to_le_bytes(),
6285            ))
6286            .unwrap();
6287        assert_eq!(
6288            pushed.snapshot.provisioning.unwrap().duty_cycle_now,
6289            Some(131)
6290        );
6291
6292        let refresh = session.refresh().unwrap();
6293        assert_eq!(refresh.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
6294        assert!(refresh.waiting_for_responses);
6295        let refresh_tail =
6296            answer_requests(
6297                &session,
6298                refresh.outbound_frames,
6299                |property| match property {
6300                    prop::DEV_NAME => (property, b"Fresh name".to_vec()),
6301                    prop::INTERFACE_TYPE => (property, vec![INTERFACE_TYPE as u8]),
6302                    prop::PHY_ENABLED => (property, vec![1]),
6303                    prop::PHY_FREQ => (property, 910_525u32.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
6304                    prop::PHY_TX_POWER => (property, vec![18]),
6305                    prop::PHY_LORA_BW => (property, 62_500u32.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
6306                    prop::PHY_LORA_SF => (property, vec![7]),
6307                    _ => unreachable!(),
6308                },
6309            );
6310        let refreshed =
6311            answer_requests(
6312                &session,
6313                refresh_tail.outbound_frames,
6314                |property| match property {
6315                    prop::PHY_LORA_CR => (property, vec![5]),
6316                    prop::PHY_DUTY_NOW => (property, 262u16.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
6317                    prop::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT => (property, 655u16.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
6318                    prop::SAVED => (property, vec![1]),
6319                    _ => unreachable!(),
6320                },
6321            );
6322        assert_eq!(refreshed.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
6323        assert!(!refreshed.waiting_for_responses);
6324        assert_eq!(
6325            refreshed.snapshot.device_name.as_deref(),
6326            Some("Fresh name")
6327        );
6328        let refreshed = refreshed.snapshot.provisioning.unwrap();
6329        assert_eq!(refreshed.frequency_khz, 910_525);
6330        assert_eq!(refreshed.duty_cycle_now, Some(262));
6331        assert_eq!(refreshed.duty_cycle_limit, Some(655));
6332    }
6333
6334    fn inserted_response(tid: u8, property: u32, item: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8> {
6335        let mut bytes = vec![0; MAX_FRAME];
6336        let length = frame::prop_inserted(&mut bytes, tid, property, item).unwrap();
6337        bytes.truncate(length);
6338        bytes
6339    }
6340
6341    fn removed_response(tid: u8, property: u32, item: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8> {
6342        let mut bytes = vec![0; MAX_FRAME];
6343        let length = frame::prop_removed(&mut bytes, tid, property, item).unwrap();
6344        bytes.truncate(length);
6345        bytes
6346    }

6348    fn dev_peer_keys(update: &UlcpSessionUpdateRecord) -> Vec<Vec<u8>> {
6349        update
6350            .snapshot
6351            .provisioning
6352            .as_ref()
6353            .unwrap()
6354            .dev_peer_keys
6355            .clone()
6356            .unwrap()
6357    }

6359    #[test]
6360    fn device_peer_insert_and_remove_patch_the_table_and_chain_a_save() {
6361        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
6362        let attached = attach_commissionable(&session, Some(vec![0xAA; 32]), vec![0xAA; 32]);
6363        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
6364        assert_eq!(dev_peer_keys(&attached), Vec::<Vec<u8>>::new());
6365
6366        let insert = session.insert_device_peer(vec![0xC1; 32]).unwrap();
6367        assert_eq!(insert.outbound_frames.len(), 1);
6368        let request = Frame::parse(&insert.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6369        assert_eq!(request.command(), Some(Cmd::PropInsert));
6370
6371        let confirmed = session
6372            .consume(inserted_response(
6373                request.header.tid(),
6374                prop::DEV_PEERS,
6375                &[0xC1; 32],
6376            ))
6377            .unwrap();
6378        assert_eq!(dev_peer_keys(&confirmed), vec![vec![0xC1; 32]]);
6379        assert_eq!(confirmed.operation_error, None);
6380        // The mutation is live but unsaved; CMD_SAVE rides behind it.
6381        assert!(confirmed.waiting_for_responses);
6382        assert_eq!(confirmed.outbound_frames.len(), 1);
6383        let save = Frame::parse(&confirmed.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6384        assert_eq!(save.command(), Some(Cmd::Save));
6385
6386        let saved = session
6387            .consume(property_response(
6388                save.header.tid(),
6389                prop::LAST_STATUS,
6390                &[umsh_ulcp::Status::OK.0 as u8],
6391            ))
6392            .unwrap();
6393        assert_eq!(saved.operation_error, None);
6394        assert!(!saved.waiting_for_responses);
6395        assert_eq!(saved.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
6396
6397        let remove = session.remove_device_peer(vec![0xC1; 32]).unwrap();
6398        let request = Frame::parse(&remove.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6399        assert_eq!(request.command(), Some(Cmd::PropRemove));
6400        let confirmed = session
6401            .consume(removed_response(
6402                request.header.tid(),
6403                prop::DEV_PEERS,
6404                &[0xC1; 32],
6405            ))
6406            .unwrap();
6407        assert_eq!(dev_peer_keys(&confirmed), Vec::<Vec<u8>>::new());
6408        let save = Frame::parse(&confirmed.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6409        assert_eq!(save.command(), Some(Cmd::Save));
6410        let saved = session
6411            .consume(property_response(
6412                save.header.tid(),
6413                prop::LAST_STATUS,
6414                &[umsh_ulcp::Status::OK.0 as u8],
6415            ))
6416            .unwrap();
6417        assert!(!saved.waiting_for_responses);
6418    }

6420    #[test]
6421    fn device_peer_failures_report_status_without_ending_the_session() {
6422        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
6423        attach_commissionable(&session, Some(vec![0xAA; 32]), vec![0xAA; 32]);
6424
6425        // NOMEM: the list is full. Nothing changed on the device, so the
6426        // cache stays put and no save is chained.
6427        let insert = session.insert_device_peer(vec![0xC2; 32]).unwrap();
6428        let request = Frame::parse(&insert.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6429        let full = session
6430            .consume(property_response(
6431                request.header.tid(),
6432                prop::LAST_STATUS,
6433                &[umsh_ulcp::Status::NOMEM.0 as u8],
6434            ))
6435            .unwrap();
6436        assert_eq!(
6437            full.operation_error,
6438            Some(UlcpOperationErrorRecord {
6439                operation: "insert device peer".into(),
6440                status_code: umsh_ulcp::Status::NOMEM.0,
6441                status_name: "Status::NOMEM".into(),
6442            })
6443        );
6444        assert_eq!(dev_peer_keys(&full), Vec::<Vec<u8>>::new());
6445        assert!(!full.waiting_for_responses);
6446        assert_eq!(full.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
6447
6448        // ALREADY: the key is on the device; the cache reflects that even
6449        // though the operation reports a non-OK status.
6450        let insert = session.insert_device_peer(vec![0xC3; 32]).unwrap();
6451        let request = Frame::parse(&insert.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6452        let already = session
6453            .consume(property_response(
6454                request.header.tid(),
6455                prop::LAST_STATUS,
6456                &[umsh_ulcp::Status::ALREADY.0 as u8],
6457            ))
6458            .unwrap();
6459        assert_eq!(
6460            already.operation_error.as_ref().unwrap().status_name,
6461            "Status::ALREADY"
6462        );
6463        assert_eq!(dev_peer_keys(&already), vec![vec![0xC3; 32]]);
6464
6465        // ITEM_NOT_FOUND on remove: the key is not on the device, which is
6466        // what the caller asked for.
6467        let remove = session.remove_device_peer(vec![0xC3; 32]).unwrap();
6468        let request = Frame::parse(&remove.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6469        let missing = session
6470            .consume(property_response(
6471                request.header.tid(),
6472                prop::LAST_STATUS,
6473                &[umsh_ulcp::Status::ITEM_NOT_FOUND.0 as u8],
6474            ))
6475            .unwrap();
6476        assert_eq!(
6477            missing.operation_error.as_ref().unwrap().status_name,
6478            "Status::ITEM_NOT_FOUND"
6479        );
6480        assert_eq!(dev_peer_keys(&missing), Vec::<Vec<u8>>::new());
6481
6482        // The session remains attached and usable.
6483        assert!(session.insert_device_peer(vec![0xC4; 32]).is_ok());
6484    }

6487        update
6488            .snapshot
6489            .provisioning
6490            .as_ref()
6491            .unwrap()
6492            .dev_admin_keys
6493            .clone()
6494            .unwrap()
6495    }

6497    #[test]
6498    fn listing_an_administrator_is_the_bench_half_of_node_management() {
6499        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
6500        let attached = attach_commissionable(&session, Some(vec![0xAA; 32]), vec![0xAA; 32]);
6501        assert!(
6502            attached
6503                .snapshot
6504                .provisioning
6505                .as_ref()
6506                .unwrap()
6507                .supports_admin
6508        );
6509        assert_eq!(dev_admin_keys(&attached), Vec::<Vec<u8>>::new());
6510
6511        // The phone's own node key, put on a radio it is holding so it can
6512        // manage that radio from somewhere else later.
6513        let phone = vec![0x11; 32];
6514        let insert = session.insert_device_admin(phone.clone()).unwrap();
6515        let request = Frame::parse(&insert.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6516        assert_eq!(request.command(), Some(Cmd::PropInsert));
6517
6518        let confirmed = session
6519            .consume(inserted_response(
6520                request.header.tid(),
6521                prop::DEV_ADMINS,
6522                &phone,
6523            ))
6524            .unwrap();
6525        assert_eq!(dev_admin_keys(&confirmed), vec![phone.clone()]);
6526        // Administrators are the one table that must survive a reboot to be
6527        // worth anything, so the save rides behind the mutation.
6528        let save = Frame::parse(&confirmed.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6529        assert_eq!(save.command(), Some(Cmd::Save));
6530        session
6531            .consume(property_response(
6532                save.header.tid(),
6533                prop::LAST_STATUS,
6534                &[umsh_ulcp::Status::OK.0 as u8],
6535            ))
6536            .unwrap();
6537
6538        // The peer table is a different list and is untouched by any of it.
6539        assert_eq!(dev_peer_keys(&confirmed), Vec::<Vec<u8>>::new());
6540
6541        let remove = session.remove_device_admin(phone.clone()).unwrap();
6542        let request = Frame::parse(&remove.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6543        assert_eq!(request.command(), Some(Cmd::PropRemove));
6544        let confirmed = session
6545            .consume(removed_response(
6546                request.header.tid(),
6547                prop::DEV_ADMINS,
6548                &phone,
6549            ))
6550            .unwrap();
6551        assert_eq!(dev_admin_keys(&confirmed), Vec::<Vec<u8>>::new());
6552    }

6554    /// An administrator on the mesh has no session to hand a configuration
6555    /// to, only the record its read produced. It must still write exactly
6556    /// what a phone holding the device would write, in the same order.
6557    #[test]
6558    fn a_configuration_reduces_the_same_way_with_or_without_a_session() {
6559        let configuration = UlcpDeviceConfigRecord {
6560            radio: UlcpRadioSettingsRecord {
6561                device_name: Some("Ridge repeater".into()),
6562                phy_enabled: true,
6563                frequency_khz: 906_875,
6564                transmit_power_dbm: 20,
6565                bandwidth_hz: None,
6566                spreading_factor: None,
6567                coding_rate_denom: None,
6568                duty_cycle_limit: None,
6569            },
6570            ident_role: None,
6571            ident_mobile: Some(false),
6572            dev_discoverable: Some(true),
6573            repeater: Some(UlcpRepeaterSettingsRecord {
6574                enabled: true,
6575                regions: Vec::new(),
6576                default_region: None,
6577                min_rssi_dbm: None,
6578                min_snr_db: None,
6579            }),
6580            tz_offset_min: None,
6581            gnss: None,
6582            advert: None,
6583        };
6584
6585        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
6586        let attached = attach_commissionable(&session, Some(vec![0xAA; 32]), vec![0xAA; 32]);
6587        let reported = attached.snapshot.provisioning.clone().unwrap();
6588
6589        let configured = session.configure_device(configuration.clone()).unwrap();
6590        let (written, order, _) = drive_configuration(&session, configured.outbound_frames);
6591
6592        let writes = device_config_writes(configuration, &reported).unwrap();
6593        assert_eq!(
6594            writes.iter().map(|(key, _)| *key).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
6595            order
6596        );
6597        for (key, value) in &writes {
6598            assert_eq!(written.get(key), Some(value));
6599        }
6600        // Not vacuous: the name leads, and the PHY is the last thing turned
6601        // on — a repeater must not start forwarding under half a policy.
6602        assert_eq!(writes.first().unwrap().0, prop::DEV_NAME);
6603        assert_eq!(writes.last().unwrap(), &(prop::PHY_ENABLED, vec![1]));
6604    }

6606    /// A device that would not report a property is a device that will
6607    /// refuse to be told it, whichever side is doing the telling.
6608    #[test]
6609    fn an_unreadable_property_is_left_out_of_an_administrator_s_write() {
6610        let mut reported = attach_commissionable(
6611            &MobileUlcpSession::new(),
6612            Some(vec![0xAA; 32]),
6613            vec![0xAA; 32],
6614        )
6615        .snapshot
6616        .provisioning
6617        .clone()
6618        .unwrap();
6619        reported.unreadable_properties = vec![prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE];
6620
6621        let configuration = UlcpDeviceConfigRecord {
6622            radio: UlcpRadioSettingsRecord {
6623                device_name: None,
6624                phy_enabled: true,
6625                frequency_khz: 906_875,
6626                transmit_power_dbm: 20,
6627                bandwidth_hz: None,
6628                spreading_factor: None,
6629                coding_rate_denom: None,
6630                duty_cycle_limit: None,
6631            },
6632            ident_role: None,
6633            ident_mobile: Some(false),
6634            dev_discoverable: Some(true),
6635            repeater: Some(UlcpRepeaterSettingsRecord {
6636                enabled: true,
6637                regions: Vec::new(),
6638                default_region: None,
6639                min_rssi_dbm: None,
6640                min_snr_db: None,
6641            }),
6642            tz_offset_min: None,
6643            gnss: None,
6644            advert: None,
6645        };
6646        // The record still states discoverability — the form does not know
6647        // which properties a device refuses — and the write does not.
6648        let writes = device_config_writes(configuration, &reported).unwrap();
6649        assert!(!writes.iter().any(|(key, _)| *key == prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE));
6650    }

6678    #[test]
6679    fn a_radio_that_cannot_be_managed_refuses_the_administrator_table() {
6680        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
6681        // CAP_ADMIN withheld: the list does not exist on this device, so
6682        // writing it is not something to try and fail at.
6683        let capabilities = vec![
6684            cap::HOST_FILTER,
6685            cap::SAVE,
6686            cap::DEV_NAME,
6687            cap::DEV_IDENTITY,
6688        ];
6689        let sync = inspect_ulcp_sync(vec![
6690            response(prop::CAPS, &encoded_capabilities(&capabilities)),
6691            response(prop::INTERFACE_TYPE, &[INTERFACE_TYPE as u8]),
6692            response(prop::PHY_ENABLED, &[1]),
6693            response(prop::PHY_FREQ, &915_000u32.to_le_bytes()),
6694            response(prop::PHY_TX_POWER, &[14]),
6695            response(prop::HOST_RX_FILTERS, &[]),
6696            response(prop::SAVED, &[saved::CURRENT]),
6697            response(prop::DEV_PEERS, &[]),
6698            response(prop::DEV_CHANNEL_KEYS, &[]),
6699            response(prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE, &[1]),
6700        ])
6701        .unwrap();
6702        assert!(!sync.supports_admin);
6703        assert_eq!(sync.dev_admin_keys, None);
6704        assert!(!sync.unreadable_properties.contains(&prop::DEV_ADMINS));
6705        assert!(
6706            !ulcp_inspection_properties(encoded_capabilities(&capabilities))
6707                .unwrap()
6708                .contains(&prop::DEV_ADMINS)
6709        );
6710
6711        assert!(session.insert_device_admin(vec![0x33; 32]).is_err());
6712    }

6733    #[test]
6734    fn device_channel_insert_and_remove_track_identifiers_not_keys() {
6735        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
6736        let attached = attach_commissionable(&session, Some(vec![0xAA; 32]), vec![0xAA; 32]);
6737        assert_eq!(dev_channel_ids(&attached), Vec::<Vec<u8>>::new());
6738
6739        // The wire carries the key; the device answers with the derived
6740        // identifier, because a channel key is never read back.
6741        let key = vec![0xB7; 32];
6742        let id = crate::derive_channel_id(key.clone()).unwrap();
6743        assert_eq!(id.len(), items::CHANNEL_ID_LEN);
6744
6745        let insert = session.insert_device_channel_key(key.clone()).unwrap();
6746        let request = Frame::parse(&insert.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6747        assert_eq!(request.command(), Some(Cmd::PropInsert));
6748
6749        let confirmed = session
6750            .consume(inserted_response(
6751                request.header.tid(),
6752                prop::DEV_CHANNEL_KEYS,
6753                &id,
6754            ))
6755            .unwrap();
6756        assert_eq!(dev_channel_ids(&confirmed), vec![id.clone()]);
6757        assert_eq!(confirmed.operation_error, None);
6758        let save = Frame::parse(&confirmed.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6759        assert_eq!(save.command(), Some(Cmd::Save));
6760        let saved = session
6761            .consume(property_response(
6762                save.header.tid(),
6763                prop::LAST_STATUS,
6764                &[umsh_ulcp::Status::OK.0 as u8],
6765            ))
6766            .unwrap();
6767        assert!(!saved.waiting_for_responses);
6768
6769        // Removal selects by key and is likewise confirmed by identifier.
6770        let remove = session.remove_device_channel_key(key).unwrap();
6771        let request = Frame::parse(&remove.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6772        assert_eq!(request.command(), Some(Cmd::PropRemove));
6773        let confirmed = session
6774            .consume(removed_response(
6775                request.header.tid(),
6776                prop::DEV_CHANNEL_KEYS,
6777                &id,
6778            ))
6779            .unwrap();
6780        assert_eq!(dev_channel_ids(&confirmed), Vec::<Vec<u8>>::new());
6781    }

6783    #[test]
6784    fn device_channel_failures_report_status_without_ending_the_session() {
6785        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
6786        attach_commissionable(&session, Some(vec![0xAA; 32]), vec![0xAA; 32]);
6787
6788        let key = vec![0xC7; 32];
6789        let id = crate::derive_channel_id(key.clone()).unwrap();
6790
6791        let insert = session.insert_device_channel_key(key.clone()).unwrap();
6792        let request = Frame::parse(&insert.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6793        let full = session
6794            .consume(property_response(
6795                request.header.tid(),
6796                prop::LAST_STATUS,
6797                &[umsh_ulcp::Status::NOMEM.0 as u8],
6798            ))
6799            .unwrap();
6800        assert_eq!(
6801            full.operation_error,
6802            Some(UlcpOperationErrorRecord {
6803                operation: "insert device channel key".into(),
6804                status_code: umsh_ulcp::Status::NOMEM.0,
6805                status_name: "Status::NOMEM".into(),
6806            })
6807        );
6808        assert_eq!(dev_channel_ids(&full), Vec::<Vec<u8>>::new());
6809        assert_eq!(full.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
6810
6811        // ALREADY means the device holds it, so the cache says so too.
6812        let insert = session.insert_device_channel_key(key.clone()).unwrap();
6813        let request = Frame::parse(&insert.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6814        let already = session
6815            .consume(property_response(
6816                request.header.tid(),
6817                prop::LAST_STATUS,
6818                &[umsh_ulcp::Status::ALREADY.0 as u8],
6819            ))
6820            .unwrap();
6821        assert_eq!(dev_channel_ids(&already), vec![id]);
6822
6823        let remove = session.remove_device_channel_key(key).unwrap();
6824        let request = Frame::parse(&remove.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6825        let missing = session
6826            .consume(property_response(
6827                request.header.tid(),
6828                prop::LAST_STATUS,
6829                &[umsh_ulcp::Status::ITEM_NOT_FOUND.0 as u8],
6830            ))
6831            .unwrap();
6832        assert_eq!(
6833            missing.operation_error.as_ref().unwrap().status_name,
6834            "Status::ITEM_NOT_FOUND"
6835        );
6836        assert_eq!(dev_channel_ids(&missing), Vec::<Vec<u8>>::new());
6837
6838        assert!(session.insert_device_channel_key(vec![0xC8; 32]).is_ok());
6839    }

6841    fn host_channel_count(update: &UlcpSessionUpdateRecord) -> Option<u32> {
6842        update
6843            .snapshot
6844            .provisioning
6845            .as_ref()
6846            .unwrap()
6847            .host_channel_count
6848    }

6850    #[test]
6851    fn host_channel_reconcile_inserts_what_the_radio_is_missing() {
6852        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
6853        let attached = attach_host_keys_capable(&session);
6854        assert_eq!(host_channel_count(&attached), Some(0));
6855
6856        let first = vec![0xD1; 32];
6857        let second = vec![0xD2; 32];
6858        let update = session
6859            .reconcile_host_channel_keys(vec![first.clone(), second.clone()])
6860            .unwrap();
6861
6862        // One insert at a time; the next rides on the previous confirmation.
6863        assert_eq!(update.outbound_frames.len(), 1);
6864        let request = Frame::parse(&update.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6865        assert_eq!(request.command(), Some(Cmd::PropInsert));
6866        let confirmed = session
6867            .consume(inserted_response(
6868                request.header.tid(),
6869                prop::HOST_CHANNEL_KEYS,
6870                &crate::derive_channel_id(first).unwrap(),
6871            ))
6872            .unwrap();
6873        assert_eq!(confirmed.outbound_frames.len(), 1);
6874        let request = Frame::parse(&confirmed.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6875        let done = session
6876            .consume(inserted_response(
6877                request.header.tid(),
6878                prop::HOST_CHANNEL_KEYS,
6879                &crate::derive_channel_id(second).unwrap(),
6880            ))
6881            .unwrap();
6882        assert!(done.outbound_frames.is_empty());
6883        assert!(!done.waiting_for_responses);
6884        assert_eq!(done.operation_error, None);
6885        assert_eq!(host_channel_count(&done), Some(2));
6886    }

6888    #[test]
6889    fn host_channel_reconcile_is_a_no_op_when_the_radio_already_matches() {
6890        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
6891        attach_host_keys_capable(&session);
6892        let key = vec![0xD3; 32];
6893
6894        let update = session
6895            .reconcile_host_channel_keys(vec![key.clone()])
6896            .unwrap();
6897        let request = Frame::parse(&update.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6898        session
6899            .consume(inserted_response(
6900                request.header.tid(),
6901                prop::HOST_CHANNEL_KEYS,
6902                &crate::derive_channel_id(key.clone()).unwrap(),
6903            ))
6904            .unwrap();
6905
6906        // Reconciling the same set again asks the radio for nothing, which is
6907        // what makes reconnecting cheap.
6908        let again = session.reconcile_host_channel_keys(vec![key]).unwrap();
6909        assert!(again.outbound_frames.is_empty());
6910        assert!(!again.waiting_for_responses);
6911    }

6914    fn host_channel_reconcile_replaces_the_table_to_shed_an_unknown_channel() {
6915        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
6916        attach_host_keys_capable(&session);
6917        let stranger = vec![0xD4; 32];
6918
6919        // Something else provisioned a channel this phone has no key for.
6920        let update = session
6921            .reconcile_host_channel_keys(vec![stranger.clone()])
6922            .unwrap();
6923        let request = Frame::parse(&update.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6924        session
6925            .consume(inserted_response(
6926                request.header.tid(),
6927                prop::HOST_CHANNEL_KEYS,
6928                &crate::derive_channel_id(stranger).unwrap(),
6929            ))
6930            .unwrap();
6931
6932        // Removal selects by key, so an unnameable entry forces a whole-table
6933        // write rather than a remove.
6934        let mine = vec![0xD5; 32];
6935        let replace = session
6936            .reconcile_host_channel_keys(vec![mine.clone()])
6937            .unwrap();
6938        let request = Frame::parse(&replace.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6939        assert_eq!(request.command(), Some(Cmd::PropSet));
6940
6941        let done = session
6942            .consume(property_response(
6943                request.header.tid(),
6944                prop::HOST_CHANNEL_KEYS,
6945                &crate::derive_channel_id(mine).unwrap(),
6946            ))
6947            .unwrap();
6948        assert_eq!(host_channel_count(&done), Some(1));
6949        assert!(!done.waiting_for_responses);
6950    }

6952    #[test]
6953    fn a_full_host_channel_table_reports_status_and_stays_attached() {
6954        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
6955        attach_host_keys_capable(&session);
6956
6957        let update = session
6958            .reconcile_host_channel_keys(vec![vec![0xD6; 32], vec![0xD7; 32]])
6959            .unwrap();
6960        let request = Frame::parse(&update.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
6961        let full = session
6962            .consume(property_response(
6963                request.header.tid(),
6964                prop::LAST_STATUS,
6965                &[umsh_ulcp::Status::NOMEM.0 as u8],
6966            ))
6967            .unwrap();
6968
6969        assert_eq!(
6970            full.operation_error.as_ref().unwrap().status_name,
6971            "Status::NOMEM"
6972        );
6973        // The pass stops rather than hammering a table it cannot fit, and the
6974        // session stays usable.
6975        assert!(full.outbound_frames.is_empty());
6976        assert!(!full.waiting_for_responses);
6977        assert_eq!(full.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
6978    }

7014    #[test]
7015    fn device_peer_operations_require_the_device_identity_capability() {
7016        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
7017        let begin = session.begin(None).unwrap();
7018        let inspection =
7019            answer_requests(&session, begin.outbound_frames, |property| match property {
7020                prop::LAST_STATUS => (property, vec![0]),
7021                prop::PROTOCOL_VERSION => (property, vec![6, 0]),
7022                prop::CAPS => (property, vec![cap::WRITABLE_RAW_STREAM as u8]),
7023                prop::DEV_KEY | prop::DEV_NAME | prop::BATTERY => (property, Vec::new()),
7024                prop::HOST_KEY => (prop::LAST_STATUS, vec![2]),
7025                _ => unreachable!(),
7026            });
7027        let attached =
7028            answer_requests(
7029                &session,
7030                inspection.outbound_frames,
7031                |property| match property {
7032                    prop::INTERFACE_TYPE => (property, vec![INTERFACE_TYPE as u8]),
7033                    prop::PHY_ENABLED => (property, vec![1]),
7034                    prop::PHY_FREQ => (property, 915_000u32.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
7035                    prop::PHY_TX_POWER => (property, vec![14]),
7036                    _ => unreachable!(),
7037                },
7038            );
7039        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
7040        assert_eq!(
7041            session.insert_device_peer(vec![0xC1; 32]).unwrap_err(),
7042            MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame
7043        );
7044        assert_eq!(
7045            session.remove_device_peer(vec![0xC1; 32]).unwrap_err(),
7046            MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame
7047        );
7048        // And a malformed key is rejected before any frame is built.
7049        assert_eq!(
7050            session.insert_device_peer(vec![0xC1; 31]).unwrap_err(),
7051            MobileError::InvalidPublicKeyLength
7052        );
7053    }
