7055    /// Attach a battery-reporting device and return the session sitting in
7056    /// the attached phase.
7057    fn attached_battery_session() -> std::sync::Arc<MobileUlcpSession> {
7058        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
7059        let begin = session.begin(None).unwrap();
7060        let inspection =
7061            answer_requests(&session, begin.outbound_frames, |property| match property {
7062                prop::LAST_STATUS => (property, vec![0]),
7063                prop::PROTOCOL_VERSION => (property, vec![6, 0]),
7064                prop::CAPS => (property, encoded_capabilities(&[cap::BATTERY])),
7065                // Voltage + level + charge state, discharging at 60 %.
7066                prop::BATTERY => (property, vec![0b111, 0x74, 0x0E, 60, 0]),
7067                prop::DEV_KEY | prop::DEV_NAME => (property, Vec::new()),
7068                prop::HOST_KEY => (prop::LAST_STATUS, vec![2]),
7069                _ => unreachable!(),
7070            });
7071        let attached =
7072            answer_requests(
7073                &session,
7074                inspection.outbound_frames,
7075                |property| match property {
7076                    prop::INTERFACE_TYPE => (property, vec![INTERFACE_TYPE as u8]),
7077                    prop::PHY_ENABLED => (property, vec![1]),
7078                    prop::PHY_FREQ => (property, 915_000u32.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
7079                    prop::PHY_TX_POWER => (property, vec![14]),
7080                    _ => unreachable!(),
7081                },
7082            );
7083        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
7084        session
7085    }
7086
7087    #[test]
7088    fn battery_is_reported_once_per_measurement_not_on_every_update() {
7089        let session = attached_battery_session();
7090
7091        // An unsolicited snapshot is carried by the update that receives
7092        // it: 45 %, now charging.
7093        let pushed = session
7094            .consume(property_response(
7095                frame::TID_UNSOLICITED,
7096                prop::BATTERY,
7097                &[0b111, 0x10, 0x10, 45, 1],
7098            ))
7099            .unwrap();
7100        let battery = pushed.snapshot.battery.expect("push carries the snapshot");
7101        assert_eq!(battery.percentage, Some(45));
7102        assert_eq!(battery.voltage_mv, Some(0x1010));
7103        assert_eq!(battery.charge_state, Some(UlcpChargeState::Charging));
7104
7105        // A later update that carries no measurement must not repeat it.
7106        // Consumers timestamp what they receive, so a repeat would report
7107        // a stale reading as a fresh one.
7108        let unrelated = session
7109            .consume(property_response(
7110                frame::TID_UNSOLICITED,
7111                prop::DEV_NAME,
7112                b"Ridge repeater",
7113            ))
7114            .unwrap();
7115        assert!(unrelated.snapshot.battery.is_none());
7116        assert_eq!(
7117            unrelated.snapshot.device_name.as_deref(),
7118            Some("Ridge repeater"),
7119            "unrelated state still propagates"
7120        );
7121
7122        // The next measurement is reported again.
7123        let again = session
7124            .consume(property_response(
7125                frame::TID_UNSOLICITED,
7126                prop::BATTERY,
7127                &[0b111, 0x20, 0x10, 50, 1],
7128            ))
7129            .unwrap();
7130        assert_eq!(
7131            again.snapshot.battery.expect("second push").percentage,
7132            Some(50)
7133        );
7134    }

7136    /// Attach an alert-capable device sitting in the attached phase.
7137    fn attached_alert_session() -> std::sync::Arc<MobileUlcpSession> {
7138        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
7139        let begin = session.begin(None).unwrap();
7140        let inspection =
7141            answer_requests(&session, begin.outbound_frames, |property| match property {
7142                prop::LAST_STATUS => (property, vec![0]),
7143                prop::PROTOCOL_VERSION => (property, vec![6, 0]),
7144                prop::CAPS => (property, encoded_capabilities(&[cap::ALERT])),
7145                prop::DEV_KEY | prop::DEV_NAME | prop::BATTERY => (property, Vec::new()),
7146                prop::HOST_KEY => (prop::LAST_STATUS, vec![2]),
7147                _ => unreachable!(),
7148            });
7149        let attached =
7150            answer_requests(
7151                &session,
7152                inspection.outbound_frames,
7153                |property| match property {
7154                    prop::INTERFACE_TYPE => (property, vec![INTERFACE_TYPE as u8]),
7155                    prop::PHY_ENABLED => (property, vec![1]),
7156                    prop::PHY_FREQ => (property, 915_000u32.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
7157                    prop::PHY_TX_POWER => (property, vec![14]),
7158                    prop::ALERT => (property, vec![0]),
7159                    _ => unreachable!(),
7160                },
7161            );
7162        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
7163        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.alert, Some(UlcpAlertState::None));
7164        session
7165    }

7167    #[test]
7168    fn alert_state_follows_the_radio_not_the_request() {
7169        let session = attached_alert_session();
7170
7171        let request = session.set_alert(UlcpAlertState::Locate).unwrap();
7172        let [frame] = &request.outbound_frames[..] else {
7173            panic!("one CMD_PROP_SET");
7174        };
7175        let parsed = Frame::parse(frame).unwrap();
7176        assert_eq!(parsed.command(), Some(Cmd::PropSet));
7177        let payload = PropPayload::parse(parsed.payload).unwrap();
7178        assert_eq!((payload.key, payload.value), (prop::ALERT, &[1u8][..]));
7179
7180        let started = session
7181            .consume(property_response(parsed.header.tid(), prop::ALERT, &[1]))
7182            .unwrap();
7183        assert_eq!(started.snapshot.alert, Some(UlcpAlertState::Locate));
7184
7185        // Unlike battery, the state persists across unrelated updates —
7186        // the UI mirrors it rather than reacting to it once.
7187        let unrelated = session
7188            .consume(property_response(
7189                frame::TID_UNSOLICITED,
7190                prop::DEV_NAME,
7191                b"Ridge repeater",
7192            ))
7193            .unwrap();
7194        assert_eq!(unrelated.snapshot.alert, Some(UlcpAlertState::Locate));
7195
7196        // Someone presses the button on the radio (or its deadline
7197        // expires): the unsolicited update is what the phone believes.
7198        let cancelled = session
7199            .consume(property_response(frame::TID_UNSOLICITED, prop::ALERT, &[0]))
7200            .unwrap();
7201        assert_eq!(cancelled.snapshot.alert, Some(UlcpAlertState::None));
7202        assert_eq!(cancelled.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
7203    }

7224    #[test]
7225    fn malformed_alert_values_are_rejected() {
7226        assert_eq!(inspect_ulcp_alert(vec![0]).unwrap(), UlcpAlertState::None);
7227        assert_eq!(inspect_ulcp_alert(vec![1]).unwrap(), UlcpAlertState::Locate);
7228        // Unknown state, trailing bytes, and the empty value.
7229        assert!(inspect_ulcp_alert(vec![2]).is_err());
7230        assert!(inspect_ulcp_alert(vec![1, 0]).is_err());
7231        assert!(inspect_ulcp_alert(Vec::new()).is_err());

7234    /// A commissionable device that also keeps a clock and has a receiver
7235    /// holding a three-dimensional fix.
7236    fn attach_positioning(session: &MobileUlcpSession) -> UlcpSessionUpdateRecord {
7237        let mut capabilities = commissionable_capabilities();
7238        capabilities.extend([cap::TIME, cap::GNSS]);
7239        let begin = session.begin(Some(vec![0xAA; 32])).unwrap();
7240        drive_reads(
7241            session,
7242            begin.outbound_frames,
7243            move |property| match property {
7244                prop::CAPS => (property, encoded_capabilities(&capabilities)),
7245                prop::HOST_KEY => (property, vec![0xAA; 32]),
7246                prop::TIME => (property, 1_754_000_000u32.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
7247                // Pacific daylight time, which is an offset and not a zone
7248                // — the device has no database to shift itself with.
7249                prop::TZ_OFFSET => (property, (-420i16).to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
7250                prop::GNSS_ENABLED | prop::GNSS_IDENT_UPDATE | prop::GNSS_TIME_TRUST => {
7251                    (property, vec![1])
7252                }
7253                prop::GNSS_LOCATION => (property, placed_location().as_bytes().to_vec()),
7254                prop::GNSS_ALTITUDE => (property, 71i32.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
7255                prop::GNSS_FIX => (property, vec![2]),
7256                prop::GNSS_PRECISION => (property, 62u16.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
7257                prop::GNSS_SATELLITES => (property, vec![9, 14]),
7258                prop::GNSS_IDENT_PRECISION => (property, vec![5]),
7259                _ => commissionable_value(property),
7260            },
7261        )
7262    }
7263
7264    fn attach_advertising(session: &MobileUlcpSession) -> UlcpSessionUpdateRecord {
7265        let mut capabilities = commissionable_capabilities();
7266        capabilities.push(cap::ADVERT);
7267        let begin = session.begin(Some(vec![0xAA; 32])).unwrap();
7268        drive_reads(
7269            session,
7270            begin.outbound_frames,
7271            move |property| match property {
7272                prop::CAPS => (property, encoded_capabilities(&capabilities)),
7273                prop::HOST_KEY => (property, vec![0xAA; 32]),
7274                prop::ADVERT_INTERVAL => (property, 14_400u32.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
7275                prop::BEACON_INTERVAL => (property, 3_600u32.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
7276                prop::STARTUP_BEACON => (property, vec![1]),
7277                _ => commissionable_value(property),
7278            },
7279        )
7280    }

7299    /// A commissioning phone has no way to ask for one property on its
7300    /// own, so the position the device advertises has to arrive with the
7301    /// attach snapshot — it is where a region proposal starts from.
7302    #[test]
7303    fn the_advertised_position_folds_into_the_sync_record() {
7304        let cell = NodeLocation::from_lat_lon(37.5119, -122.2495, 4);
7305        let session = MobileUlcpSession::administrative();
7306        let begin = session.begin(Some(vec![0xAA; 32])).unwrap();
7307        let bytes = cell.as_bytes().to_vec();
7308        let update = drive_reads(
7309            &session,
7310            begin.outbound_frames,
7311            move |property| match property {
7312                prop::HOST_KEY => (property, vec![0xAA; 32]),
7313                prop::IDENT_LOCATION => (property, bytes.clone()),
7314                prop::IDENT_ALTITUDE => (property, vec![0x64]),
7315                _ => commissionable_value(property),
7316            },
7317        );
7318        let sync = update.snapshot.provisioning.expect("device described");
7319        let position = sync.ident_position.expect("position reported");
7320
7321        assert_eq!(position.location, cell.as_bytes());
7322        assert!((position.latitude_deg.unwrap() - 37.5119).abs() < 0.01);
7323        assert!((position.longitude_deg.unwrap() + 122.2495).abs() < 0.01);
7324        assert!((610.0..613.0).contains(&position.cell_meters.unwrap()));
7325        assert_eq!(position.altitude_m, Some(100));
7326    }

7382    /// Catching the bounds here means an out-of-range interval fails
7383    /// before any of the group is written, rather than half-changing the
7384    /// schedule.
7385    #[test]
7386    fn an_advertisement_record_must_match_what_the_device_can_do() {
7387        let session = MobileUlcpSession::administrative();
7388        attach_advertising(&session);
7389        let whole = UlcpAdvertSettingsRecord {
7390            advert_interval_seconds: 14_400,
7391            beacon_interval_seconds: 3_600,
7392            startup_beacon: true,
7393        };
7394
7395        // Absent on a device that advertises the capability.
7396        assert_eq!(
7397            session.configure_advertising(None),
7398            Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
7399        );
7400        for out_of_range in [
7401            MIN_AUTO_ANNOUNCE_INTERVAL_S - 1,
7402            MAX_AUTO_ANNOUNCE_INTERVAL_S + 1,
7403        ] {
7404            assert_eq!(
7405                session.configure_advertising(Some(UlcpAdvertSettingsRecord {
7406                    beacon_interval_seconds: out_of_range,
7407                    ..whole
7408                })),
7409                Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
7410            );
7411        }
7412        // Zero is the off switch, not a too-short interval.
7413        assert!(
7414            session
7415                .configure_advertising(Some(UlcpAdvertSettingsRecord {
7416                    beacon_interval_seconds: 0,
7417                    ..whole
7418                }))
7419                .is_ok()
7420        );
7421    }

7439    /// A five-byte fix — a ~38 m cell, the default identity precision.
7440    fn placed_location() -> NodeLocation {
7441        NodeLocation::from_e7(377_749_290, -1_224_194_160, 5)
7442    }

7444    #[test]
7445    fn a_positioning_device_reports_its_fix_and_its_policy() {
7446        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
7447        let attached = attach_positioning(&session);
7448        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
7449
7450        let sync = attached.snapshot.provisioning.clone().expect("described");
7451        assert!(sync.supports_time && sync.supports_gnss);
7452        assert_eq!(sync.tz_offset_min, Some(-420));
7453        assert_eq!(
7454            sync.gnss,
7455            Some(UlcpGnssSettingsRecord {
7456                enabled: true,
7457                ident_update: true,
7458                ident_precision: 5,
7459                time_trust: true,
7460            })
7461        );
7462        assert!(sync.unreadable_properties.is_empty());
7463
7464        let gnss = attached.snapshot.gnss.expect("a receiver was read");
7465        assert_eq!(gnss.fix, UlcpFixKind::ThreeD);
7466        assert_eq!(gnss.altitude_m, Some(71));
7467        assert_eq!(gnss.accuracy_dm, Some(62));
7468        assert_eq!(
7469            (gnss.satellites_used, gnss.satellites_in_view),
7470            (9, Some(14))
7471        );
7472        assert_eq!(gnss.location, placed_location().as_bytes());
7473        // The center of the cell the fix named, which is as close to the
7474        // encoded position as a cell that size can be.
7475        let (latitude, longitude) = (gnss.latitude_deg.unwrap(), gnss.longitude_deg.unwrap());
7476        assert!((latitude - 37.774_929).abs() < 5e-4, "{latitude}");
7477        assert!((longitude + 122.419_416).abs() < 5e-4, "{longitude}");
7478        assert!((38.0..39.0).contains(&gnss.location_cell_meters.unwrap()));
7479    }

7481    #[test]
7482    fn the_clock_is_reported_once_and_the_fix_is_mirrored() {
7483        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
7484        attach_positioning(&session);
7485
7486        // Unrelated news must not restamp the clock read at attach as a
7487        // fresh one, but it must not lose the position either: the pin
7488        // stays on the map, the clock does not get a new timestamp.
7489        let unrelated = session
7490            .consume(property_response(
7491                frame::TID_UNSOLICITED,
7492                prop::DEV_NAME,
7493                b"Ridge repeater",
7494            ))
7495            .unwrap();
7496        assert_eq!(unrelated.snapshot.time, None);
7497        assert_eq!(
7498            unrelated.snapshot.gnss.expect("still known").fix,
7499            UlcpFixKind::ThreeD
7500        );
7501
7502        // An announced fix change folds into what is already known rather
7503        // than replacing it: the satellite count came a frame earlier and
7504        // is still true.
7505        let lost = session
7506            .consume(property_response(
7507                frame::TID_UNSOLICITED,
7508                prop::GNSS_FIX,
7509                &[1],
7510            ))
7511            .unwrap();
7512        let gnss = lost.snapshot.gnss.expect("still known");
7513        assert_eq!(gnss.fix, UlcpFixKind::TwoD);
7514        assert_eq!(gnss.satellites_used, 9);
7515        assert_eq!(gnss.altitude_m, Some(71));
7516
7517        // The device finding the time on its own is announced, and is
7518        // reported once like any other reading.
7519        let stepped = session
7520            .consume(property_response(
7521                frame::TID_UNSOLICITED,
7522                prop::TIME,
7523                &1_754_000_600u32.to_le_bytes(),
7524            ))
7525            .unwrap();
7526        assert_eq!(
7527            stepped.snapshot.time,
7528            Some(UlcpTimeRecord {
7529                epoch_seconds: Some(1_754_000_600)
7530            })
7531        );
7532        assert_eq!(stepped.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
7533    }
7534
7535    #[test]
7536    fn sampling_a_position_asks_for_the_position_and_nothing_else() {
7537        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
7538        attach_positioning(&session);
7539
7540        // The device never announces where it is — a receiver reports
7541        // about a fix a second and noise moves the reading, so a host that
7542        // wants a position asks for one.
7543        let poll = session.refresh_positioning().unwrap();
7544        let asked: Vec<u32> = poll
7545            .outbound_frames
7546            .iter()
7547            .map(|frame| {
7548                let parsed = Frame::parse(frame).unwrap();
7549                PropPayload::parse(parsed.payload).unwrap().key
7550            })
7551            .collect();
7552        assert_eq!(
7553            asked,
7554            vec![
7555                prop::GNSS_LOCATION,
7556                prop::GNSS_ALTITUDE,
7557                prop::GNSS_FIX,
7558                prop::GNSS_PRECISION,
7559                prop::GNSS_SATELLITES,
7560            ]
7561        );
7562
7563        // Narrower than a full refresh, which is the point: a screen
7564        // watching a position must not re-read the radio's whole
7565        // configuration once a minute to do it.
7566        assert!(!asked.contains(&prop::PHY_FREQ));
7567        assert!(!asked.contains(&prop::DEV_NAME));
7568
7569        // The answers land in the same snapshot field the announcements
7570        // used to fill, so nothing downstream can tell the two apart.
7571        let sampled = answer_requests(&session, poll.outbound_frames, |property| match property {
7572            prop::GNSS_LOCATION => (property, placed_location().as_bytes().to_vec()),
7573            prop::GNSS_ALTITUDE => (property, 88i32.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
7574            prop::GNSS_FIX => (property, vec![2]),
7575            prop::GNSS_PRECISION => (property, 40u16.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
7576            prop::GNSS_SATELLITES => (property, vec![11, 15]),
7577            _ => unreachable!("{property}"),
7578        });
7579        let gnss = sampled.snapshot.gnss.expect("a receiver was sampled");
7580        assert_eq!(gnss.altitude_m, Some(88));
7581        assert_eq!(gnss.satellites_used, 11);
7582        assert_eq!(sampled.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
7583    }

7596    #[test]
7597    fn setting_the_clock_writes_the_epoch_and_clearing_it_writes_nothing() {
7598        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
7599        attach_positioning(&session);
7600
7601        let request = session.set_time(Some(1_754_000_900)).unwrap();
7602        let [frame] = &request.outbound_frames[..] else {
7603            panic!("one CMD_PROP_SET");
7604        };
7605        let parsed = Frame::parse(frame).unwrap();
7606        let payload = PropPayload::parse(parsed.payload).unwrap();
7607        assert_eq!(payload.key, prop::TIME);
7608        assert_eq!(payload.value, &1_754_000_900u32.to_le_bytes()[..]);
7609
7610        // What the device answers is what the snapshot reports, even when
7611        // it is not what was written — a trusted receiver may have moved
7612        // the clock between the write and the echo.
7613        let set = session
7614            .consume(property_response(
7615                parsed.header.tid(),
7616                prop::TIME,
7617                &1_754_000_901u32.to_le_bytes(),
7618            ))
7619            .unwrap();
7620        assert_eq!(
7621            set.snapshot.time,
7622            Some(UlcpTimeRecord {
7623                epoch_seconds: Some(1_754_000_901)
7624            })
7625        );
7626
7627        // The empty value is how a clock goes back to unknown.
7628        let clearing = session.set_time(None).unwrap();
7629        let parsed = Frame::parse(&clearing.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
7630        let payload = PropPayload::parse(parsed.payload).unwrap();
7631        assert_eq!((payload.key, payload.value), (prop::TIME, &[][..]));
7632        let cleared = session
7633            .consume(property_response(parsed.header.tid(), prop::TIME, &[]))
7634            .unwrap();
7635        assert_eq!(
7636            cleared.snapshot.time,
7637            Some(UlcpTimeRecord {
7638                epoch_seconds: None
7639            })
7640        );
7641    }

7661    #[test]
7662    fn positioning_settings_are_written_whole_with_the_receiver_last() {
7663        let session = MobileUlcpSession::administrative();
7664        attach_positioning(&session);
7665
7666        let configured = session
7667            .configure_device(UlcpDeviceConfigRecord {
7668                radio: UlcpRadioSettingsRecord {
7669                    device_name: None,
7670                    phy_enabled: true,
7671                    frequency_khz: 915_000,
7672                    transmit_power_dbm: 14,
7673                    bandwidth_hz: None,
7674                    spreading_factor: None,
7675                    coding_rate_denom: None,
7676                    duty_cycle_limit: None,
7677                },
7678                ident_role: None,
7679                ident_mobile: Some(false),
7680                dev_discoverable: Some(true),
7681                repeater: Some(UlcpRepeaterSettingsRecord {
7682                    enabled: false,
7683                    regions: Vec::new(),
7684                    default_region: None,
7685                    min_rssi_dbm: None,
7686                    min_snr_db: None,
7687                }),
7688                tz_offset_min: Some(60),
7689                gnss: Some(UlcpGnssSettingsRecord {
7690                    enabled: true,
7691                    ident_update: false,
7692                    ident_precision: 3,
7693                    time_trust: false,
7694                }),
7695                advert: None,
7696            })
7697            .unwrap();
7698        let (written, order, _) = drive_configuration(&session, configured.outbound_frames);
7699        assert_eq!(
7700            written.get(&prop::TZ_OFFSET),
7701            Some(&60i16.to_le_bytes().to_vec())
7702        );
7703        assert_eq!(written.get(&prop::GNSS_IDENT_UPDATE), Some(&vec![0]));
7704        assert_eq!(written.get(&prop::GNSS_IDENT_PRECISION), Some(&vec![3]));
7705        assert_eq!(written.get(&prop::GNSS_TIME_TRUST), Some(&vec![0]));
7706        assert_eq!(written.get(&prop::GNSS_ENABLED), Some(&vec![1]));
7707
7708        // The receiver starts under the disclosure and trust policy just
7709        // written, never the one it happened to be holding.
7710        let switch = order.iter().position(|key| *key == prop::GNSS_ENABLED);
7711        for policy in [
7712            prop::GNSS_IDENT_UPDATE,
7713            prop::GNSS_IDENT_PRECISION,
7714            prop::GNSS_TIME_TRUST,
7715        ] {
7716            assert!(
7717                order.iter().position(|key| *key == policy) < switch,
7718                "{policy}"
7719            );
7720        }
7721    }

7723    #[test]
7724    fn a_tethered_phone_changes_positioning_without_restating_the_domain() {
7725        // The companion case: switching a receiver on must not require
7726        // saying anything about the radio's role or what it forwards.
7727        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
7728        attach_positioning(&session);
7729
7730        let configured = session
7731            .configure_positioning(
7732                Some(UlcpGnssSettingsRecord {
7733                    enabled: false,
7734                    ident_update: false,
7735                    ident_precision: 3,
7736                    time_trust: false,
7737                }),
7738                Some(0),
7739            )
7740            .unwrap();
7741        let (written, order, save_tid) = drive_configuration(&session, configured.outbound_frames);
7742
7743        assert_eq!(
7744            written,
7745            HashMap::from([
7746                (prop::TZ_OFFSET, 0i16.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
7747                (prop::GNSS_IDENT_UPDATE, vec![0]),
7748                (prop::GNSS_IDENT_PRECISION, vec![3]),
7749                (prop::GNSS_TIME_TRUST, vec![0]),
7750                (prop::GNSS_ENABLED, vec![0]),
7751            ]),
7752            "only the zone and the positioning policy are written"
7753        );
7754        assert_eq!(order.last(), Some(&prop::GNSS_ENABLED));
7755
7756        // It closes like any configuration pass: a save, then the
7757        // device's own answers reduced into a fresh snapshot.
7758        let attached = session
7759            .consume(property_response(save_tid, prop::LAST_STATUS, &[0]))
7760            .unwrap();
7761        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
7762        assert_eq!(
7763            attached.snapshot.provisioning.unwrap().gnss,
7764            Some(UlcpGnssSettingsRecord {
7765                enabled: false,
7766                ident_update: false,
7767                ident_precision: 3,
7768                time_trust: false,
7769            })
7770        );
7771    }

7773    #[test]
7774    fn positioning_on_its_own_still_matches_the_capabilities() {
7775        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
7776        attach_positioning(&session);
7777        let whole = UlcpGnssSettingsRecord {
7778            enabled: true,
7779            ident_update: false,
7780            ident_precision: 5,
7781            time_trust: true,
7782        };
7783        // The same presence rule as the whole-domain write.
7784        assert_eq!(
7785            session.configure_positioning(Some(whole), None),
7786            Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
7787        );
7788        assert_eq!(
7789            session.configure_positioning(None, Some(0)),
7790            Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
7791        );
7792
7793        // And a radio with neither capability has nothing to configure,
7794        // which is a caller mistake rather than an empty success.
7795        let plain = MobileUlcpSession::new();
7796        attach_commissionable(&plain, Some(vec![0xAA; 32]), vec![0xAA; 32]);
7797        assert_eq!(
7798            plain.configure_positioning(None, None),
7799            Err(MobileError::UnsupportedCapability)
7800        );
7801    }

7803    #[test]
7804    fn a_positioning_record_must_match_what_the_device_can_do() {
7805        let session = MobileUlcpSession::administrative();
7806        attach_positioning(&session);
7807
7808        let whole = UlcpDeviceConfigRecord {
7809            radio: UlcpRadioSettingsRecord {
7810                device_name: None,
7811                phy_enabled: true,
7812                frequency_khz: 915_000,
7813                transmit_power_dbm: 14,
7814                bandwidth_hz: None,
7815                spreading_factor: None,
7816                coding_rate_denom: None,
7817                duty_cycle_limit: None,
7818            },
7819            ident_role: None,
7820            ident_mobile: Some(false),
7821            dev_discoverable: Some(true),
7822            repeater: Some(UlcpRepeaterSettingsRecord {
7823                enabled: false,
7824                regions: Vec::new(),
7825                default_region: None,
7826                min_rssi_dbm: None,
7827                min_snr_db: None,
7828            }),
7829            tz_offset_min: Some(0),
7830            gnss: Some(UlcpGnssSettingsRecord {
7831                enabled: true,
7832                ident_update: false,
7833                ident_precision: 5,
7834                time_trust: true,
7835            }),
7836            advert: None,
7837        };
7838
7839        // Both gated fields are required on a device that has them.
7840        assert_eq!(
7841            session.configure_device(UlcpDeviceConfigRecord {
7842                tz_offset_min: None,
7843                ..whole.clone()
7844            }),
7845            Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
7846        );

7848            session.configure_device(UlcpDeviceConfigRecord {
7849                gnss: None,
7850                ..whole.clone()
7851            }),
7852            Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
7853        );
7854        // A precision outside 1–7 names no cell.
7855        assert_eq!(
7856            session.configure_device(UlcpDeviceConfigRecord {
7857                gnss: Some(UlcpGnssSettingsRecord {
7858                    ident_precision: 8,
7859                    ..whole.gnss.unwrap()
7860                }),
7861                ..whole.clone()
7862            }),
7863            Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
7864        );
7865        // And an offset no zone on Earth uses.
7866        assert_eq!(
7867            session.configure_device(UlcpDeviceConfigRecord {
7868                tz_offset_min: Some(15 * 60),
7869                ..whole.clone()
7870            }),
7871            Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
7872        );
7873    }

7875    #[test]
7876    fn half_a_positioning_policy_is_withdrawn_whole() {
7877        let session = MobileUlcpSession::administrative();
7878        let mut capabilities = commissionable_capabilities();
7879        capabilities.extend([cap::TIME, cap::GNSS]);
7880        let begin = session.begin(Some(vec![0xAA; 32])).unwrap();
7881        let attached = drive_reads(
7882            &session,
7883            begin.outbound_frames,
7884            move |property| match property {
7885                prop::CAPS => (property, encoded_capabilities(&capabilities)),
7886                prop::HOST_KEY => (property, vec![0xAA; 32]),
7887                // Firmware older than the capability it advertises.
7888                prop::GNSS_TIME_TRUST => (
7889                    prop::LAST_STATUS,
7890                    vec![umsh_ulcp::Status::PROP_NOT_FOUND.0 as u8],
7891                ),
7892                prop::TIME => (property, Vec::new()),
7893                prop::TZ_OFFSET => (property, 0i16.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
7894                prop::GNSS_ENABLED | prop::GNSS_IDENT_UPDATE => (property, vec![0]),
7895                prop::GNSS_IDENT_PRECISION => (property, vec![5]),
7896                prop::GNSS_LOCATION | prop::GNSS_ALTITUDE | prop::GNSS_PRECISION => {
7897                    (property, Vec::new())
7898                }
7899                prop::GNSS_FIX => (property, vec![0]),
7900                prop::GNSS_SATELLITES => (property, vec![0]),
7901                _ => commissionable_value(property),
7902            },
7903        );
7904
7905        let sync = attached.snapshot.provisioning.clone().expect("described");
7906        assert!(sync.supports_gnss);
7907        assert_eq!(sync.gnss, None);
7908        assert_eq!(sync.unreadable_properties, vec![prop::GNSS_TIME_TRUST]);
7909        // A receiver that is off answers the facts it is sure of and
7910        // leaves the position empty.
7911        let gnss = attached.snapshot.gnss.expect("the receiver was read");
7912        assert_eq!(gnss.fix, UlcpFixKind::None);
7913        assert!(gnss.location.is_empty());
7914        assert_eq!(gnss.latitude_deg, None);
7915
7916        // None of the group is written, because a receiver switched on
7917        // under half a policy is worse than one left alone.
7918        let configured = session
7919            .configure_device(UlcpDeviceConfigRecord {
7920                radio: UlcpRadioSettingsRecord {
7921                    device_name: None,
7922                    phy_enabled: true,
7923                    frequency_khz: 915_000,
7924                    transmit_power_dbm: 14,
7925                    bandwidth_hz: None,
7926                    spreading_factor: None,
7927                    coding_rate_denom: None,
7928                    duty_cycle_limit: None,
7929                },
7930                ident_role: None,
7931                ident_mobile: Some(false),
7932                dev_discoverable: Some(true),
7933                repeater: Some(UlcpRepeaterSettingsRecord {
7934                    enabled: false,
7935                    regions: Vec::new(),
7936                    default_region: None,
7937                    min_rssi_dbm: None,
7938                    min_snr_db: None,
7939                }),
7940                tz_offset_min: Some(0),
7941                gnss: Some(UlcpGnssSettingsRecord {
7942                    enabled: true,
7943                    ident_update: true,
7944                    ident_precision: 5,
7945                    time_trust: true,
7946                }),
7947                advert: None,
7948            })
7949            .unwrap();
7950        let (written, _, _) = drive_configuration(&session, configured.outbound_frames);
7951        for property in [
7952            prop::GNSS_ENABLED,
7953            prop::GNSS_IDENT_UPDATE,
7954            prop::GNSS_IDENT_PRECISION,
7955            prop::GNSS_TIME_TRUST,
7956        ] {
7957            assert!(!written.contains_key(&property), "property {property}");
7958        }
7959        // The zone is its own property and is unaffected.
7960        assert_eq!(
7961            written.get(&prop::TZ_OFFSET),
7962            Some(&0i16.to_le_bytes().to_vec())
7963        );
7964    }
7965
7966    #[test]
7967    fn a_receiver_without_a_clock_is_not_a_device_this_phone_believes() {
7968        // CAP_GNSS requires CAP_TIME: the device dates its own fixes, so a
7969        // receiver with no clock to date them against is a malformed
7970        // capability set rather than a limited device.
7971        assert_eq!(
7972            ulcp_inspection_properties(encoded_capabilities(&[cap::GNSS])),
7973            Err(MobileError::InvalidUlcpFrame)
7974        );
7975
7976        // The clock is read at attach along with the policy, so a phone
7977        // that has just connected knows whether the device knows the time
7978        // rather than waiting for the next announcement to find out.
7979        let asked =
7980            ulcp_inspection_properties(encoded_capabilities(&[cap::TIME, cap::GNSS])).unwrap();
7981        for property in [
7982            prop::TIME,
7983            prop::TZ_OFFSET,
7984            prop::GNSS_ENABLED,
7985            prop::GNSS_LOCATION,
7986            prop::GNSS_FIX,
7987            prop::GNSS_SATELLITES,
7988            prop::GNSS_TIME_TRUST,
7989        ] {
7990            assert!(asked.contains(&property), "property {property}");
7991        }
7992        // And a device with a clock and no receiver is asked for neither.
7993        let clock_only = ulcp_inspection_properties(encoded_capabilities(&[cap::TIME])).unwrap();
7994        assert!(clock_only.contains(&prop::TIME));
7995        assert!(!clock_only.contains(&prop::GNSS_ENABLED));
7996    }

7999    fn a_precision_outside_the_encoding_names_no_cell() {
8000        assert_eq!(ulcp_location_cell_meters(0), None);
8001        assert_eq!(ulcp_location_cell_meters(8), None);
8002        // The default identity precision discloses a ~38 m cell.
8003        let five = ulcp_location_cell_meters(5).unwrap();
8004        assert!((38.0..39.0).contains(&five), "{five}");
8005    }

8007    #[test]
8008    fn battery_push_does_not_move_an_attached_session_out_of_phase() {
8009        let session = attached_battery_session();
8010        let pushed = session
8011            .consume(property_response(
8012                frame::TID_UNSOLICITED,
8013                prop::BATTERY,
8014                &[0b111, 0x10, 0x10, 45, 1],
8015            ))
8016            .unwrap();
8017        assert_eq!(pushed.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
8018        assert!(!pushed.waiting_for_responses);
8019        assert!(pushed.outbound_frames.is_empty());
8020    }
8021
8022    #[test]
8023    fn battery_push_to_a_tethered_session_on_another_phones_radio_stays_attached() {
8024        // A tethered claim that did not take is an answer, not a failure:
8025        // synchronization completes and the session attaches while
8026        // `ownership()` reports `OtherHost` (see the `Claim` arm). Battery
8027        // is the first unsolicited notification that arrives *routinely*,
8028        // so this is the path that turns a latent re-decision into one the
8029        // user would actually see — a settled session must not bounce back
8030        // to awaiting-host every time the radio reports its charge.
8031        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
8032        let ours = vec![0x11; 32];
8033        let theirs = vec![0x22; 32];
8034        let begin = session.begin(Some(ours.clone())).unwrap();
8035        let synchronized =
8036            answer_requests(&session, begin.outbound_frames, |property| match property {
8037                prop::LAST_STATUS => (property, vec![0]),
8038                prop::PROTOCOL_VERSION => (property, vec![6, 0]),
8039                // A readable `PROP_HOST_KEY` requires `CAP_HOST_FILTER`
8040                // and vice versa (`advance_completed_stage` enforces it).
8041                prop::CAPS => (
8042                    property,
8043                    encoded_capabilities(&[cap::BATTERY, cap::HOST_FILTER]),
8044                ),
8045                prop::BATTERY => (property, vec![0b111, 0x74, 0x0E, 60, 0]),
8046                prop::DEV_KEY | prop::DEV_NAME => (property, Vec::new()),
8047                // The radio already belongs to another identity.
8048                prop::HOST_KEY => (property, theirs.clone()),
8049                _ => unreachable!(),
8050            });
8051        assert_eq!(
8052            synchronized.snapshot.host_ownership,
8053            UlcpHostOwnership::OtherHost
8054        );
8055        assert_eq!(synchronized.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::AwaitingHost);
8056
8057        // The user takes the radio; the device refuses the write and keeps
8058        // reporting the other identity's key. No `CAP_SAVE`, so the claim
8059        // answer runs straight into inspection.
8060        let claim = session.claim(ours).unwrap();
8061        let claim_tid = Frame::parse(&claim.outbound_frames[0])
8062            .unwrap()
8063            .header
8064            .tid();
8065        let claimed = session
8066            .consume(property_response(claim_tid, prop::HOST_KEY, &theirs))
8067            .unwrap();
8068        let attached = answer_requests(
8069            &session,
8070            claimed.outbound_frames,
8071            |property| match property {
8072                prop::INTERFACE_TYPE => (property, vec![INTERFACE_TYPE as u8]),
8073                prop::PHY_ENABLED => (property, vec![1]),
8074                prop::PHY_FREQ => (property, 915_000u32.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
8075                prop::PHY_TX_POWER => (property, vec![14]),
8076                prop::HOST_RX_FILTERS => (property, Vec::new()),
8077                _ => unreachable!(),
8078            },
8079        );
8080        assert_eq!(attached.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
8081        assert_eq!(
8082            attached.snapshot.host_ownership,
8083            UlcpHostOwnership::OtherHost,
8084            "the claim did not take"
8085        );
8086
8087        let pushed = session
8088            .consume(property_response(
8089                frame::TID_UNSOLICITED,
8090                prop::BATTERY,
8091                &[0b111, 0x10, 0x10, 45, 1],
8092            ))
8093            .unwrap();
8094        assert_eq!(
8095            pushed.snapshot.battery.expect("push carries it").percentage,
8096            Some(45)
8097        );
8098        assert_eq!(
8099            pushed.snapshot.phase,
8100            UlcpSessionPhase::Attached,
8101            "a battery report is not an attach decision"
8102        );
8103
8104        // The decision itself is still re-opened by the one change that
8105        // warrants it: the radio reporting a different owner.
8106        let reclaimed = session
8107            .consume(property_response(
8108                frame::TID_UNSOLICITED,
8109                prop::HOST_KEY,
8110                &[0x33; 32],
8111            ))
8112            .unwrap();
8113        assert_eq!(
8114            reclaimed.snapshot.phase,
8115            UlcpSessionPhase::AwaitingHost,
8116            "a third phone taking the radio is the user's call"
8117        );
8118    }

8122    /// The capabilities of a full-featured tracker, for planning against.
8123    fn managed_capabilities() -> Vec<u8> {
8124        encoded_capabilities(&[
8125            cap::DEV_NAME,
8126            cap::BATTERY,
8127            cap::PHY_LORA,
8128            cap::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT,
8129            cap::REPEATER,
8130            cap::IDENT,
8131            cap::DEV_IDENTITY,
8132            cap::GNSS,
8133            cap::TIME,
8134            cap::ADVERT,
8135            cap::ADMIN,
8136            cap::ALERT,
8137            cap::SAVE,
8138            cap::CMD_MULTI,
8139        ])
8140    }

8142    #[test]
8143    fn a_category_asks_only_for_its_own_screen() {
8144        let caps = managed_capabilities();
8145        let radio = ulcp_category_properties(UlcpManageCategory::Radio, caps.clone()).unwrap();
8146        assert_eq!(
8147            radio,
8148            vec![
8149                prop::PHY_ENABLED,
8150                prop::PHY_FREQ,
8151                prop::PHY_TX_POWER,
8152                prop::PHY_LORA_BW,
8153                prop::PHY_LORA_SF,
8154                prop::PHY_LORA_CR,
8155                prop::PHY_DUTY_NOW,
8156                prop::PHY_DUTY_LIMIT,
8157            ]
8158        );
8159        assert_eq!(
8160            ulcp_category_properties(UlcpManageCategory::Power, caps).unwrap(),
8161            vec![prop::BATTERY],
8162            "one property is the whole of a power screen"
8163        );
8164    }

8166    /// The bug this pins: an edit to one property must not drag its
8167    /// neighbors into the write. A device holding a value nobody touched
8168    /// keeps it because nothing was sent, not because the same value was
8169    /// sent back — restating it can be refused, and a refusal abandons
8170    /// the settings someone actually changed.
8171    #[test]
8172    fn only_what_was_edited_is_written() {
8173        let written = dirty(
8174            UlcpDevicePropertiesRecord {
8175                gnss_enabled: Some(true),
8176                gnss_ident_update: Some(false),
8177                gnss_ident_precision: Some(4),
8178                gnss_time_trust: Some(true),
8179                ..Default::default()
8180            },
8181            &[prop::GNSS_IDENT_UPDATE],
8182        )
8183        .unwrap();
8184        assert_eq!(
8185            written,
8186            vec![(prop::GNSS_IDENT_UPDATE, vec![0])],
8187            "the other three GNSS settings were not edited, so they do not travel"
8188        );
8189    }

8191    #[test]
8192    fn a_category_leaves_out_what_the_device_cannot_do() {
8193        // A repeater with no receiver and no modem knobs.
8194        let caps = encoded_capabilities(&[cap::REPEATER, cap::IDENT, cap::DEV_IDENTITY]);
8195        assert_eq!(
8196            ulcp_category_properties(UlcpManageCategory::Radio, caps.clone()).unwrap(),
8197            vec![prop::PHY_ENABLED, prop::PHY_FREQ, prop::PHY_TX_POWER],
8198            "a device without CAP_PHY_LORA is not asked for a modem profile"
8199        );
8200        assert!(
8201            ulcp_category_properties(UlcpManageCategory::Gnss, caps.clone())
8202                .unwrap()
8203                .is_empty(),
8204            "a device with no receiver has no GNSS screen to fill"
8205        );
8206        let identity = ulcp_category_properties(UlcpManageCategory::Identity, caps).unwrap();
8207        assert!(
8208            identity.contains(&prop::IDENT_LOCATION),
8209            "a fixed repeater still has a position to state"
8210        );
8211        assert!(
8212            !identity.contains(&prop::GNSS_IDENT_UPDATE),
8213            "nothing to auto-update from"
8214        );
8215    }

8345    #[test]
8346    fn a_receiver_readout_needs_the_fix_to_mean_anything() {
8347        let without = inspect_ulcp_properties(vec![
8348            response(prop::GNSS_ENABLED, &[1]),
8349            response(prop::GNSS_SATELLITES, &[7, 9]),
8350        ]);
8351        assert_eq!(without.gnss_enabled, Some(true));
8352        assert_eq!(
8353            without.gnss, None,
8354            "satellite counts alone do not say whether there is a position"
8355        );
8356
8357        let with = inspect_ulcp_properties(vec![
8358            response(prop::GNSS_FIX, &[FixKind::ThreeD as u8]),
8359            response(prop::GNSS_LOCATION, &[0x84, 0x21, 0x9f, 0x40]),
8360            response(prop::GNSS_ALTITUDE, &1_400i32.to_le_bytes()),
8361            response(prop::GNSS_SATELLITES, &[7, 9]),
8362        ]);
8363        let readout = with.gnss.expect("a fix makes a readout");
8364        assert_eq!(readout.altitude_m, Some(1_400));
8365        assert_eq!(readout.satellites_used, 7);
8366        assert!(readout.latitude_deg.is_some());
8367    }
8368
8369    /// The writes a dirty-apply produced, in order.
8370    fn dirty(
8371        desired: UlcpDevicePropertiesRecord,
8372        edited: &[u32],
8373    ) -> Result<Vec<(u32, Vec<u8>)>, MobileError> {
8374        Ok(ulcp_dirty_writes(desired, edited.to_vec())?
8375            .into_iter()
8376            .map(|write| (write.property_id, write.value))
8377            .collect())
8378    }
8379
8380    #[test]
8381    fn one_edit_is_one_write() {
8382        let written = dirty(
8383            UlcpDevicePropertiesRecord {
8384                device_name: Some("Saddle".into()),
8385                // Everything a full read would have filled in, none of
8386                // which was touched.
8387                ident_mobile: Some(true),
8388                repeater_enabled: Some(true),
8389                dev_discoverable: Some(false),
8390                ..Default::default()
8391            },
8392            &[prop::DEV_NAME],
8393        )
8394        .unwrap();
8395        assert_eq!(
8396            written,
8397            vec![(prop::DEV_NAME, b"Saddle".to_vec())],
8398            "the point of the whole design: a rename costs one write"
8399        );
8400    }

8405            dirty(UlcpDevicePropertiesRecord::default(), &[prop::DEV_NAME]).is_err(),
8406            "reporting success for an edit that carries no value would be a lie"
8407        );
8408    }

8410    #[test]
8411    fn a_modem_knob_travels_alone_inside_the_bracket() {
8412        let written = dirty(
8413            UlcpDevicePropertiesRecord {
8414                phy_enabled: Some(true),
8415                bandwidth_hz: Some(250_000),
8416                spreading_factor: Some(10),
8417                coding_rate_denom: Some(5),
8418                ..Default::default()
8419            },
8420            &[prop::PHY_LORA_SF],
8421        )
8422        .unwrap();
8423        let keys: Vec<u32> = written.iter().map(|(key, _)| *key).collect();
8424        assert_eq!(
8425            keys,
8426            vec![prop::PHY_ENABLED, prop::PHY_LORA_SF, prop::PHY_ENABLED],
8427            "the untouched bandwidth and coding rate stay home"
8428        );
8429        assert_eq!(written.first().unwrap().1, vec![0], "the radio goes down");
8430        assert_eq!(
8431            written.last().unwrap().1,
8432            vec![1],
8433            "and comes back up as it was"
8434        );
8435    }

8473    #[test]
8474    fn an_altitude_takes_no_more_octets_than_it_needs() {
8475        for (meters, expected) in [
8476            (100i32, vec![0x64]),
8477            (-100, vec![0x9C]),
8478            (200, vec![0xC8, 0x00]),
8479            (-200, vec![0x38, 0xFF]),
8480            (100_000, vec![0xA0, 0x86, 0x01]),
8481            (-100_000, vec![0x60, 0x79, 0xFE]),
8482        ] {
8483            let written = dirty(
8484                UlcpDevicePropertiesRecord {
8485                    ident_altitude_m: Some(meters),
8486                    ..Default::default()
8487                },
8488                &[prop::IDENT_ALTITUDE],
8489            )
8490            .unwrap();
8491            assert_eq!(
8492                written,
8493                vec![(prop::IDENT_ALTITUDE, expected)],
8494                "{meters} m"
8495            );
8496        }
8497    }

8550    #[test]
8551    fn a_forwarding_policy_edit_travels_alone() {
8552        let written = dirty(
8553            UlcpDevicePropertiesRecord {
8554                repeater_enabled: Some(true),
8555                repeater_regions: Some(vec!["SJC".into()]),
8556                repeater_default_region: None,
8557                repeater_min_rssi_dbm: Some(-115),
8558                repeater_min_snr_db: None,
8559                ..Default::default()
8560            },
8561            &[prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI, prop::MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS],
8562        )
8563        .unwrap();
8564        assert_eq!(
8565            written.iter().map(|(key, _)| *key).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
8566            vec![prop::MAC_REPEATER_REGIONS, prop::MAC_REPEATER_MIN_RSSI],
8567            "the three untouched policy settings stay home"
8568        );
8569        assert_eq!(
8570            written[0].1,
8571            vec![3, b'S', b'J', b'C'],
8572            "regions pack the same way they do over the local link"
8573        );
8574    }

8576    // ─── Local management operations ─────────────────────────────────
8577
8578    /// A `CMD_PROP_SET` request, decoded.
8579    fn set_request(bytes: &[u8]) -> (u8, u32, Vec<u8>) {
8580        let parsed = Frame::parse(bytes).unwrap();
8581        assert_eq!(parsed.command(), Some(Cmd::PropSet));
8582        let payload = PropPayload::parse(parsed.payload).unwrap();
8583        (parsed.header.tid(), payload.key, payload.value.to_vec())
8584    }
8585
8586    #[test]
8587    fn local_fetch_reports_values_and_refusals_alike() {
8588        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
8589        attach_commissionable(&session, Some(vec![0xAA; 32]), vec![0xAA; 32]);
8590
8591        let update = session
8592            .begin_property_fetch(vec![prop::DEV_NAME, prop::GNSS_ENABLED])
8593            .unwrap();
8594        assert_eq!(update.outbound_frames.len(), 2);
8595
8596        let (name_tid, name_prop) = property_request(&update.outbound_frames[0]);
8597        assert_eq!(name_prop, prop::DEV_NAME);
8598        let (gnss_tid, gnss_prop) = property_request(&update.outbound_frames[1]);
8599        assert_eq!(gnss_prop, prop::GNSS_ENABLED);
8600
8601        let mid = session
8602            .consume(property_response(name_tid, prop::DEV_NAME, b"Ridge"))
8603            .unwrap();
8604        assert!(
8605            mid.management_event.is_none(),
8606            "half-answered is not answered"
8607        );
8608        // The device has no receiver, so it refuses the second property.
8609        let done = session
8610            .consume(property_response(
8611                gnss_tid,
8612                prop::LAST_STATUS,
8613                &[umsh_ulcp::Status::PROP_NOT_FOUND.0 as u8],
8614            ))
8615            .unwrap();
8616        let event = done.management_event.expect("both answers are in");
8617        assert_eq!(
8618            event.answers,
8619            vec![
8620                MobileMeshManagementAnswerRecord {
8621                    property_id: prop::DEV_NAME,
8622                    value: Some(b"Ridge".to_vec()),
8623                    status_code: None,
8624                },
8625                MobileMeshManagementAnswerRecord {
8626                    property_id: prop::GNSS_ENABLED,
8627                    value: None,
8628                    status_code: Some(umsh_ulcp::Status::PROP_NOT_FOUND.0),
8629                },
8630            ],
8631            "a refusal is that property's answer, not the operation's failure"
8632        );
8633        assert_eq!(event.status_code, None);
8634        assert_eq!(
8635            done.snapshot.device_name.as_deref(),
8636            Some("Ridge"),
8637            "what a fetch learns, the session snapshot learns too"
8638        );
8639    }

8641    #[test]
8642    fn local_fetch_asks_in_bounded_batches() {
8643        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
8644        attach_commissionable(&session, Some(vec![0xAA; 32]), vec![0xAA; 32]);
8645
8646        // Nine properties: seven in the first round, two in the second.
8647        let properties: Vec<u32> = vec![
8648            prop::DEV_NAME,
8649            prop::PHY_ENABLED,
8650            prop::PHY_FREQ,
8651            prop::PHY_TX_POWER,
8652            prop::IDENT_MOBILE,
8653            prop::DEV_DISCOVERABLE,
8654            prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED,
8655            prop::DEV_PEERS,
8656            prop::DEV_ADMINS,
8657        ];
8658        let update = session.begin_property_fetch(properties.clone()).unwrap();
8659        assert_eq!(
8660            update.outbound_frames.len(),
8661            usize::from(frame::TID_MAX),
8662            "a round asks for no more than the transaction space holds"
8663        );
8664        let second = answer_requests(&session, update.outbound_frames, commissionable_value);
8665        assert_eq!(second.outbound_frames.len(), 2);
8666        assert!(second.management_event.is_none());
8667        let done = answer_requests(&session, second.outbound_frames, commissionable_value);
8668        let event = done.management_event.expect("all nine answered");
8669        assert_eq!(event.answers.len(), properties.len());
8670    }
8671
8672    #[test]
8673    fn local_writes_go_out_one_at_a_time_and_survive_a_refusal() {
8674        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
8675        attach_commissionable(&session, Some(vec![0xAA; 32]), vec![0xAA; 32]);
8676
8677        let update = session
8678            .begin_property_writes(vec![
8679                MobileMeshPropertyWriteRecord {
8680                    property_id: prop::DEV_NAME,
8681                    value: b"Saddle".to_vec(),
8682                },
8683                MobileMeshPropertyWriteRecord {
8684                    property_id: prop::IDENT_MOBILE,
8685                    value: vec![1],
8686                },
8687            ])
8688            .unwrap();
8689        assert_eq!(
8690            update.outbound_frames.len(),
8691            1,
8692            "order is load-bearing, so nothing is pipelined"
8693        );
8694        let (tid, property, value) = set_request(&update.outbound_frames[0]);
8695        assert_eq!(
8696            (property, value.as_slice()),
8697            (prop::DEV_NAME, &b"Saddle"[..])
8698        );
8699
8700        // The device refuses the name; the run continues to the next write.
8701        let mid = session
8702            .consume(property_response(
8703                tid,
8704                prop::LAST_STATUS,
8705                &[umsh_ulcp::Status::INVALID_ARGUMENT.0 as u8],
8706            ))
8707            .unwrap();
8708        assert!(mid.management_event.is_none());
8709        assert_eq!(mid.outbound_frames.len(), 1);
8710        let (tid, property, value) = set_request(&mid.outbound_frames[0]);
8711        assert_eq!((property, value), (prop::IDENT_MOBILE, vec![1]));
8712
8713        let done = session
8714            .consume(property_response(tid, prop::IDENT_MOBILE, &[1]))
8715            .unwrap();
8716        let event = done.management_event.expect("both writes answered");
8717        assert_eq!(
8718            event.answers,
8719            vec![
8720                MobileMeshManagementAnswerRecord {
8721                    property_id: prop::DEV_NAME,
8722                    value: None,
8723                    status_code: Some(umsh_ulcp::Status::INVALID_ARGUMENT.0),
8724                },
8725                MobileMeshManagementAnswerRecord {
8726                    property_id: prop::IDENT_MOBILE,
8727                    value: Some(vec![1]),
8728                    status_code: None,
8729                },
8730            ],
8731        );
8732    }

8734    #[test]
8735    fn local_save_reports_the_device_status() {
8736        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
8737        attach_commissionable(&session, Some(vec![0xAA; 32]), vec![0xAA; 32]);
8738
8739        let update = session.begin_save().unwrap();
8740        assert_eq!(update.outbound_frames.len(), 1);
8741        let parsed = Frame::parse(&update.outbound_frames[0]).unwrap();
8742        assert_eq!(parsed.command(), Some(Cmd::Save));
8743        let done = session
8744            .consume(property_response(
8745                parsed.header.tid(),

8748            ))
8749            .unwrap();
8750        let event = done.management_event.expect("the save answered");
8751        assert!(event.answers.is_empty());
8752        assert_eq!(event.status_code, Some(0));
8753    }

8755    #[test]
8756    fn local_save_without_the_capability_is_already_done() {
8757        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
8758        let capabilities: Vec<u32> = commissionable_capabilities()
8759            .into_iter()
8760            .filter(|&capability| capability != cap::SAVE)
8761            .collect();
8762        let begin = session.begin(Some(vec![0xAA; 32])).unwrap();
8763        drive_reads(
8764            &session,
8765            begin.outbound_frames,
8766            move |property| match property {
8767                prop::CAPS => (property, encoded_capabilities(&capabilities)),
8768                prop::HOST_KEY => (property, vec![0xAA; 32]),
8769                _ => commissionable_value(property),
8770            },
8771        );
8772
8773        let update = session.begin_save().unwrap();
8774        assert!(update.outbound_frames.is_empty());
8775        let event = update
8776            .management_event
8777            .expect("nothing to ask, so the operation is already complete");
8778        assert_eq!(event.status_code, None);
8779    }

8781    #[test]
8782    fn one_local_operation_at_a_time() {
8783        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
8784        attach_commissionable(&session, Some(vec![0xAA; 32]), vec![0xAA; 32]);
8785
8786        let update = session.begin_property_fetch(vec![prop::DEV_NAME]).unwrap();
8787        assert!(
8788            session.begin_property_fetch(vec![prop::DEV_NAME]).is_err(),
8789            "a second operation must wait for the first"
8790        );
8791        assert!(session.refresh().is_err(), "so must a refresh");
8792
8793        let (tid, _) = property_request(&update.outbound_frames[0]);
8794        let done = session
8795            .consume(property_response(tid, prop::DEV_NAME, b"Ridge"))
8796            .unwrap();
8797        assert!(done.management_event.is_some());
8798        assert!(
8799            session.begin_property_fetch(vec![prop::DEV_NAME]).is_ok(),
8800            "and once it completes, the next may run"
8801        );
8802    }

8804    #[test]
8805    fn an_unsolicited_value_is_carried_out_verbatim() {
8806        let session = MobileUlcpSession::new();
8807        attach_commissionable(&session, Some(vec![0xAA; 32]), vec![0xAA; 32]);
8808
8809        let update = session
8810            .consume(property_response(
8811                frame::TID_UNSOLICITED,
8812                prop::IDENT_MOBILE,
8813                &[1],
8814            ))
8815            .unwrap();
8816        assert_eq!(
8817            update.pushed_properties,
8818            vec![UlcpPropertyPushRecord {
8819                property_id: prop::IDENT_MOBILE,
8820                value: vec![1],
8821            }],
8822            "a push reaches whoever caches values by number, not just the snapshot"
8823        );
8824    }

8826    #[test]
8827    fn a_lazy_administrative_attach_reads_only_what_attaching_requires() {
8828        let session = MobileUlcpSession::administrative_lazy();
8829        let begin = session.begin(None).unwrap();
8830        let mut asked = Vec::new();
8831        let mut pending = begin.outbound_frames;
8832        let mut last = None;
8833        while !pending.is_empty() {
8834            let update = answer_requests(&session, pending.clone(), |property| match property {
8835                // Someone else's radio, which an administrative session
8836                // attaches to anyway.
8837                prop::HOST_KEY => (property, vec![0xBB; 32]),
8838                _ => commissionable_value(property),
8839            });
8840            for request in &pending {
8841                asked.push(property_request(request).1);
8842            }
8843            pending = update.outbound_frames.clone();
8844            last = Some(update);
8845        }
8846        let update = last.unwrap();
8847        assert_eq!(update.snapshot.phase, UlcpSessionPhase::Attached);
8848        assert_eq!(
8849            asked.len(),
8850            11,
8851            "the seven-property preamble and the four the sync reduction insists on"
8852        );
8853        assert!(
8854            !asked.contains(&prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED),
8855            "the device domain is left to be read on demand"
8856        );
8857
8858        // And reading on demand works.
8859        let fetch = session
8860            .begin_property_fetch(vec![prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED])
8861            .unwrap();
8862        let (tid, _) = property_request(&fetch.outbound_frames[0]);
8863        let done = session
8864            .consume(property_response(tid, prop::MAC_REPEATER_ENABLED, &[0]))
8865            .unwrap();
8866        assert!(done.management_event.is_some());
8867    }

8869    #[test]
8870    fn radio_presets_cross_the_bindings_whole() {
8871        let presets = ulcp_radio_presets();
8872        assert_eq!(presets.len(), umsh_ulcp::profiles::VETTED.len());
8873        assert_eq!(presets[0].id, umsh_ulcp::profiles::DEFAULT.id);
8874
8875        let default = &presets[0];
8876        assert_eq!(default.frequency_khz, 910_525);
8877        assert_eq!(default.bandwidth_hz, 62_500);
8878        assert_eq!(default.spreading_factor, 7);
8879        assert_eq!(default.coding_rate_denom, 5);
8880        assert_eq!(default.transmit_power_dbm, Some(21));
8881        assert_eq!(default.duty_cycle_limit, u16::MAX);
8882
8883        // A profile with no vetted power crosses as an absent one
8884        // rather than a zero.
8885        assert!(
8886            presets
8887                .iter()
8888                .any(|preset| preset.transmit_power_dbm.is_none()),
8889            "the optional power is reachable from the app"
8890        );
8891        assert_eq!(ulcp_supported_bandwidths_hz().len(), 10);
8892    }
